政策法规移民局 (USCIS)重要#职业移民与工卡#难民·庇护·TPS

DHS最终确定规则,取消部分EAD的自动延期

发布日期 2026年10月13日生效日期 2026年10月14日

DHS发布最终规则,在未作任何修改的情况下确认并定稿了2025年10月30日发布的临时最终规则(IFR),该规则终止了在特定类别中按时提交续签申请者EAD(Form I-766)有效期的自动延期。该文件目前处于公开查阅阶段,预计于2026年10月14日在联邦公报(Federal Register)刊登,规则文本将该日期同时定为生效日期。

要点

  • 本最终规则在未作修改的情况下确认并定稿了2025年10月30日生效的临时最终规则(IFR)。
  • 适用于2025年10月30日及以后提交的Form I-765续签申请,对该日期之前已获得自动延期的EAD不产生影响。
  • 不改变依据法律或联邦公报通知提供的自动延期,例如与TPS(Temporary Protected Status)相关的就业文件自动延期。
  • DHS表示,此举旨在确保在批准新一期就业授权或新EAD之前完成必要的审查与甄别。
  • 该规则修订了8 CFR 274a.13,修改了(d)段标题并新增(e)段。
  • 该文件目前处于公开查阅阶段,预计于2026年10月14日在联邦公报刊登,规则正文将刊登当日定为生效日期。

影响对象

持有本规则涉及类别EAD并按时提交Form I-765续签申请的人员,以及依赖EAD有效性办理Form I-9手续的雇主。

建议行动

受影响类别的续签申请人不应仅凭已提交申请就认为就业授权会自动延续,建议查阅最终规则原文及USCIS相关指南,了解对自身类别的具体影响。

Billing Code: 9111-97 美国国土安全部 8 CFR 第274a部分 [CIS编号：2859-26；DHS案卷编号：USCIS-2025-0271] RIN 1615-AD05 取消特定就业授权文件的自动延期 机构：美国公民及移民服务局（USCIS），国土安全部（DHS）。
Billing Code: 9111-97 DEPARTMENT OF HOMELAND SECURITY 8 CFR Part 274a [CIS No. 2859-26; DHS Docket No. USCIS-2025-0271] RIN 1615-AD05 Removal of Automatic Extensions for Certain Employment Authorization Documents AGENCY: U.S. Citizenship and Immigration Services, DHS.
行动：最终规则。
ACTION: Final rule.
摘要：2025年10月30日，国土安全部（DHS）发布了一项临时最终规则（IFR），修订了国土安全部的法规，以终止对已及时提交续签申请的特定就业授权类别外国人自动延长其就业授权文件（表格I-766或EAD）有效期的做法。本最终规则回应了对该临时最终规则所收到的意见，并重申和最终确定了该临时最终规则的各项规定。终止EAD的自动延期有助于加强福利诚信，并在授予新的就业授权期限和/或新的EAD之前，优先确保对外国人进行适当的审查和筛查。
SUMMARY: On October 30, 2025, DHS published an interim final rule (IFR) that amended DHS regulations to end the practice of automatically extending the validity of employment authorization documents (Forms I-766 or EADs) for aliens who have timely filed an application to renew their EAD in certain employment authorization categories. This final rule responds to comments received on the IFR and reaffirms and finalizes the provisions of the IFR. Ending the automatic extension of EADs enhances benefit integrity and prioritizes the proper vetting and screening of aliens before granting a new period of employment authorization and/or a new EAD.
日期：本最终规则自【插入在联邦公报上的发布日期】起生效。
DATES: This final rule is effective on [INSERT DATE OF PUBLICATION IN THE FEDERAL REGISTER].

FOR FURTHER INFORMATION CONTACT: Business and Foreign Workers Division, Office of Policy and Strategy, U.S. Citizenship and Immigration Services (USCIS), DHS, 5900 Capital Gateway Drive, MD, Camp Springs, 20746; telephone (240) 721-3000 (this is not a toll-free number).

Executive Summary A. Basis and Purpose DHS’s mission is to safeguard the American people, our homeland, and our values with honor and integrity. In service of that mission, DHS protects the United States from threats by terrorists, criminals, smugglers, transnational criminal organizations, failed-state actors, and unpredictable lone offenders that constitute present and future threats to public safety and national security.
美国公民及移民服务局(USCIS)向某些类别的外国人签发就业授权文件(EAD)。这些文件在规定期限内有效。打算在其就业授权文件(EAD)上注明的日期之后继续工作的外国人，通常必须提交申请以续展其就业授权和/或就业授权文件(EAD)。
USCIS issues EADs to certain classes of aliens. These documents are valid for a specified period. Aliens who intend to continue their employment beyond the date specified on their EAD must generally file an application to renew their employment authorization and/or EAD.
EAD（就业许可文件）有效期的自动延期，使外国人仅通过及时提交续期EAD申请即可获得延长其已过期的就业许可和/或EAD的利益，而无需先完成裁决审查和相关审查核实，包括解决在审查核实过程中发现的任何不利信息。也就是说，自动延期允许在美国公民及移民服务局（USCIS）未先采取以下行动的情况下授予该利益：资格认定、完成审查核实和筛查、解决与该外国人有关的潜在不利信息匹配，以及（如适用）确定应依自由裁量权授予就业许可。2025年10月30日，美国国土安全部（DHS）发布了一项临时最终规则（IFR），题为《取消就业许可文件的自动延期》，并征求公众意见。见90 FR 48799（2025年10月30日）。该IFR修订了DHS的法规，终止了对及时提交续期EAD申请的某些就业许可类别外国人自动延长其EAD有效期的做法。在认真考虑了就该IFR提交的公众意见后，DHS重申并最终确定该IFR，不作任何更改。该IFR序言中就相关法规修订所提供的理由和推理保持不变，因此DHS同样采纳该推理以支持本最终规则。在重申并最终确定IFR所作出的更改时，本规则的目的与IFR中所阐明的目的保持一致：通过终止对某些就业许可类别自动延长就业许可和/或EAD有效期的做法，优先对外国人进行适当的审查核实和筛查，并在授予新一期就业许可和/或新EAD之前确保其持续符合资格。
The automatic extension of the validity of an EAD grants the alien the benefit of extending his or her expired employment authorization and/or EAD merely by filing a timely renewal EAD application and without first completing adjudicative review and related vetting, including resolution of any derogatory information identified during the vetting process. That is, the automatic extension allows for the granting of the benefit without USCIS first performing the following actions: an eligibility determination, completing vetting and screening checks, resolving potential hits of derogatory information in connection with the alien, and a determination that the employment authorization should be granted in the exercise of discretion, if applicable. On October 30, 2025, DHS issued an IFR titled, “Removal of the Automatic Extension of Employment Authorization Documents,” with a request for comment. See 90 FR 48799 (Oct. 30, 2025). The IFR amended DHS regulations to end the practice of automatically extending the validity of EADs for aliens in certain employment authorization categories who timely filed an application to renew their EAD. After careful consideration of the public comments submitted in connection with the IFR, DHS is reaffirming and finalizing the IFR without changes. The rationale and reasoning provided in the preamble for the IFR remains unchanged with respect to the regulatory amendments, and DHS, therefore, adopts such reasoning also to support this final rule. In reaffirming and finalizing the changes made in the IFR, the purpose of this rule remains the same as articulated in the IFR: to prioritize the proper vetting and screening of aliens and ensure continued eligibility before granting a new period of employment authorization and/or a new EAD by ending the practice of automatically extending the validity of employment authorization and/or EADs for certain employment authorization categories.
终止提供EAD自动延期的做法，符合特朗普总统在第14159号行政命令《保护美国人民免受侵害》第16节中的指示，该指示要求国土安全部部长与国务卿及司法部长协调，采取一切适当行动，使本部各项活动与总统制定的政策保持一致，并确保，除其他事项外，“就业许可的提供方式符合《移民与国籍法》第274A条（8 U.S.C. 1324a）的规定，且不向美国境内任何未获授权的外国人提供就业许可”。[1]此举也符合第14161号行政命令《保护美国免受外国恐怖分子及其他国家安全和公共安全威胁》第2节的规定，该节指示国务卿与国土安全部部长、司法部长及国家情报总监协调，“识别可用于确保所有寻求进入美国或已在美国境内的外国人均获得最大程度审查核实和筛查的一切资源”。[2] B. 法律依据 本规则制定的法律依据见于《移民与国籍法》（INA）第103(a)条，即8 U.S.C. 1103(a)，该条授权部长管理和执行移民及国籍法律，并制定部长认为执行该授权所必需的法规；以及《国土安全法》（HSA）第101(b)(1)(F)条，即6 U.S.C. 111(b)(1)(F)，该条确立了DHS的一项主要使命，即有责任“确保美国的整体经济安全不因旨在保障国土安全的各项努力、活动和计划而受到削弱”。[1] 见第14159号行政命令《保护美国人民免受侵害》（2025年1月20日），90 FR 8443, 8446（2025年1月29日）。
Ending the practice of providing automatic extensions of EADs is consistent with Section 16 of President Trump’s directive in Executive Order (E.O.) 14159, Protecting the American People Against Invasion, which directs the Secretary of Homeland Security, in coordination with the Secretary of State and the Attorney General, to take all appropriate action to align any departmental activities with the policies set out by the President and to ensure, among others, “that employment authorization is provided in a manner consistent with section 274A of the Immigration and Nationality Act (8 U.S.C. 1324a), and that employment authorization is not provided to any unauthorized alien in the United States.”1 It is also consistent with Section 2 of E.O. 14161, Protecting the United States From Foreign Terrorists and Other National Security and Public Safety Threats, which directs the Secretary of State, in coordination with the Secretary of Homeland Security, the Attorney General, and the Director of National Intelligence to “identify all resources that may be used to ensure that all aliens seeking admission to the United States, or who are already in the United States, are vetted and screened to the maximum degree possible.”2 B. Legal Authority The legal basis of this rulemaking is found in section 103(a) of the Immigration and Nationality Act (INA), 8 U.S.C. 1103(a), which authorizes the Secretary to administer and enforce the immigration and nationality laws and establish such regulations as the Secretary deems necessary for carrying out such authority, and section 101(b)(1)(F) of the Homeland Security Act (HSA), 6 U.S.C. 111(b)(1)(F), which establishes as a primary mission of DHS the duty to “ensure that the overall economic security of the United States is not diminished by efforts, activities, and programs aimed at securing the homeland.” 1 See E.O. 14159, Protecting the American People Against Invasion (Jan. 20, 2025), 90 FR 8443, 8446 (Jan. 29, 2025).
[2] 见第14161号行政命令《保护美国免受外国恐怖分子及其他国家安全和公共安全威胁》（2025年1月20日），90 FR 8451, 8451（2025年1月30日）。
2 See E.O. 14161, Protecting the United States from Foreign Terrorists and Other National Security and Public Safety Threats (Jan. 20, 2025), 90 FR 8451, 8451 (Jan. 30, 2025).
C. 监管行动主要条款概述 本最终规则在认真考虑所收到的意见后，将该IFR原样确定为最终规则，不作任何更改。据此，DHS将该IFR所作出的以下更改确定为最终规则：
C. Summary of the Major Provisions of the Regulatory Action This final rule adopts the IFR as final, without any changes, after careful consideration of the comments received. Accordingly, DHS is adopting as final the following changes that the IFR has made:
•修订了8 CFR 274a.13(d)的标题,以明确表明最长540天的自动延期期限仅适用于在2025年10月30日之前提交的续签EAD申请。
•Revised the heading of 8 CFR 274a.13(d) to clearly indicate that the up-to- 540-day automatic extension period only applies to renewal EAD applications filed before October 30, 2025.
•新增了8 CFR 274a.13(e)条款。该新规定说明,除8 CFR 274a.13(d)另有规定、法律另有规定,或通过联邦公报(Federal Register)通知就临时保护身份(TPS)相关就业文件另有规定外,对于在2025年10月30日当日或之后提交的续签EAD申请,已过期或即将过期的就业许可文件(Employment Authorization Document)及/或就业许可的有效期将不会自动延长。
•Added new 8 CFR 274a.13(e). The new provision explains that, unless otherwise provided in 8 CFR 274a.13(d), by law, or through a Federal Register notice for Temporary Protected Status (TPS)-related employment documentation, the validity period of an expired or expiring Employment Authorization Document and/or employment authorization will not be automatically extended by a renewal EAD application filed on or after October 30, 2025.
美国国土安全部(DHS)在临时最终规则(IFR)中说明,该临时最终规则不影响在2025年10月30日之前已自动延长的EAD的有效性。该规则还说明,该临时最终规则不影响依据法律或联邦公报通知所提供的EAD及/或就业许可的自动延期,例如根据《移民与国籍法》第244条、8 U.S.C. 1254a及8 CFR第244部分向TPS申请人提供的自动延期。本最终规则同样不影响这些规定。
DHS explained in the IFR that the IFR did not impact the validity of EADs that were automatically extended prior to October 30, 2025. It also explained that the IFR did not impact automatic extensions of EADs and/or employment authorization provided by law or Federal Register notices, such as those for TPS applicants pursuant to section 244 of the Act, 8 U.S.C. 1254a, and 8 CFR part 244.3 This final rule similarly does not impact these provisions.
D. 成本与收益 本最终规则已被认定为行政命令12866第3(f)(1)节所定义的“重大监管行动”,并具有经济重大性,因为其 3 然而,2025年7月4日,国会通过并经总统签署的《One Big Beautiful Bill Act》(公法119-21,139 Stat. 72,2025年7月4日)(众议院法案H.R. 1)第100003(c)条及第100012(a)条,将根据《移民与国籍法》第244条、8 U.S.C. 1254a获得TPS身份的外国人的就业许可有效期限限定为一年或TPS指定期限,以两者中较短者为准。
D. Costs and Benefits This final rule has been designated a “significant regulatory action” and economically significant as defined under section 3(f)(1) of E.O. 12866, because its 3 However, on July 4, 2025, Congress passed and the President signed into law sections 100003(c) and 100012(a) of the One Big Beautiful Bill Act, Pub. L. 119-21, 139 Stat.72 (July 4, 2025) (H.R 1), which limit the validity period of any employment authorization for aliens granted TPS under section 244 of the INA, 8 U.S.C. 1254a, to a period of one year or for the duration of the designation of TPS, whichever is shorter.
本分析任一年度对经济的年度影响可能超过1亿美元。因此,本规则已由管理和预算办公室(OMB)审查。
annual effects on the economy may exceed $100 million in any year of the analysis. Accordingly, this rule has been reviewed by the Office of Management and Budget (OMB).
本最终规则重申并最终确定了临时最终规则对国土安全部相关法规所作的修改,以终止对已在规定期限内提交续签申请的特定就业许可类别外国人的就业许可文件(I-766表,即EAD)自动延长有效期的做法。此项变更的目的在于优先确保对外国人进行适当的审查与甄别,并在授予新一期就业许可及/或新EAD之前确认其持续符合资格。本最终规则不影响在临时最终规则生效前已自动延长的EAD的有效性。在此前规定基于及时提交续签EAD申请而自动延长EAD有效期的各项规则中,国土安全部曾试图稳定外国人的收入并避免雇主因劳动力流动而产生的成本。然而,正如临时最终规则所述,本部已将重点转向优先保障公共安全与国家安全,并确保在授予持续就业许可之前,外国人继续符合资格并在适用情况下值得获得自由裁量权的有利裁决。参见90 FR 48799, 48816(2025年10月30日)。
This final rule reaffirms and finalizes changes made by the IFR to DHS regulations to end the practice of automatically extending the validity of employment authorization documents (Forms I-766 or EADs) for aliens who have timely filed an application to renew their EAD in certain employment authorization categories. The purpose of this change is to prioritize the proper vetting and screening of aliens and ensure continued eligibility before granting a new period of employment authorization and/or a new EAD. This final rule does not affect the validity of EADs that were automatically extended prior to the IFR. In previous rules providing for the automatic extension of EADs based on the timely filing of a renewal EAD application, DHS attempted to stabilize aliens’ earnings and avoid labor turnover costs of employers. However, as explained in the IFR, the Department has shifted focus to prioritizing public safety and national security, and ensuring that aliens continue to be eligible and merit a favorable exercise of discretion, as applicable, before being granted continued employment authorization. See 90 FR 48799, 48816 (Oct. 30, 2025).
国土安全部已就本最终规则所产生的影响准备了两类量化估算。在一个为期18个月的单一窗口期内(自2027年5月起),根据若干假设,预计约有20,000至33,000份EAD将失效。由于USCIS对续签EAD申请的处理延误,本规则可能导致部分EAD持有人因EAD失效而遭受收入损失(“收入减少”),因为在本规则下,其收入可能因EAD失效而受到中断。若其他美国工人能够相对无摩擦地替代上述外国人,则此类外国人的收入损失可能转化为对其他美国工人的有利转移。此外,就本规则导致EAD持有人职位空缺而言,这将对企业及雇员向联邦政府缴纳的税收转移产生影响。4 本规则还将给雇用受影响的失效EAD持有人的企业带来劳动力流动成本,因为在本规则下,EAD持有人的就业许可及/或文件有效性将受到中断。在进行这些估算时,国土安全部强调,涉及该影响的各项因素,包括处理时间、申请数量、续签申请提交时间以及相关的基础福利申请等方面的变化,都将导致影响程度出现差异。
DHS has prepared two types of quantified estimates of the impacts generated by this final rule. For a single 18-month window (starting in May 2027) from about 20,000 to 33,000 EADs are estimated to lapse, based on several assumptions. This rule may result in some EAD holders incurring a loss of earnings (“reduced earnings”) because of USCIS process delays for renewal EAD applications, as under this rule there could be disruption to their earnings due to a lapsed EAD. Such earnings loss to aliens could comprise beneficial transfers to other U.S. workers if the substitution of the latter for the former is relatively frictionless. Additionally, to the extent this rule affects EAD holders’ jobs going unfilled, there will be impacts to tax transfers from businesses and employees to the Federal Government.4 This rule will also generate labor turnover costs to businesses that employ the affected-lapsed EAD holders, as under this rule there would be a disruption to the EAD holders’ employment authorization and/or document validity. In conducting these estimates DHS emphasizes that variation in factors involved in the impact, including impact processing times, filing volumes, the timing of renewal filings, and underlying benefit requests, will cause variation in the impacts.
由于未来人口数量和处理时间的变化,影响估算值很可能存在较大差异。因此,美国国土安全部(DHS)针对估算值的均值(预期值)制定了涵盖高限和低限的较宽区间。据估计,本规则将导致无法轻易替换工作许可已失效外国人的雇主遭受1.381亿美元的生产力损失成本,将使美国雇主因潜在的劳动力流动而产生3.121亿美元的成本,将产生价值1.381亿美元的因收入变化而从获得工作许可的外国人转移至替代工人的转移支付,并预计在18个月期间产生1450万美元的税收减少影响。预计约有1,700至2,800家雇主将受到影响。
Due to variation in future populations and process times, there is likely to be substantial variation in the impact estimates. For this reason, DHS develops wide ranges capturing a high and low bound for the estimates, in terms of the means (expected value). This rule is estimated to result in $138.1 million in lost productivity costs to employers that are not able to easily replace aliens with lapsed EADs, costs of $312.1 million to U.S. employers from potential labor turnover, transfers due to earnings changes worth $138.1 million from employment-authorized aliens to replacement workers and is expected to generate tax reduction impacts of $14.5 million over an 18-month period. Approximately 1,700 and 2,800 employers are estimated to be impacted.
第V.B节提供了支持本分析的A-4会计报表,随附的经济分析详细说明了支持影响估算的数据和方法。
Section V.B. provides an A-4 accounting statement supporting the analysis and the accompanying economic analysis details the data and methodology supporting the impact estimates.

B. 就业许可和证明文件的自动延期 在2016年11月之前,8 CFR 274a.13(d)规定,美国公民及移民服务局(USCIS)应在收到就业许可证(EAD)申请后90天内作出裁定。如果USCIS未能在该期限内对EAD申请作出裁定,该外国人有资格申请有效期不超过240天的临时EAD。7 2016年11月18日,作为国土安全部(DHS)实施2000年《21世纪美国竞争力法》(AC21)工作的一部分,DHS发布了一项最终规则,取消了临时EAD,代之以针对特定续签申请人的最长180天自动延期期限。8 C. 将自动延期期限从最长180天增加到最长540天 自授权对某些EAD续签申请人实行最长180天自动延期期限的最终规则颁布以来,USCIS在其目标处理时限内处理初次和续签EAD申请的能力受到了各种临时性因素的不利影响,包括6 法院已承认国会已授权DHS向某些类别的外国人授予或延长就业许可。参见,例如,Wash. All. of Tech. Workers诉DHS案,50 F.4th 164, 191-192(哥伦比亚特区巡回上诉法院,2022年)(“重要的是,第1324a(h)(3)条明确承认,就业许可不必由制定法具体授予,它也可以通过法规授予。”)。DHS正在依据所有适用的权限行使这一自由裁量权,包括《国土安全法》(HSA)中所引用的权限,以及《移民与国籍法》(INA)第103、208、214、244和274A(h)(3)条,即8 U.S.C. 1103、1158、1184、1254a和1324a(h)(3)条,以及《行政程序法》5 U.S.C. 553条。参见Loper Bright Enterprises诉Raimondo案,144 S. Ct. 2244, 2263(2024年)(“当然,在涉及某一行政机构的案件中,制定法的含义很可能是该机构被授权行使一定程度的自由裁量权。国会经常制定此类法规。例如,一些法规‘明确授权’某一行政机构对特定法律术语赋予含义。其他法规则授权某一行政机构制定规则以‘填补’法定方案的细节,或在诸如‘适当’或‘合理’等留给行政机构灵活性的术语或短语所施加的限制内进行监管。”)(内部引用省略)。7 参见8 CFR 274a.13(d)(2016年)。
B. Automatic Extension of Employment Authorization and Documentation Before November 2016, 8 CFR 274a.13(d) stated that USCIS would adjudicate an EAD application within 90 days of receipt. If USCIS did not adjudicate the EAD application within that timeframe, the alien was eligible to request an interim EAD with a validity period not to exceed 240 days.7 On November 18, 2016, as part of DHS’s efforts to implement the American Competitiveness in the Twenty-first Century Act of 2000 (AC21), DHS published a final rule that eliminated Interim EADs and replaced them with a maximum 180-day automatic extension period for certain renewal applicants.8 C. Increasing the Automatic Extension Period from a Maximum of 180 Days to a Maximum of 540 Days Since the promulgation of the final rule authorizing up to 180-day automatic extension period for certain renewal EAD applicants, USCIS’ ability to process both 6 Courts have acknowledged that Congress delegated authority to DHS to grant or extend employment authorization to certain classes of aliens. See, e.g., Wash. All. of Tech. Workers v. DHS, 50 F.4th 164, 191- 192 (D.C. Cir. 2022) (“What matters is that section 1324a(h)(3) expressly acknowledges that employment authorization need not be specifically conferred by statute; it can also be granted by regulation.”). DHS is exercising this discretionary authority consistent with all applicable authorities, including the referenced authorities in the HSA, and sections 103, 208, 214, 244, and 274A(h)(3) of the INA, 8 U.S.C. 1103, 1158, 1184, 1254a, and 1324a(h)(3), as well as the Administrative Procedure Act at 5 U.S.C. 553. See Loper Bright Enterprises v. Raimondo, 144 S. Ct. 2244, 2263 (2024) (“In a case involving an agency, of course, the statute's meaning may well be that the agency is authorized to exercise a degree of discretion. Congress has often enacted such statutes. For example, some statutes ‘expressly delegate’ to an agency the authority to give meaning to a particular statutory term. Others empower an agency to prescribe rules to ‘fill up the details’ of a statutory scheme, or to regulate subject to the limits imposed by a term or phrase that leaves agencies with flexibility, such as ‘appropriate’ or ‘reasonable.’”) (internal citations omitted). 7 See 8 CFR 274a.13(d) (2016).
8 参见81 FR 82398(2016年11月18日)(AC21最终规则)。
8 See 81 FR 82398 (Nov. 18, 2016) (AC21 Final Rule).
初次和续签EAD申请在USCIS目标处理时限内的处理工作受到了多种临时性因素的不利影响。9 2022年5月10和2024年4月,11 DHS将自动延期期限从最长180天临时增加到最长540天。2022年5月和2024年4月的临时最终规则旨在减少根据8 CFR 274a.13(d)有资格获得EAD有效期自动延期的续签EAD申请人因USCIS处理延误而经历就业许可和/或EAD有效期中断的人数。
initial and renewal EAD applications within USCIS’ targeted processing times was adversely impacted by a variety of temporary circumstances.9 In May 202210 and April 2024,11 DHS temporarily increased the automatic extension from up to 180 days to up to 540 days. The May 2022 and April 2024 temporary final rules were intended to reduce the number of renewal EAD applicants eligible for an automatic extension of their EAD validity under 8 CFR 274a.13(d) experiencing lapses in their employment authorization and/or EAD validity because of USCIS processing delays.
国土安全部（DHS）于2024年12月还发布了一项最终规则12，无限期地延长了截至2022年5月4日仍待决或于2022年5月4日或之后正式提交的某些申请最长540天的自动延期。该规则旨在减少可能严重影响就业许可文件（EAD）续签处理时间的未来未知情况所造成的影响。13 D. 2025年临时最终规则取消了某些就业许可文件的自动延期 该临时最终规则（IFR）修订了国土安全部的法规，终止了自动延长某些就业许可文件有效期的做法。参见8 CFR 274a.13(e)。该临时最终规则并未影响已根据8 CFR 274a.13(d)(1)授予续签就业许可文件申请人的自动延期，前提是续签就业许可文件申请是在2025年10月30日之前提交的。参见8 CFR 274a.13(d)。该临时最终规则也未影响法律另行规定的自动延期，或根据有关续签程序的适用联邦公报通知所规定的自动延期9 参见87 FR 26614, 26617-26（2022年5月4日）（指出美国公民及移民服务局（USCIS）当时财政状况不稳、COVID-19突发公共卫生紧急事件以及I-765表格申请数量的急剧增加）；另见89 FR 24628, 24634-40（2024年4月8日）（指出转介至美国公民及移民服务局进行可信恐惧评估的案件增加，以及肯定性庇护和抗辩性庇护申请的增加，是导致就业许可文件处理时间延长的促成因素）。10 87 FR 26614（2022年5月4日）（将自动延期期限临时延长至最长540天）。11 89 FR 24628（2024年4月8日）（将自动延期期限临时延长至最长540天）。12 参见89 FR 101208（2024年12月13日）（将自动延期期限永久延长至最长540天）。
DHS also issued a final rule in December 202412 that indefinitely increased the up to 540-day automatic extension for certain applications pending on May 4, 2022, or properly filed on or after May 4, 2022. That rule was intended to reduce the effects of unknown future circumstances that could significantly impact EAD renewal processing times.13 D. The 2025 Interim Final Rule Removed Automatic Extensions for Certain Employment Authorization Documents The IFR amended DHS regulations to end the practice of automatically extending the validity of certain EADs. See 8 CFR 274a.13(e). The IFR did not impact the automatic extensions already granted to renewal EAD applicants under 8 CFR 274a.13(d)(1), if the renewal EAD application was filed before October 30, 2025. See 8 CFR 274a.13(d). The IFR also did not impact automatic extensions otherwise provided by law or in an applicable Federal Register notice regarding procedures for renewing 9 See 87 FR 26614, 26617–26 (May 4, 2022) (identifying USCIS’ precarious fiscal status, the COVID-19 public health emergency, and dramatic increases in Form I-765 filings); see also 89 FR 24628, 24634–40 (Apr. 8, 2024) (identifying an increase in referrals to USCIS for Credible Fear Assessment and an increase in affirmative and defensive asylum filings as contributing factors to increased EAD processing times). 10 87 FR 26614 (May 4, 2022) (temporarily increased the automatic extension period to up to 540 days). 11 89 FR 24628 (Apr. 8, 2024) (temporarily increased the automatic extension period to up to 540 days). 12 See 89 FR 101208 (Dec. 13, 2024) (permanently increased the automatic extension period to up to 540 days).

根据《移民与国籍法》（INA）第244条、8 U.S.C. 1254a及8 CFR第244部分规定的与临时保护身份（TPS）相关的就业文件。14 参见8 CFR 274a.13(e)。
TPS-related employment documentation pursuant to section 244 of the INA, 8 U.S.C. 1254a, and 8 CFR part 244.14 See 8 CFR 274a.13(e).
该临时最终规则对规范就业许可文件的法规作出了两项修改，具体论述如下。
The IFR made two changes to the regulations governing EAD, as discussed below.
1. 新增8 CFR 274a.13(e) 该临时最终规则新增了第(e)款，该款自该临时最终规则于2025年10月30日公布之日起立即生效。通过8 CFR 274a.13(e)，国土安全部取消了对2025年10月30日或之后提交的续签申请，就业授权和/或就业许可文件有效期提供最长540天自动延期的做法。因此，如果续签就业许可文件申请人在2025年10月30日或之后提交申请，将不再获得其就业授权和/或就业许可文件有效期最长540天的自动延期。参见8 CFR 274a.13(e)。
1. Added New 8 CFR 274a.13(e) The IFR added a new paragraph (e) that went into effect immediately with the publication of the IFR on October 30, 2025. With 8 CFR 274a.13(e), DHS eliminated the practice of providing automatic extension periods for employment authorization and/or EAD validity for up to 540 days for renewal applications filed on or after October 30, 2025. Therefore, renewal EAD applicants no longer receive an up to 540-day automatic extension of their employment authorization and/or EAD validity if they file their application on or after October 30, 2025. See 8 CFR 274a.13(e).
除非法律另有规定，或8 CFR 274a.13(d)另有规定，或根据有关续签临时保护身份相关就业文件程序的适用联邦公报通知另有规定，外国人的就业授权和/或就业许可文件有效期将按照以下各段所述情形届满：
Except as otherwise provided by law, in 8 CFR 274a.13(d), or in accordance with an applicable Federal Register notice regarding procedures for renewing TPS-related employment documentation, an alien’s employment authorization and/or EAD validity will expire as explained in the following paragraphs:
对于根据8 CFR 274a.12(a)因其身份而获得就业授权的外国人，除非法律另有规定，其就业许可文件将于该文件正面所载有效期届满日期的次日失效。参见8 CFR 274a.13(e)(1)。由于该外国人的就业授权与其在美国的身份相关联，该外国人的就业授权不会在就业许可文件所标注的日期届满。相反，该外国人的就业授权将在其在美国的身份届满或终止之日同日届满或终止。
For those aliens who are employment authorized incident to status under 8 CFR 274a.12(a), unless otherwise provided by law, their EAD will expire on the day after the end validity date stated on the face of the EAD. See 8 CFR 274a.13(e)(1). Because the alien’s employment authorization is tied to the alien’s status in the United States, the alien’s employment authorization will not expire on the date indicated on the EAD. Instead, the alien’s employment authorization will expire or terminate on the same date the alien’s status in the United States expires or terminates.
14 然而，国土安全部指出，H.R. 1法案（公法119-21号（2025年），139 Stat. 221（2025年），编入8 U.S.C. 1802-1815）第100003(c)条和第100012(a)条，将根据《移民与国籍法》第244条、8 U.S.C. 1254a获得临时保护身份的外国人的任何就业授权的有效期限制为1年或临时保护身份指定期限，以两者中较短者为准。

例如,作为L-1非移民配偶的L-2非移民外国人,其就业许可是基于身份自动获得的。参见移民与国籍法(INA)第214(c)(2)(E)条,8 U.S.C. 1184(c)(2)(E)。如果L-2非移民选择申请就业许可文件(EAD)以证明其就业授权,该EAD将于其正面所载日期届满。在某些情况下,该日期可能与L-2身份的到期日期相同。但在其他情况下,L-2身份的到期日期可能晚于EAD的到期日期,尤其是在L-2非移民在获得EAD后曾离开美国,并在返回美国时被给予一个将在EAD到期之后才届满的新身份到期日期的情况下。15在这种情况下,L-2非移民在其L-2非移民身份有效期间仍保有就业授权,即使EAD已经过期,但过期的EAD将不再是证明该L-2非移民就业授权的有效文件。16一旦该外国人不再具有L-2身份(例如,L-2非移民身份已届满),该外国人将不再作为L-2非移民而获得就业授权,因为该就业授权取决于其是否具有L-2非移民身份。
For example, an alien in L-2 nonimmigrant status as the spouse of an L-1 nonimmigrant is employment authorized incident to status. See INA sec. 214(c)(2)(E), 8 U.S.C. 1184(c)(2)(E). If the L-2 nonimmigrant chooses to apply for an EAD to evidence his or her employment authorization, the EAD will expire as of the date indicated on the face of the EAD. In some cases that may be the same date as the expiration of the L-2’s nonimmigrant status. But in other cases, the L-2 status expiration date may be after the EAD expiration date, particularly if the L-2 nonimmigrant travelled outside of the United States after obtaining an EAD and, upon return to the United States, was provided a new status expiration date that will expire after the EAD expires.15 In that scenario, the L-2 nonimmigrant would remain employment authorized while in L-2 nonimmigrant status, even after the EAD expires, but the expired EAD would no longer be a valid document to evidence the L-2 nonimmigrant’s employment authorization.16 Once the alien is no longer in L-2 status (for example, the L-2 nonimmigrant status expires), the alien would no longer be employment authorized as an L-2 nonimmigrant because such employment authorization is dependent on being in L-2 nonimmigrant status.
对于那些并非基于其移民身份而自动获得就业授权、而是必须依据8 CFR 274a.12(c)在美国境内接受雇佣之前先向美国公民及移民服务局(USCIS)申请就业授权的外国人,例如调整身份申请人或有待决庇护申请的外国人,USCIS将行使自由裁量权确定就业授权期限的长短,随后签发反映该有效期的EAD。17因此,该EAD将届满,就业授权亦将于15 在此情形下,新的身份到期日期是该外国人I-94表格(入境/离境记录文件)上所载的日期。
For aliens who are not employment authorized incident to their immigration status and who instead must obtain employment authorization from USCIS, pursuant to 8 CFR 274a.12(c) before accepting employment in the United States, such as adjustment of status applicants or aliens with a pending asylum application, USCIS determines the length of the period of employment authorization in the exercise of its discretion and thereafter issues an EAD reflecting the validity period.17 Therefore, the EAD will expire and the employment authorization will terminate the day after the end validity date stated 15 In this case, the new status expiration date is the date stated on the alien’s Form I-94, Arrival Departure document.
16 L-2身份人员仍可持有其他证明就业授权的文件,例如标注有L-2S分类的I-94表格,入境/离境记录。
16 An L-2 can still have other evidence of documentation of employment authorization, such as a Form I- 94, Arrival/Departure Record, designated with the L-2S classification.

例如,一名有待决调整身份申请(I-485表格)的外国人持有一张将于2026年5月30日届满的EAD。该外国人的调整身份申请尚未被裁决,仍处于待决状态。该外国人有资格基于该待决调整身份申请申请续签EAD。该外国人于2026年2月4日申请续签EAD,该日期晚于临时最终规则(IFR)2025年10月30日的生效日期。如果其续签申请在其现有就业授权于2026年5月30日届满之前获得批准,该外国人将保持连续的就业授权。如果该续签EAD申请在2026年5月31日仍未获裁决,则该外国人自2026年5月31日起不得继续为其雇主工作,除非该外国人另有其他依据的就业授权。参见8 CFR 274a.13(e)。如果该续签EAD申请随后获得批准,该外国人将重新获得就业授权,并可在新EAD所载有效期内恢复工作。外国人越晚提交续签EAD申请,其就业授权和/或EAD有效性出现暂时中断的可能性就越大。
For example, an alien with a pending adjustment of status application (Form I- 485) has an EAD that expires on May 30, 2026. The alien’s adjustment of status application has not been adjudicated and continues to be pending. The alien is eligible to apply for a renewal EAD based on the pending adjustment of status application. The alien applies for a renewal of the EAD on February 4, 2026, which is after the October 30, 2025, effective date of the IFR. The alien will maintain continuous employment authorization if his or her renewal application is granted before his or her current employment authorization expires on May 30, 2026. If the renewal EAD application remains unadjudicated on May 31, 2026, the alien cannot continue to work for his or her employer on or after May 31, 2026, unless the alien is employment authorized on a separate basis. See 8 CFR 274a.13(e). If the renewal EAD application is subsequently approved, the alien would again be employment authorized and may resume employment during the validity period stated on the new EAD. The longer an alien waits to file a renewal EAD application, the more likely it is that he or she may experience a temporary lapse in his or her employment authorization and/or EAD validity.
2. 修改8 CFR 274a.13(d)的标题 2024年12月13日,美国国土安全部(DHS)发布一项最终规则,修订8 CFR 274a.13(d),永久延长某些就业授权和/或就业授权文件(EAD)有效期的自动延期期限。该规则已于2025年1月13日生效。19 18 例如,根据8 CFR 274a.14的规定,就业授权也可能在EAD上显示的到期日期之前终止,如果针对该外国人启动了排除或驱逐程序;如果授予EAD所依据的条件未得到满足或已不复存在;或者如有证据表明EAD申请中所含信息并非真实和正确。 19 见89 FR 101208(2024年12月13日)。
2. Modified the heading of 8 CFR 274a.13(d) On December 13, 2024, DHS published a final rule amending 8 CFR 274a.13(d) to permanently increase the automatic extension period for certain employment authorization and/or EAD validity. The rule became effective on January 13, 2025.19 18 For example, employment authorization may also end prior to the expiration date displayed on the EAD, in accordance with 8 CFR 274a.14, if exclusion or deportation proceedings are instituted against the alien; if a condition upon which the EAD was granted has not been met or no longer exists; or upon a showing that the information contained in the request for an EAD was not true and correct. 19 See 89 FR 101208 (Dec. 13, 2024).
DHS保留了针对那些在2025年10月30日之前及时提交续签EAD申请,且在其他方面符合8 CFR 274a.13(d)要求的外国人给予自动延期的规定。临时最终规则(IFR)修订了8 CFR 274a.13(d),修改了该款的标题,以反映该款适用于在2025年10月30日之前正式提交的续签申请,从而避免在2025年10月30日之前提交的续签EAD申请与在IFR公布当日或之后提交的申请之间,就8 CFR 274a.13(d)项下授予的自动延期期限产生混淆。通过此最终规则,DHS正式采纳并最终确定了IFR所作的修改,未对相关法规作出任何其他修改。
DHS is retaining the provision granting an automatic extension for those aliens who had timely filed a renewal EAD request before October 30, 2025, and who otherwise meet the requirements of 8 CFR 274a.13(d). The IFR amended 8 CFR 274a.13(d) by revising the paragraph’s heading to reflect that the paragraph applies to renewal requests properly filed before October 30, 2025, to avoid confusion between the automatic extension period granted under 8 CFR 274a.13(d) for those renewal EAD requests filed prior to October 30, 2025, and those filed on the day of or after the publication of the IFR. With this final rule, DHS is adopting and finalizing the changes made by the IFR and not making any additional changes to the regulations.
对8 CFR 274a.13(d)标题的这一修改确保了IFR和本规则不会对那些已在2025年10月30日之前及时且正式提交续签EAD申请的外国人产生追溯性影响。对于这些外国人,根据本规则,一份表面上已过期(“表面过期”)的EAD,若与表明及时提交(即USCIS签发的I-765表格收据通知的收据日期早于EAD上的到期日期,且早于2025年10月30日)的《行动通知书》(I-797C表格)相结合,基于与表面过期EAD所载相同的就业资格类别提交的续签申请(或者,如果EAD与I-797C通知所含类别代码为A12或C19其中之一,则类别代码无需相符),将被视为自EAD正面所示到期日期起最长540天内未过期。在此类情形下,该外国人表面过期的EAD自EAD正面所示到期日期起最长540天内被视为未过期。20 美国公民及移民服务局(USCIS)已在其网站上更新相关网页,提供适当的 20 如果调整身份申请人(C09)的EAD卡与预先许可(advance parole)授权合并签发,即申请人获发组合卡(此情况下,EAD本身会标注“SERVES AS I-512 ADVANCE PAROLE”,即“兼作I-512预先许可”),则8 CFR 274a.13(d)项下最长540天的自动延期不适用于该申请人组合卡中的预先许可部分。
This change to the heading of 8 CFR 274a.13(d) ensures that the IFR and this rule do not retroactively affect those aliens who have already timely and properly filed a renewal EAD application before October 30, 2025. For these aliens, an EAD that appears on its face to be expired (“facially expired”) is considered unexpired under this rule for up to 540 days from the expiration date on the front of the EAD when combined with a Notice of Action (Form I-797C) indicating timely filing (that is, the receipt notice for the Form I-765 issued by USCIS has a receipt date that is before the expiration date on the EAD and before October 30, 2025) of the renewal application based on the same employment eligibility category as stated on the facially expired EAD (or in the case of an EAD and I-797C notice) that contains either an A12 or C19 category code, the category codes need not match). In those cases, the alien’s facially expired EAD is considered unexpired for up to 540 days from the expiration date on the face of the EAD.20 USCIS updated the web page on the USCIS website with the appropriate 20 If an adjustment of status applicant’s (C09) EAD card is combined with the advance parole authorization, i.e., the applicant is issued a combo card (in this case, the EAD itself has an annotation “SERVES AS I-512 ADVANCE PAROLE”), the up-to-540-day automatic extension under 8 CFR 274a.13(d) does not apply to the advance parole part of the applicant’s combo card.

information.21 USCIS also updated I-9 Central on the USCIS website and the Handbook for Employers, M-274, to provide employers and employees with additional guidance.22 E. Severability In issuing this final rule, like the IFR, DHS continues to intend that the rule’s various provisions be considered severable from one another to the greatest extent possible. For instance, if a court of competent jurisdiction were to hold that ending the practice of automatically extending the validity of employment authorization and/or EADs for aliens who have timely filed an application to renew their employment authorization and/or EAD in certain employment categories may only be applied to a particular category of renewal EAD applicants or in a particular circumstance, DHS would intend for the court to leave the remainder of the rule in place with respect to all other covered persons and circumstances. DHS’s overarching goal is to militate against threats to national security and public safety and to ensure that employment authorization and/or EADs are provided only after USCIS conducts adequate vetting and determines that the alien continues to be eligible and merits a favorable exercise of discretion, if applicable.
III. 最终规则中的变更 本最终规则将2025年10月30日发布于《联邦公报》第90卷第48799页的临时最终规则(IFR)中对8 CFR第274a.13(d)和(e)条的修订内容原文照准,不作任何修改,予以最终确定。 IV. 对临时最终规则的公众意见回应 A. 意见摘要 在发布临时最终规则(IFR)时,国土安全部(DHS)邀请公众通过21 鉴于该临时最终规则,名为“自动延长就业授权文件(EAD)有效期”的网页已被存档,但仍可通过以下网址查阅该网页:https://www.uscis.gov/archive/automatic-employment-authorization-document-ead-extension(最后更新于2025年10月29日)。
III.Changes in the Final Rule This final rule adopts as final, without change, the amendments to 8 CFR 274a.13(d) and (e) in the IFR, published on October 30, 2025, at 90 FR 48799. IV. Response to Public Comments on the Interim Final Rule A. 意见由多方实体和个人提交,包括律师和法律服务提供者、申请人、个人评论者、专业组织、工会、倡导团体、宗教组织、研究和社区组织,以及州和地方政府机构或民选官员。部分评论者表示支持该规则或提出了改进建议。部分评论者表示总体反对该规则,部分评论者提出了替代方案。对于部分公众意见,国土安全部无法确定评论者是支持还是反对该临时最终规则。最后,部分评论者讨论的议题超出了本规则制定的范围。国土安全部仔细审阅了针对该临时最终规则收到的所有公众意见,并在本最终规则中按主题领域对相关意见予以回应。 B. 支持该规则的意见 意见:多位评论者基于国家安全考虑支持该规则,指出有必要对非移民工人进行适当审查。具体而言,他们表示该规则将使国土安全部能够在延长非移民在美国的工作授权之前对其进行全面审查。评论者称该规则是支持公众安全与安保的“关键”措施。一位评论者强调,在美国工作是一项特权,而非一项权利,另一位评论者则表示,安全与国家安全应是政府的最高优先事项。多位评论者基于对签证系统中欺诈与滥用问题的担忧而支持该规则,例如,他们表示该规则将有助于防止潜在的欺诈与滥用。23 该等意见可通过访问联邦电子规则制定门户网站上本规则的文档查阅,网址为:https://www.regulations.gov/document/USCIS-2025-0271-0001/comment。
Comments were submitted by a range of entities and individuals, including attorneys and legal service providers, applicants, individual commenters, professional organizations, unions, advocacy groups, religious organizations, research and community organizations, and State and local government agencies or elected officials. Some commenters expressed support for the rule or offered suggestions for improvement. Some expressed general opposition to the rule, and some offered alternatives. For some of the public comments, DHS could not ascertain whether the commenter supported or opposed the IFR. Finally, some commenters addressed topics outside the scope of this rulemaking. DHS carefully reviewed all the public comments received in response to the IFR and addresses relevant comments in this final rule, grouped by subject area. B. Comments in Support of the Rule Comment: Several commenters supported the rule based on national security concerns, citing the need for proper vetting of nonimmigrant workers. Specifically, they stated that the rule would allow for thorough vetting of nonimmigrants before DHS extends their authorization to work in the United States. Commenters called the rule a “critical” measure to support the safety and security of the public. One commenter emphasized that working in the United States is a privilege, not a right, while another said that safety and national security should be government’s highest priority. Several commenters supported the rule based on concerns about fraud and abuse in the visa system, stating, for example, that it would help deter potential fraud and abuse 23 The comments can be viewed by visiting the docket for this rule on the Federal eRulemaking Portal site at https://www.regulations.gov/document/USCIS-2025-0271-0001/comment.
从而防止外国人在未对其资格作出最终裁定的情况下长期留在劳动力队伍中。
by preventing aliens from remaining in the workforce for extended periods without final adjudication of their eligibility.
许多评论者基于保护美国公民就业机会的考虑支持本规则。一位评论者指出,自动延期使非移民工人在未经及时重新评估的情况下继续就业,使其得以与美国工人竞争职位,而要求在继续就业前获得重新授权则确保只有符合资格的外国人留在劳动力队伍中。回应:美国国土安全部(DHS)同意,本规则维护了对申请人进行全面评估的程序,以确定其是否持续符合资格、识别国家安全和公共安全方面的关切,并通过严格的审查和筛查程序加强防止欺诈的能力。本届政府将美国人民的安全置于优先地位。确保只有在美国公民及移民服务局(USCIS)进行充分审查并确定该外国人持续符合资格,且在适用情况下值得获得自由裁量权的有利行使之后,才授予就业许可和/或签发就业许可文件(EAD),这能更好地保护国家安全、公共安全以及移民体系的完整性。本规则是国土安全部更广泛努力的一部分,旨在减少无意义、欺诈性及其他不具实质理由的EAD申请,并将裁决及其他资源集中用于确保国家安全和项目完整性。
Multiple commenters supported the rule based on concerns about protecting job opportunities for American citizens. A commenter stated that automatic extensions keep nonimmigrant workers employed without timely reevaluation, allowing them to compete with American workers for positions while requiring renewed authorization before continuing employment ensures that only eligible aliens remain in the workforce. Response: DHS agrees that this rule safeguards the process to fully evaluate the applicants in order to determine continued eligibility, identify national security and public safety concerns, and enhance fraud prevention through robust vetting and screening processes. This administration prioritizes the safety of the American people. Ensuring that employment authorization and/or EADs are provided only after USCIS conducts adequate vetting and determines that the alien continues to be eligible and merits a favorable exercise of discretion, if applicable, better protects national security, public safety, and the integrity of the immigration system. This rule is part of a larger DHS effort to reduce frivolous, fraudulent, and otherwise non-meritorious EAD filings and focus adjudicatory and other resources on ensuring national security and program integrity.
C. 反对本规则的评论意见及意见不一的评论
C. Comments in Opposition to the Rule and Mixed Comments
1. 国土安全部的法定权限 i. 国土安全部的法定权限与国会意图 评论意见:多位评论者要求撤回本规则,称该规则违反了国土安全部的法定职责,包括根据6 U.S.C. 111(b)(1)(F)确保“旨在确保国土安全的各项努力……不会削弱美国整体经济安全”的职责。许多评论者指出,与该法定职责相一致,国土安全部最近认定,有必要设立540天的EAD自动延期,以保护雇主和工人免受因USCIS延误而导致的工作授权中断之害,而且国土安全部此前曾认定,即便是2016年最终规则所确立的180天延期,也不足以避免工作授权出现有害的中断。
1. DHS Legal Authority i. DHS Statutory Authority and Congressional Intent Comment: Multiple commenters requested that the rule be withdrawn stating that the rule contradicts DHS’s statutory mandate, including the mandate to ensure that “the overall economic security of the United States is not diminished by efforts . . . aimed at securing the homeland” under 6 U.S.C. 111(b)(1)(F). Many of these commenters stated that, consistent with this statutory mandate, DHS recently concluded that a 540-day automatic EAD extension was necessary to protect employers and workers from lapses in work authorization due to USCIS delays, and DHS previously found that even the 180- day extension established under the 2016 Final Rule was not enough time to avoid harmful gaps in work authorization.
回应:国土安全部不同意本规则与其法定职责(包括6 U.S.C. 111(b)(1)(F)项下的职责)不一致的说法。根据6 U.S.C. 111(b),国土安全部的主要使命包括:防止在美国境内发生恐怖袭击,降低美国遭受恐怖主义威胁的脆弱性,最大限度减少在美国境内发生恐怖袭击所造成的损害并协助恢复,以及确保旨在确保国土安全的各项努力、活动和项目不会削弱美国整体经济安全。参见6 U.S.C. 111(b)(1)(A)、(B)、(C)及(F)。尽管该条款并未具体界定“经济安全”,但6 U.S.C.第1章(国土安全组织)中的另一条款将“经济安全”界定为“拥有安全且具韧性的国内生产能力,并可靠获取维持可接受生活水准及保护核心国家价值所需的全球资源的状态”。参见6 U.S.C. 474(c)(2)。24此外,国土安全部还负责执行《移民与国籍法》(INA)第274A条,即8 U.S.C. 1324a,以防止外国人非法就业。本规则作为确保国土安全各项努力的一部分,支持了国会在6 U.S.C. 111(b)及该法第274A条(8 U.S.C. 1324a)中所赋予国土安全部的使命,确保就业的合法性,并维护美国整体的国家安全与经济安全。
Response: DHS disagrees that this rule is inconsistent with DHS’s statutory mandate, including the mandate under 6 U.S.C. 111(b)(1)(F). According to 6 U.S.C. 111(b), DHS’s primary mission includes preventing terrorist attacks within the United States, reducing the vulnerability of the United States to terrorism, minimizing the damage, and assisting in the recovery, from terrorist attacks that occur within the United States, and ensuring that the overall economic security of the United States is not diminished by efforts, activities, and programs aimed at securing the homeland. See 6 U.S.C. 111(b)(1)(A), (B), (C), and (F). While the provision does not specifically define “economic security,” another provision within 6 U.S.C. Chapter 1 (Homeland Security Organization), defines “economic security” as the “condition of having secure and resilient domestic production capacity, combined with reliable access to the global resources necessary to maintain an acceptable standard of living and to protect core national values.” See 6 U.S.C. 474(c)(2).24 In addition, DHS is charged with the implementation of section 274A of the INA, 8 U.S.C. 1324a, preventing unlawful employment of aliens. This rule, which is part of an effort to secure the homeland, supports DHS’s mission as provided by Congress in 6 U.S.C. 111(b) and section 274A of the Act, 8 U.S.C. 1324a, ensures lawful employment, and supports the overall national and economic security of the United States.
终止自动延期就业许可和/或EAD的做法,增强了就业裁决中的福利完整性 24参见Sorenson诉美国财政部部长案,475 U.S. 851,860(1986年)(“制定法律解释的一般规则,假定同一法案不同部分使用的相同词语具有相同含义。”)。
Ending the practice of providing automatic extension of employment authorization and/or EADs enhances the benefit integrity in adjudications of employment 24 See Sorenson v. Sec’y of Treasury of U.S., 475 U.S. 851, 860 (1986) (“The normal rule of statutory construction assumes that identical words used in different parts of the same act are intended to have the same meaning.”).
授权申请,并通过确保外国人在获得就业授权之前得到适当的审查并被认定为符合资格(如适用,还须值得自由裁量权的有利行使),从而更好地保护公共安全和国家安全。EAD的自动延期仅通过提交及时的续期EAD申请即可延长外国人已过期的就业授权和/或EAD,而无需首先完成裁决审查及相关审查,包括解决审查过程中发现的任何不良信息。也就是说,在美国公民及移民服务局(USCIS)首先采取以下行动之前,该福利即被授予:资格认定、完成审查和筛查、解决与该外国人有关的潜在不良信息匹配项,以及(如适用)在自由裁量权行使中认定应予续期就业授权。如果没有临时最终规则(IFR)和本最终规则,外国人仍可能在存在可能将其标记为国家安全或公共安全风险的不良信息的情况下获得自动延期。因此,自动延期构成安全风险。根据《移民与国籍法》第274A条,即8 U.S.C. 1324a,国土安全部(DHS)负责打击非法就业,国会制定该条款的原因之一是非法就业和非法移民对美国及国民经济造成的不利负面影响。25 第103条 25 《移民与国籍法》第274A条,即8 U.S.C. 1324a,是作为1986年《移民改革与控制法》(Pub. L. 99-603)的一部分而编纂的,其目的是通过设立对明知雇用无证件外国人的雇主的处罚,以及禁止雇用因非法入境或移民身份不允许就业而无授权在美国工作的外国人等方式,控制美国的非法移民问题。参见众议院报告第99-682号,第46页,1986 U.S.C.C.A.N. 5649,5649-5650页。立法历史表明,国会在立法过程中考虑了包括先前研究证据在内的多项因素,这些研究证明了非法移民的不利影响,证明有必要通过立法保护美国劳工和经济。参见例如,众议院报告第99-682号,第46页
authorization requests and better protects public safety and national security by ensuring that aliens are properly vetted and determined to be eligible (and merit a favorable exercise of discretion, if applicable), for employment authorization before such authorization is provided to the alien. The automatic extension of an EAD grants the benefit of extending an alien’s expired employment authorization and/or EAD merely by filing a timely renewal EAD application and without first completing adjudicative review and related vetting, including resolution of any derogatory information identified during the vetting process. That is, the benefit is granted without USCIS first performing the following actions: an eligibility determination, completing vetting and screening checks, resolving potential hits of derogatory information in connection with the alien, and a determination that the employment authorization should be renewed in the exercise of discretion, if applicable. Without the IFR and this final rule, aliens could still obtain an automatic extension despite derogatory information that could flag them as national security or public safety risks. Automatic extensions, therefore, pose a security risk. DHS is charged with combating unauthorized employment in accordance with section 274A of the Act, 8 U.S.C. 1324a, which Congress implemented, among other reasons, because of the detrimental negative effect that unauthorized employment and illegal immigration has on the United States and the national economy.25 Sections 103 25 Section 274A of the Act, 8 U.S.C. 1324a was codified as part of the Immigration Reform and Control Act of 1986, Pub. L. 99-603, for the purpose of controlling illegal immigration to the United States by, among other things, establishing penalties for employers who knowingly hire undocumented aliens and prohibiting the employment of aliens who are unauthorized to work in the United States either because they entered the country illegally or are in an immigration status which does not permit employment. See H. Rep. No. 99-682, 46, 1986 U.S.C.C.A.N. 5649, 5649-5650. The legislative history reflects that Congress considered, among other things, evidence of prior studies demonstrating the adverse impact of illegal immigration that warranted legislation to protect U.S. labor and the economy. See, e.g., H. Rep. 99-682, 46
52, 1986 U.S.C.C.A.N. 5649, 5649–5656 [“管制的必要性因国际人口结构而更加凸显。无证移民往往来自人口增长迅速且就业机会稀少的国家。美国无力通过将这些工人吸纳进我国的经济和人口来纠正这种失衡。美国目前的失业率为7%,而在与无证工人在就业方面竞争最为直接的少数族裔群体中,失业率更高……自1971年开始的第92届国会期间,众议院司法委员会特别小组委员会就无证移民问题举行了广泛的调查和立法听证会,以及该法第274A条、8 U.S.C. 1103(a)和8 U.S.C. 1324a等条款,赋予国土安全部(DHS)和美国公民及移民服务局(USCIS)相当大的灵活性来管理和执行《移民与国籍法》(INA),包括授予就业许可和签发EAD。目前并无普遍适用的法定授权要求DHS提供自动延期。正如临时最终规则(IFR)中所解释的那样,自动延期存在这样的风险,即雇主可能在不知情的情况下继续雇用一名实际上就业许可已不再有效的外国人。参见90 FR第48810页。这包括外国人可能因不再具有使其有权工作的基础身份,而不再有权获得就业许可的情形。此外,在授予包括就业许可在内的移民利益之前,对每一名外国人进行审查一直是国会的关键考量之一。26 该规则并不取消就业许可,也不妨碍符合条件的外国人为就业目的或身份证明而获得EAD,或妨碍其寻求和维持经授权的就业。该规则不会削弱经济安全。相反,该规则有助于维持安全且具有韧性的国内生产能力这一状况,并按照国会的指示,以依法获得工作许可的美国工人来保护核心的国家价值观。因此,该临时最终规则符合DHS的法定职责,包括6 U.S.C. 111(b)(1)(F)条所规定的职责,因为它对移民事务拥有管辖权,当时的主席是众议员小彼得·W·罗迪诺(Peter W. Rodino, Jr.)。引用1975年众议院司法委员会的一份报告,‘小组委员会多数委员通过听证会得出的基本结论是,非法移民所造成的不利影响是重大的,因此有必要立法,既保护美国的劳动力和经济,又确保移民有序进入本国。’(H. Rept. 94–506, 第3页)”]。
52, 1986 U.S.C.C.A.N. 5649, 5649 – 5656 [“The need for control is underscored by international demographics. Undocumented aliens tend to come from countries with high population growth and few employment opportunities. The United States is not in a position to redress this imbalance by absorbing these workers into our economy and our population. U.S. unemployment currently stands at 7%, and is much higher among the minority groups with whom undocumented workers compete for jobs most directly. . . Extensive investigative and legislative hearings on the problem of undocumented aliens were held during the 92d Congress beginning in 1971, by the House Judiciary Subcommittee with special and 274A of the Act, 8 U.S.C. 1103(a) and 8 U.S.C. 1324a, among other provisions, provide DHS and USCIS with considerable flexibility to administer and enforce the INA, including granting employment authorization and the issuance of an EAD. There is no generally applicable statutory mandate that requires DHS to provide an automatic extension. As explained in the IFR, the automatic extension carries the risk that employers unwittingly continue to employ a worker whose employment authorization is in fact no longer valid. See 90 FR at 48810. This includes that aliens may no longer be entitled to employment authorization because they no longer have an underlying status which entitles them to work. Additionally, vetting every alien before granting an immigration benefit, including employment authorization, has been one of the key considerations of Congress.26 The rule does not eliminate employment authorizations or prevent eligible aliens from obtaining EADs for employment purposes or proof of identity; or from seeking and maintaining authorized employment. The rule does not reduce economic security. To the contrary, the rule supports the condition of having secure and resilient domestic production capacity, and protects core national values, as directed by Congress, with U.S. workers who are legally authorized to work. Therefore, the IFR is consistent with DHS’s statutory mandate, including the mandate at 6 U.S.C. 111(b)(1)(F), because it jurisdiction over immigration matters, then under the chairmanship of Representative Peter W. Rodino, Jr. Quoting from a 1975 House Judiciary Committee report, ‘The basic conclusion reached by the majority of the members of the subcommittee as a result of the hearings was that the adverse impact of illegal aliens was substantial, and warranted legislation both to protect U.S. labor and the economy, and to assure the orderly entry of immigrants into this country.’ (H. Rept. 94–506, p. 3)”].
26 例如,国会曾建议设立一个机构,“负责制定、实施、指导和监督USCIS与美国移民及海关执法局(ICE)联合开展的反欺诈行动,并在授予移民利益之前,对每一名申请人、受益人和申请方进行执法/背景调查。”参见《H.R. 4567法案配套会议报告》[报告108-774],《截至2005年9月30日财年国土安全部拨款法案》,第74页,https://www.gpo.gov/fdsys/pkg/CRPT-108hrpt774/pdf/CRPT-108hrpt774.pdf [强调为另加]。为响应这一建议,USCIS设立了欺诈与国家安全司(FDNS)。参见USCIS,DHS,“欺诈侦测与国家安全局”,https://www.uscis.gov/about-us/organization/directorates-and-program-offices/fraud-detection-and-national-security-directorate(最后更新于2026年4月16日);USCIS,“欺诈侦测与国家安全局(FDNS)”,2022年5月,https://www.uscis.gov/sites/default/files/document/brochures/FDNS_Brochure_Web_V2_508.pdf。该规则在维持总体EAD资格认定做法的同时,适当平衡了安全与审查方面的需要。

国土安全部(DHS)意识到,先前的规则制定文件曾指出,DHS当时认为,立即采取行动并提供工作许可的自动延期,有助于支持美国公民及移民服务局(USCIS,作为DHS的一个组成部分)依法有效管理工作许可的法定使命,并作为一项措施,确保整体经济安全不会因工作许可可能出现的有害断档而受损。参见,例如,89 FR 101208, 101209(2024年12月13日);89 FR 24628, 24629及24651(2024年4月8日)。正如本答复以及本规则通篇所解释的那样,DHS认为先前规则所依据的事实情况,特别是2024年12月13日的规则,已发生重大变化,其中包括DHS已采取重大措施,以减少曾使USCIS裁决资源不堪重负的EAD申请激增问题。27 此外,2024年最终规则基于当时的运营实际情况所作出的假设,即认为将会出现有害断档,从而将自动延期期限永久化。28 即使2024年最终规则确实考虑到了美国的安全态势,鉴于该规则是基于对个别申请人及其雇主可能造成的损害,而非更广泛的国家经济,因此该规则如何促进了经济安全尚不明确。由此可见,取消既能改善美国安全态势又符合移民法律的自动延期期限,将如何损害美国整体经济安全同样不明确。意见:一位意见提交者主张,在未确保及时裁决的情况下取消工作许可的自动延期,将剥夺合法27 参见90 FR 48799, 48809(2025年10月30日)。
DHS is cognizant that prior rulemakings stated that DHS believed, at the time, that taking immediate action and providing automatic extensions of employment authorization supported USCIS’ (as a component of DHS) statutory mission to administer employment authorization effectively, and as a measure to ensure that the overall economic security was not diminished by potentially harmful gaps in employment authorizations. See, e.g., 89 FR 101208, 101209 (Dec. 13, 2024); 89 FR 24628, 24629 and 24651(Apr. 8, 2024). As explained in this response, and throughout this rule, DHS believes that the factual situation underlying prior rules, in particular the Dec. 13, 2024 rule, have significantly changed, including that DHS has taken significant steps to reduce the flood of filings of EADs that overwhelmed USCIS’ adjudicative resources.27 Furthermore, the 2024 final rule made the automatic extension period permanent based on the presumption that harmful gaps would occur given the operational realities at the time.28 Even if the 2024 final rule had taken into account the security posture of the United States, it was unclear how the rule furthered the economic security given that the rule was based on potential harm to individual applicants and their employers, rather than the broader national economy. It is thus unclear how removing the automatic extension period, which improves the security posture of the United States and is consistent with immigration laws, would undermine the overall economic security of the United States. 答复:DHS不认同上述意见提交者的主张。本最终规则及临时最终规则(IFR)符合该法案第274A条,即8 U.S.C. 1324a的规定,该条款特别承认部长有权酌情决定授予工作许可。DHS不认同意见提交者关于非公民在美国享有广泛适用的工作"权利"的主张。相反,INA明确规定,非公民若要在美国合法工作,必须依法获得授权或由部长授予工作许可。参见INA第274A(h)(3)条,即8 U.S.C. 1324a(h)(3)。与意见提交者的主张相反,本规则并不影响DHS是否续签非公民工作许可的决定,其仅取消了EAD有效期内的一项中间性自动延期。如果DHS认定该非公民符合继续获得工作许可的资格,且在适用情况下值得获得有利的自由裁量,DHS可以批准续签工作许可。本最终规则仅终止了在USCIS尚未完成以下各项工作之前即自动授予继续工作许可这一惠益的做法:资格认定、完成审查和甄别核查、解决与该非公民相关的潜在不良信息匹配问题,以及对就业29 USCIS在讨论在美外国人时通常使用"非公民"(aliens)一词。"非公民"是INA所定义的一个法律术语,用于指代任何非美国公民或国民的人员。参见该法案第101(a)(1)条,即8 U.S.C. 1101(a)(1)。部分意见提交者使用了"非公民"一词,但其他人可能使用了不同的措辞(例如"人员"或"个人")。总体而言,在本前言中描述相关意见时,DHS力求如实反映这些意见中所使用的措辞,有时会以引号标明以求清晰。除此之外,DHS使用"非公民"或"申请人"、"受益人"或"请求人"等用语。
Response: DHS disagrees with these commenters’ assertions. This final rule and the IFR are consistent with section 274A of the Act, 8 U.S.C. 1324a, which, among other things, recognizes the Secretary’s authority to grant employment authorization in his or her discretion. DHS disagrees with the commenter’s assertion that aliens have a broadly applicable “right” to work in the United States. Rather, the INA makes clear that aliens, to lawfully work in the United States, must be so authorized by law or a grant of employment authorization by the Secretary. See INA 274A(h)(3), 8 U.S.C. 1324a(h)(3). Contrary to the commenters’ assertions, this rule does not impact whether DHS will renew an alien’s employment authorization; it only removes an intermediate automatic extension of the EAD period. If DHS determines that the alien is eligible for continued employment authorization and merits a favorable exercise of discretion, as applicable, DHS may grant the renewal of employment authorization. This final rule merely ends the practice of automatically granting the benefit of continued employment authorization without USCIS first performing the following actions: eligibility determination, completing vetting and screening checks, resolving potential hits of derogatory information in connection with the alien, and a determination that the employment 29 USCIS generally refers to “aliens” when discussing foreign nationals present in the United States. The term “alien” is a legal term defined in the INA that is used to describe any person who is not a citizen or national of the United States. See INA sec. 101(a)(1) of the Act, 8 U.S.C. 1101(a)(1). Some commenters used “aliens”, but others may have used different terminology (such as person or individual). In general, when describing comments in this preamble, DHS has sought to mirror the language used in those comments, sometimes with quotation marks for clarity. Otherwise, DHS has used alien, or applicant, beneficiary, or requestor.
授权应根据自由裁量权予以批准,如适用的话。评论:一位评论者指出,在为庇护申请人提供获得工作授权的能力时,国会不可能意图造成这种武断和反复无常的影响,即对已获授权工作的外国人及依赖该外国人的雇主而言,在美国公民及移民服务局(USCIS)裁定庇护申请或新的工作许可文件(EAD)之前,其在不确定的时间段内处于无授权状态。一位评论者未指出具体条款,但表示应停止实施该临时最终规则(IFR),因为其政策变化既不符合法律或立法意图,也不符合那些投票支持主张移民改革的候选人的选民的意愿。
authorization should be granted in the exercise of discretion, if applicable. Comment: A commenter stated that, when providing the ability for asylum seekers to obtain work authorization, Congress could not have intended the arbitrary and capricious impact, on both the alien authorized to work and the employer who has relied on that alien, of being without authorization for an indeterminate amount of time until USCIS adjudicates either the asylum application or a new EAD. A commenter stated, without pointing to specific provisions, that the IFR should be discontinued because its policy changes do not reflect the law or the intent of the law or even the will of the people who voted for candidates who would make immigration changes.
回应:国土安全部(DHS)不同意评论者的主张。《移民与国籍法》第208(d)(2)条,即8 U.S.C. 1158(d)(2),明确规定“庇护申请人无权获得工作授权”,但同时也授权部长可自由裁量,在庇护申请提交之日起满180天后,给予庇护申请人工作授权。该临时最终规则以及本最终规则均清楚地阐明了此项变更的法律依据,且与部长就授予庇护申请人工作授权及该授权期限所拥有的自由裁量权相一致。
Response: DHS disagrees with the commenters’ assertion. Section 208(d)(2) of the Act, 8 U.S.C. 1158(d)(2), expressly states that “[a]n applicant for asylum is not entitled to employment authorization,” though it also provides the Secretary with authority to grant employment authorization, in his or her discretion, to applicants for asylum if 180 days have passed since filing an application for asylum. The IFR, and this final rule, clearly outline the legal authority for this change, and are consistent with the Secretary’s discretionary authority regarding the grant of employment authorization for asylum applicants and the duration of such authorization.
二、宪法关切 评论:多位评论者表示,该临时最终规则引发了对《宪法第一修正案》的担忧,因为该规则暗示美国公民及移民服务局(USCIS)必须评估续签申请人是否对美国公民、文化、政府、机构或建国原则“怀有敌对态度”。评论者认为,工作授权不得以意识形态上的一致性为条件,且基于违宪因素采取的行政行为因其在宪法上存疑且不被允许而不能维持。另一位评论者表示,针对工作授权续签实行意识形态审查制度,将使美国公民及移民服务局进一步陷入国土安全部以国家安全之名压制《第一修正案》权利的“令人担忧的模式”,并为美国公民及移民服务局对个人进行归类定性打开方便之门。
ii. Constitutional Concerns Comment: Multiple commenters expressed that the IFR raises First Amendment concerns, because the IFR suggests that USCIS must evaluate whether renewal applicants “bear hostile attitudes” toward U.S. citizens, culture, government, institutions, or founding principles. The commenters reasoned that employment authorization may not be conditioned on ideological conformity and that agency action on unconstitutional factors may not be sustained as it is constitutionally questionable and impermissible. Another commenter stated that an ideological screening regime for work authorization renewals would further entrench USCIS in DHS’s “troubling pattern” of suppressing First Amendment rights in the name of national security and opens the door for USCIS to profile individuals.
答复：国土安全部不同意评论者的意见。公民和外国人可以反对各种法规、条例和政策,并公开表达这些反对意见。本规则既不会因受保护的政治信仰或结社行为而惩罚个人,也不会使其遭受诸如意识形态审查或特征画像之类的活动。本规则不会削减言论自由、新闻自由或结社及和平集会自由。相反,本规则终止了在国土安全公民及移民服务局(USCIS)未同时执行以下行动的情况下自动授予继续获得就业授权这一福利的做法：资格认定、完成审查和甄别检查、解决与该外国人有关的潜在不良信息匹配问题,或在适用情况下酌情决定是否应续发就业授权。国土安全部指出,外国人的言论自由并非绝对。参见,例如,《移民与国籍法》(INA)第212(a)(3)(B)(i)(VII)条,8 U.S.C. 1182(a)(3)(B)(i)(VII)(规定任何赞同或主张恐怖活动,或说服他人赞同或主张恐怖活动或支持恐怖组织的外国人,均不可入境),以及《移民与国籍法》第212(a)(6)(C)(ii)条,8 U.S.C. 1182(a)(6)(C)(ii)(规定任何出于联邦或州法律下的任何目的或利益,虚假陈述或曾虚假陈述自己为美国公民的外国人,均不可入境)。国土安全部在批准福利申请之前对外国人进行甄别和审查,尤其是在此类申请系由USCIS酌情批准的情况下,具有令人信服的利益。国土安全部还指出,除非个人能够证明其面临“因其言论而受到起诉的可信威胁”,即“根据某项似乎使该个人可能受保护的言论成为非法的法规[或规则]”,否则不能提出对第一修正案的质疑。参见,例如,Am. Library Ass'n v. Barr案,956 F.2d 1178, 1194 (D.C. Cir. 1992)。如上所述,本情形并非如此,因为该法案第274A条,8 U.S.C. 1324a,以及相关法规,均未使任何形式的言论成为非法,也未试图直接或间接规范或惩罚言论。相反,临时最终规则(IFR)和本最终规则使所有EAD续签申请人均接受与首次申请人或此前不符合其续签就业授权和/或EAD自动延期资格的申请人所适用的相同甄别和审查程序。
Response: DHS disagrees with the commenters. Citizens and aliens may object to various statutes, regulations, and policies and express those objections publicly. This rule neither penalizes individuals for protected political beliefs or associations nor subjects them to activities such as ideological screenings or profiling. This rule does not abridge the freedom of speech, of the press, or of association or peaceful assembly. Rather, it ends the practice of automatically granting the benefit of continued employment authorization without USCIS concurrently performing the following actions: eligibility determination, completing vetting and screening checks, resolving potential hits of derogatory information in connection with the alien, or a determination that the employment authorization should be renewed in the exercise of discretion, if applicable. DHS notes that freedom of speech for aliens is not absolute. See, e.g., INA sec. 212(a)(3)(B)(i)(VII), 8 U.S.C. 1182(a)(3)(B)(i)(VII) (stating that any alien who endorses or espouses terrorist activity, or persuades others to endorse or espouse terrorist activity or support a terrorist organization, is inadmissible), and INA sec. 212(a)(6)(C)(ii), 8 U.S.C. 1182(a)(6)(C)(ii) (stating that any alien who falsely represents, or has falsely represented, himself or herself to be a citizen of the United States for any purpose or benefit under Federal or State law is inadmissible). DHS has a compelling interest in vetting and screening aliens before granting a benefit request, particularly when such request is granted in USCIS’ discretion, as applicable DHS also notes that a challenge to the First Amendment cannot lie unless an individual establishes that he or she faces a “credible threat of prosecution” specifically for his or her speech, “under a statute [or rule] that appears to render the [individual]’s arguably protected speech illegal.” See, e.g., Am. Library Ass’n v. Barr, 956 F.2d 1178, 1194 (D.C. Cir. 1992). As indicated above, that is not the case in this scenario as neither section 274A of the Act, 8 U.S.C. 1324a, nor the associated regulations, renders any form of speech illegal or attempts to directly or indirectly regulate or penalize speech. Rather, the IFR and this final rule subject all EAD renewal applicants to the same vetting and screening procedures used for initial applications or those applicants who were not previously eligible for an automatic extension of their renewal employment authorization and/or EAD.
评论:若干评论者提出了与正当程序有关的担忧。
Comment: Several commenters raised concerns related to due process.
一位评论者援引第五修正案指出,该临时最终规则(IFR)通过取消自动延期,损害了正当程序权利和寻求庇护的权利,这将导致大量寻求庇护者和其他移民在非因自身过错的情况下丧失工作授权。另一位评论者表示,该规则以连带关系推定有罪取代了正当程序,放弃了维持公平合理移民制度的任何表象。其他评论者对该临时最终规则立即生效所涉及的正当程序问题表示担忧,称该临时最终规则的实施似乎旨在体现并推进本届政府“制造和加剧混乱与机能失调”的政策与努力。答复:国土安全部(DHS)不同意评论者关于该临时最终规则,以及本最终规则因重申并最终确定该临时最终规则,会损害正当程序或外国人申请庇护能力的主张,也不同意其会导致大量寻求庇护者或其他外国人丧失工作授权的说法。
Referencing the Fifth Amendment, a commenter stated that the IFR undermines the right to due process and the right to seek asylum by eliminating automatic extensions, which will cause large numbers of asylum seekers and other immigrants to lose employment authorization through no fault of their own. Another commenter said the rule replaces due process with guilt by association and abandons any pretense of a fair and rational immigration system. Other commenters expressed concerns about due process related to the immediate effective date of the IFR, stating that implementation of the IFR appears to be designed to reflect and further the current administration's policies and efforts toward “creating and enhancing chaos and dysfunction.” Response: DHS disagrees with the commenters’ assertions that the IFR, and this final rule by virtue of reaffirming and finalizing the IFR, undermine due process or the ability of aliens to request asylum, or that it will cause large numbers of asylum seekers or other aliens to lose employment authorization.
一般而言,美国宪法第五修正案对政府福利的正当程序权利予以保护的前提是,外国人对该福利享有应得权利,但若政府官员可自由裁量批准或拒绝该福利,则该福利不属于受保护的应得权利。参见Town of Castle Rock, Colorado v. Gonzales, 545 U.S. 748, 756 (2005)。目前并无普遍适用的法定强制性规定,要求自动延长就业授权。根据《移民与国籍法》(INA)第103条和第274A条,即8 U.S.C. 1103(a)和8 U.S.C. 1324a等条款,国土安全部(DHS)和美国公民及移民服务局(USCIS)在执行和实施《移民与国籍法》,包括批准就业授权方面,被赋予了相当大的灵活性和自由裁量权。因此,关于自动延长就业授权和/或就业授权文件(EAD)的事项,通常不存在可被认定的正当程序利益。此外,与评论者的主张相反,该规则并未暗示'连坐'或暗示移民体系不公平或不合理。一个公平合理的移民体系应确保外国人得到充分审查,并在认定该外国人有资格延长其就业授权和/或EAD,以及(如适用)值得获得有利的自由裁量权行使之前,解决任何背景调查中出现的问题。临时最终规则(IFR)中提供的示例并未暗示'连坐'。相反,这些示例凸显了在完成适当的裁决审查之前不得向外国人授予就业授权的重要性和紧迫性,以及若自动授予此类福利将给国家安全和公共安全带来的风险。
In general, the Fifth Amendment of the U.S. Constitution protects due process rights to a government benefit if the alien has an entitlement to it, but a benefit is not a protected entitlement if government officials may grant or deny it in their discretion. See Town of Castle Rock, Colorado v. Gonzales, 545 U.S. 748, 756 (2005). There is no generally applicable statutory mandate to grant automatic extensions of employment authorizations. DHS and USCIS have been provided with considerable flexibility and discretion under section 103 and 274A of the INA, 8 U.S.C. 1103(a) and 8 U.S.C. 1324a, among other provisions, to administer and enforce the INA, including granting employment authorization. Therefore, there is generally no cognizable due process interest in matters related to automatic extensions of employment authorizations and/or EADs. Additionally, contrary to the commenter’s assertion, the rule does not suggest guilt by association or that the immigration system is unfair or irrational. A fair and rational immigration system ensures that aliens are sufficiently vetted, and any background hits are resolved before a determination is made that the alien is eligible for an extension of his or her employment authorization and/or an EAD and merits a favorable exercise of discretion, as applicable. The examples provided in the IFR do not suggest guilt by association. Rather, those examples highlight the importance and urgency of ensuring that aliens are not granted employment authorization until appropriate adjudicative review has been completed, and the risks to the national security and public safety if such benefits are granted automatically.
国土安全部同样不同意有关立即实施该临时最终规则旨在或意图制造混乱或功能失调的主张。国土安全部已在该临时最终规则中说明了其立即生效实施该规则的理由。国土安全部认定,5 U.S.C. 553(b)(B)和(d)(3)规定的正当理由例外情形适用于该临时最终规则,且若将该规则的实施推迟至通知与评议程序结束以及延迟生效日期届满,将不切实际且有悖公共利益。参见90 FR 48814(2025年10月30日)。国土安全部还说明,该临时最终规则之所以立即生效,是因为该规则明显涉及美国的外交事务政策,若采用通知与评议程序以及30天的延迟生效日期,将导致不良的国际后果。参见90 FR 48816(2025年10月30日)。
DHS also disagrees with the assertion that immediate implementation of the IFR was designed or intended to create chaos or dysfunction. DHS explained in the IFR why it was implementing the rule with immediate effect. DHS concluded that the good cause exceptions in 5 U.S.C. 553(b)(B) and (d)(3) apply to the IFR and that delaying the implementation of the IFR until the conclusion of notice and comment procedures and the delayed effective date would be impracticable and contrary to public interest. See 90 FR 48814 (Oct. 30, 2025). DHS also explained that the IFR was issued with an immediate effective date because the rule clearly implicates the foreign affairs policy of the United States, and that notice and comment procedure as well as a 30-day delayed effective date would result in undesirable international consequences. See 90 FR 48816 (Oct. 30, 2025).
三、国际法与难民保护 评论意见:多位评论者指出,该临时最终规则违反了难民保护相关规定,包括《关于难民地位的1967年议定书》和《1980年难民法》。30 其中一位评论者表示,《1980年难民法》明确规定了难民享有工作、获得证件及接受教育的权利,联合国难民署(UNHCR)的指导意见也明确指出,等待案件裁决的寻求庇护者应被赋予工作权利。该评论者表示,寻求庇护者在其案件待决期间必须取得EAD方可工作,而EAD的中断限制了这些权利的享有,这或是直接影响安全工作的权利,或是因为许多服务要求出示证件,而EAD可能是唯一可获得的证明形式。另一位评论者表示,取消自动延期违反《1951年难民公约》第34条,因为由此导致的就业丧失及合法就业受阻会妨碍难民的融入和入籍。该评论者还表示,该临时最终规则违反第33条关于不驱回原则的规定,以及《难民公约》和《议定书》要求便利获得公平有效的难民身份认定程序,以作为防止被驱回的保障措施的规定。该评论者认为,由于削弱了寻求庇护者在其申请待决期间通过合法就业满足基本需求的能力,该规则阻碍了获得公平庇护程序的机会,并可能迫使外国人放弃其申请,从而可能导致其被遣返回迫害发生国。
iii. International Law and Refugee Protections Comment: Several commenters stated that the IFR violates refugee protections, including the 1967 Protocol Relating to the Status of Refugees and the Refugee Act of 1980.30 One of these commenters said the Refugee Act of 1980 specifically enshrines the right of refugees to work, obtain documentation, and access education, and UNHCR Guidance makes clear that asylum seekers awaiting adjudication of their cases are to be afforded the right to work. The commenter said asylum seekers must obtain EADs to work while their cases are pending, and gaps in EADs restrict the enjoyment of these rights either by directly impacting the right to safe work, or because many services require documentation for which an EAD may be the only form of proof available. Another commenter stated that eliminating the automatic extension was contrary to Article 34 of the 1951 Refugee Convention, because the resulting loss of employment and the bar of legal employment impede assimilation and naturalization of refugees. The commenter also said the IFR was contrary to Article 33’s nonrefoulement, and the Refugee Convention’s and Protocol’s mandate to facilitate access to fair and efficient refugee status determination procedures that function as a safeguard against refoulement. The commenter reasoned that by undermining asylum seekers’ ability to meet their basic needs through legal employment while their claims are pending, the rule obstructs access to a fair asylum procedure and risks forcing aliens to abandon their claims, potentially resulting in their return to the country of persecution.
一位评论者表示,剥夺逃离迫害人群的工作授权,违背了我们的法律义务和长期秉持的价值观。
A commenter stated that cutting off work authorization from people fleeing persecution contradicts our legal obligations and longstanding values.
回应:美国国土安全部(DHS)不同意终止为就业许可文件(EAD)续签自动延期的做法这一行为与美国在难民保护方面所承担的国际义务不符的观点,包括《难民公约》及其30 1968年,美国加入了《关于难民地位的1967年议定书》(“议定书”),该议定书采纳了《关于难民地位的1951年公约》(“公约”)的相关条款。议定书以及1980年《难民法》。尽管美国是1967年议定书的缔约国,该议定书纳入了包括1951年《难民公约》第17条、第33条和第34条在内的若干条款,但该条约并非自动执行条约,在美国法律中不具有直接可执行性。真正起规范作用的是国内实施性法律。最高法院及其他判例法明确指出,该议定书仅可作为确定国会制定1980年《难民法》时立法意图的有用参考,因为该法案旨在使美国法律与该议定书保持一致。参见,例如,INS诉Stevic案,467 U.S. 407, 428 n.22 (1984年);Khan诉Holder案,584 F.3d 773, 783 (第九巡回上诉法院,2009年)。美国国会通过1980年《难民法》实施了美国就《难民公约》若干条款所承担的义务,包括《移民与国籍法》(INA)第208条、第235条和第241条(8 U.S.C. 1158、1225和1231),以及美国国土安全部的相关法规,包括8 CFR第208部分和第1208部分。《移民与国籍法》第208(d)(2)条,即8 U.S.C. 1158(d)(2),授权部长可自行裁量,在庇护申请人提交庇护申请满180天后,授予其就业许可。
Response: DHS disagrees that ending the practice of granting automatic extensions for EAD renewals is inconsistent with the United States’ international obligations related to refugee protections, including the Refugee Convention and its 30 In 1968, the U.S. acceded to the 1967 Protocol Relating to the Status of Refugees (“Protocol”), which adopted articles of the 1951 Convention Relating to the Status of Refugees (“Convention”). Protocol and the Refugee Act of 1980. Although the United States is party to the 1967 Protocol, which incorporates, among others, Articles 17, 33, and 34 of the 1951 Refugee Convention, the treaty is not self-executing and is not directly enforceable in U.S. law. It is the domestic implementing law that governs. The Supreme Court and other case law makes clear that the Protocol serves only as a useful guide in determining congressional intent in enacting the Refugee Act of 1980, because the Act sought to bring U.S. law into conformity with the Protocol. See, e.g., INS v. Stevic, 467 U.S. 407, 428 n.22 (1984); Khan v. Holder, 584 F.3d 773, 783 (9th Cir. 2009). The United States Congress implemented U.S. obligations with respect to certain provisions of the Refugee Convention in the Refugee Act of 1980, including in sections 208, 235, and 241 of the INA, 8 U.S.C. 1158, 1225 and 1231, and DHS regulations, including 8 CFR part 208 and 1208. Section 208(d)(2) of the INA, 8 U.S.C. 1158(d)(2), provides the Secretary with authority to grant employment authorization, in his or her discretion, to applicants for asylum if 180 days have passed since filing an application for asylum.
本临时最终规则(IFR)的适用范围有限,仅终止为在特定就业类别中已及时提交EAD续签申请的外国人自动延长就业许可和/或EAD有效期的做法。无论是国际法还是美国法律,均未强制要求美国国土安全部(DHS)在外国人提交EAD续签申请时自动延长其就业许可和/或EAD的有效期,更遑论在未对其持续资格进行充分审查核实的情况下予以延长。正在等待庇护申请审理的外国人仍可申请就业许可,本规则并未限制申请EAD的能力。31 2025年12月4日,美国公民及移民服务局(USCIS)调整了包括待决庇护申请人(“C08”)类别在内的若干其他EAD类别的有效期,以加强审查与核实工作。参见美国国土安全部、美国公民及移民服务局,政策提示,主题:更新部分就业许可文件有效期(2025年12月4日)。相反,本临时最终规则以及本最终规则均与部长就庇护申请人就业许可的授予及其授权期限所享有的自由裁量权相符,也与包括对寻求庇护以躲避本国迫害者给予优待在内的国际义务相符。美国国土安全部最后指出,庇护获准者(但不包括庇护申请待决者)、难民及某些其他外国人,依据1996年《个人责任与工作机会协调法》第四编的规定,属于有资格领取用于满足基本生活需求的公共福利的“合资格外国人”。32 因此,即使因审理延迟而出现就业中断的情形,这些外国人仍有可能获得公共福利。
The IFR is limited in scope, merely ending the practice of automatically extending the validity of employment authorization and/or EADs for aliens who have timely filed an application to renew their EAD in certain employment categories. There is no mandate under international or United States law that DHS provide an automatic extension of employment authorization and/or EAD validity for aliens filing renewal EAD applications, and certainly not without adequately being vetted for continued eligibility. Aliens with a pending asylum application are permitted to still apply for employment authorization as nothing in this rule restricts the ability to apply for an EAD.31 Rather, the IFR, and this final rule, are consistent with the Secretary’s 31 On December 4, 2025, USCIS changed the validity periods for certain other EAD categories, including pending asylum seeker (“C08”) to enhance screening and vetting efforts. See DHS, USCIS, Policy Alert, Subject: Updating Certain Employment Authorization Document Validity Periods (December 4, 2025), discretionary authority regarding the grant of employment authorization for asylum applicants and the duration of such authorization, and with international obligations, including the favorable treatment of those seeking refuge from persecution in their countries. DHS finally notes that asylees (but not those with pending asylum applications), refugees, and certain other aliens are “qualified aliens” for purpose of receiving public benefits to cover basic needs under Title IV of the Personal Responsibility and Work Opportunity Reconciliation Act of 1996.32 Therefore, even if there are gaps in employment on account of adjudication delays, these aliens may be able to obtain public benefits.
评论意见:一位评论者援引《难民公约》第17条,该条规定,合法居留在东道国领土内的难民(这不仅包括已获庇护者和安置难民,还包括所有正在申请续签EAD的庇护申请外国人),应获得“在相同情形下给予外国国民的最优惠待遇”。该评论者指出,由于本规则并未终止所有申请续签者EAD有效期的自动延期(例如,通过联邦公报通知(FRN)方式延长基于临时保护身份(TPS)的EAD有效期),因此该规则使申请续签EAD的难民所获待遇低于其他外国国民所获待遇。该评论者据此得出结论,认为本规则的发布违反了《难民公约》第17条,应予撤回。

The commenter concluded that the rule is therefore issued in violation of Article 17 of the Refugee Convention and should be withdrawn.
https://www.uscis.gov/sites/default/files/document/policy-manual-updates/20251204- EmploymentAuthorizationValidity.pdf。因此,C08申请人现在必须比此前5年有效期的EAD制度下更频繁地申请续签其就业许可文件(EAD)。然而,国土安全部(DHS)无法准确预测此项政策变化是否会对EAD续签处理时间产生不利影响,因为EAD续签申请可能出现的增长可能会被其他近期或未来的政策变化所抵消。例如,2026年2月23日,DHS发布了一项拟议规则制定通知,拟修改基于待决庇护申请而申请就业许可及就业许可文件的外国人的申请和资格要求。参见91 FR 8616(2026年2月23日)。截至本最终规则发布之日,该拟议规则制定通知尚未定稿。如按拟议内容定稿,C08就业许可资格的变化可能会大幅减少最初的C08申请数量,进而从长期看减少续签申请的数量。
