答复:国土安全部(DHS)不同意评论者的意见。美国通过《移民与国籍法》(INA)第207条和第208条(即8 U.S.C. 1157、1158)及其实施细则8 CFR第207部分、第208部分和第274a部分,履行了1951年《难民地位公约》(经1967年《议定书》纳入)第17条关于“有偿工作和就业”的规定。难民(及获准庇护者)33在抵达后即有权获得就业许可,所受限制极少,并被提供获得合法永久居留权的途径,因为他们在美国的身份是永久性的。这一做法符合第17条的要求,并履行了美国的相关义务,包括确保这些人所受的限制条件不得比处于类似情况的其他外国人更为严格。即使第17条确实施加了具有约束力的义务,第17条中也没有任何内容要求DHS在DHS或移民法官就外国人是否符合庇护或难民身份要求并批准其申请作出最终裁定之前,向寻求难民身份或庇护的非公民提供就业许可。临时保护身份(TPS)申请人并不享有与难民和获准庇护者相同的保护和法律定义。获得TPS的外国人及TPS申请人仅因部长根据《法案》第244条(8 U.S.C. 1254a)及8 CFR第244部分作出的指定而暂时留在美国。作为TPS制定法的一部分,国会规定在整个指定期间内提供就业许可。参见INA第244(a)(1)(B)条和第(2)款,8 U.S.C. 1254a(a)(1)(B)和(2)。为遵守国会制定的法律,DHS通常通过《联邦公报》通知延长就业许可。但是,TPS申请人仍须作为重新指定的一部分申请续签就业许可文件,以获得就业证明。此外,TPS可能33获准庇护者是指实际身处美国境内或美国入境口岸,且因对迫害怀有《法案》第101(a)(42)条(8 U.S.C. 1101(a)(42))所界定的合理恐惧而符合难民定义的外国人。相比之下,难民是指身处美国境外、已被认定对迫害怀有恐惧的外国人。符合《法案》第208条(8 U.S.C. 1158)规定条件的获准庇护者,在第17条有偿工作和就业要求方面享有与难民相同的待遇。与评论者的主张相反,庇护申请人并非获准庇护者,也不属于第17条的适用范围。即使属于该范围,美国也是通过INA第207条和第208条(8 U.S.C. 1157、1158)履行第17条义务的。
Response: DHS disagrees with the commenters. The United States implemented the “Wage-earning and Employment” provision of Article 17 of the 1951 Refugee Convention (as incorporated through the 1967 Protocol) through sections 207 and 208 of the INA, 8 U.S.C. 1157, 1158, and implementing regulations at 8 CFR 207, 208 and 274a. Refugees (and asylees)33 have the right to employment authorization upon arrival, with minimal restrictions and are provided a pathway to lawful permanent residency because their status in the United States is permanent. This approach aligns with Article 17’s requirements and complies with the U.S. obligation including that these individuals are not subject to more restrictive conditions than other foreign nationals in similar circumstances. Even if Article 17 were imposing a binding obligation, nothing in Article 17 requires DHS to provide employment authorization to noncitizens seeking refugee status or asylum before DHS or an immigration judge has made a final determination that they meet requirements for asylum or refugee status and grant the alien’s application. TPS applicants do not have the same protections and legal definition as refugees and asylees. Aliens granted TPS and TPS applicants are only temporarily in the United States on account of the Secretary’s designation under section 244 of the Act, 8 U.S.C. 1254a, and 8 CFR part 244. As part of the TPS statute, Congress provided employment authorization for purposes of the entire designation period. See INA sec. 244(a)(1)(B) and (2), 8 U.S.C. 1254a(a)(1)(B) and (2). To comply with the law enacted by Congress, DHS is typically extending employment authorization via a Federal Register notice. However, TPS applicants still must apply for the renewal employment authorization document as part of the redesignation to obtain evidence of employment. Additionally, TPS may be 33 Asylees are aliens who are physically present in the United States or at a U.S. port of entry and who meets the definition of a refugee because he or she has a reasonable fear of persecution as defined in section 101(a)(42) of the Act, 8 U.S.C. 1101(a)(42). In contrast, a refugee is an alien located outside the United States who has been determined to have a fear of persecution. Asylees who meet the conditions of section 208 of the Act, 8 U.S.C. 1158 are afforded the same treatment as refugees in regard to the wage- earning and employment requirement of Article 17. Contrary to the commenter’s assertion, applicants for asylum are not asylees or covered by Article 17. Even if they were, the United States implemented Article 17 through sections 207 and 208 of the INA, 8 U.S.C. 1157, 1158.
终止,临时保护身份（TPS）永远不会导致永久居留权。参见移民与国籍法（INA）第244(b)(3)(B)条、第(h)款，8 U.S.C. 1254a(b)(3)(B)、(h)。因此,在临时保护身份终止后,临时保护身份受益人和申请人可能丧失在美国的工作授权。参见8 CFR第244.12条和第13条。难民（及被庇护者）一旦获得身份后,通常不会丧失其难民身份,因此也不会丧失其工作权利以及通往合法永久居留权直至最终入籍的途径。因此,该实施方案以及自动延期的终止并不违反美国的国际义务。
terminated, and TPS never leads to permanent residency. See INA sec. 244(b)(3)(B), (h), 8 U.S.C. 1254a(b)(3)(B), (h). Thus, upon termination of TPS, TPS beneficiaries and applicants may lose employment authorization in the United States. See 8 CFR 244.12 and 13. Refugees (and asylees) once granted, do not typically lose their refugee status and thus, their right to employment and path to lawful permanent residency and eventually citizenship. Therefore, the implementation scheme and the termination of automatic extension is not contrary to the United States international obligations.
2. 国土安全部（DHS）的理由说明及该规则的必要性 i. 该规则的安全考量依据 a. 一般性安全考量意见 意见：多位评论者表示,临时最终规则（IFR）仅基于一项理由：即在批准个人34的工作许可续签之前完成审查和安全核查的愿望,国土安全部并未就取消自动延期提供其他政策依据。评论者称,该临时最终规则是在没有新证据或合理解释的情况下对政策的逆转,违反了《行政程序法》（APA）。评论者表示,国土安全部所提出的唯一理由存在诸多问题,并揭示出所声称的担忧与自动延期的实际目的及影响之间存在严重不匹配。
2. DHS Justification and Need for the Rule i. Security Rationale for the Rule a. General Security Rationale Comments Comment: Multiple commenters said the IFR rests on a single asserted justification: the desire to complete vetting and security checks before approving an individual’s34 work permit renewal, and DHS offers no other policy rationale for eliminating automatic extensions. Commenters stated the IFR is a reversal in policy without new evidence or a reasoned explanation and violates the APA. The commenters stated that DHS’s sole rationale suffers from numerous problems and reveals a significant mismatch between the concern asserted and the real-world purpose and impact of automatic extensions.
另有评论者表示,国土安全部为该临时最终规则所提出的安全理由与其自身2024年最终规则中的调查结论相矛盾,后者并未发现自动延期对美国公民及移民服务局（USCIS）进行安全核查的能力产生任何不利影响。评论者表示,国土安全部并未就这一新结论提供可信的理由。34 美国公民及移民服务局在讨论身处美国的外国国民时,通常使用“外国人”一词。“外国人”是《移民与国籍法》中定义的一个法律术语,用于描述任何非美国公民或国民的人员。参见该法第101(a)(1)条,8 U.S.C. 1101(a)(1)。部分评论者使用了“外国人”一词,但其他人可能使用了不同的术语（例如“人员”或“个人”）。总体而言,在本序言中描述评论内容时,国土安全部力求遵循评论中所使用的措辞,有时为清晰起见加注引号。除此之外,国土安全部使用“外国人”,或“申请人”“受益人”或“请求人”等用语。
Additional commenters stated that DHS’s security rationale for the IFR contradicts findings in its own 2024 Final Rule, which did not identify any adverse impact of automatic extensions on USCIS’ ability to conduct security checks. Commenters stated that DHS offers no credible rationale for this new finding. Some 34 USCIS generally refers to “aliens” when discussing foreign nationals present in the United States. The term “alien” is a legal term defined in the INA that is used to describe any person who is not a citizen or national of the United States. See INA sec. 101(a)(1) of the Act, 8 U.S.C. 1101(a)(1). Some commenters used “aliens”, but others may have used different terminology (such as person or individual). In general, when describing comments in this preamble, DHS has sought to mirror the language used in those comments, sometimes with quotation marks for clarity. Otherwise, DHS has used alien, or applicant, beneficiary, or requestor.
一些评论者还表示,该规则忽视了国土安全部此前已将自动延期的适用范围严格限定于申请续签就业许可文件（EAD）的特定申请人,这“合理确保”了该机构相信相关个人仍符合工作授权资格,并保护该项目免受滥用。
commenters also stated that this rule ignores that DHS narrowly tailored the prior automatic extensions to certain applicants who applied for EAD renewals, which “reasonably assured” the agency that the individual remained eligible for employment authorization and protected the program from abuse.
答复：国土安全部不同意该临时最终规则是“在没有新证据或合理解释的情况下对政策的逆转”,也不同意该规则的理由是“所声称的担忧与自动延期的实际目的及影响之间存在不匹配”,国土安全部也不认同其未能考虑该项目的重要方面这一说法。国土安全部在该临时最终规则中已说明,其终止向就业许可文件提供自动延期的做法,是为了增强就业授权请求裁决的完整性,并通过确保在向外国人提供工作授权之前,对其进行适当审查并确认其仍符合资格（如适用,还需值得给予有利的自由裁量权行使）,从而更好地保护公共安全和国家安全。该部门还说明了此项变更的合法性,并特别承认并考虑了申请人可能因此前政策而产生的信赖利益,同时说明了为何相关政策考量胜过这些利益。该临时最终规则及本最终规则体现了国土安全部在就业授权事务中长期以来对安全、防止欺诈及利益完整性的持续关注与细化。
Response: DHS does not agree that the IFR is a “reversal of policy without new evidence or a reasoned explanation,” that the justification for the rule is a “mismatch between the concern asserted and the real-world purpose and impact of automatic extensions,” or that DHS failed to consider important aspects of the program. DHS, in the IFR, explained that it is ending the practice of providing automatic extensions of employment authorization documents to enhance integrity in adjudications of employment authorization requests and better protect public safety and national security by ensuring that aliens are properly vetted and determined to continue to be eligible (and merit a favorable exercise of discretion, if applicable), for employment authorization before such authorization is provided to the alien. The Department also explained the permissibility of the change and specifically acknowledged and considered reliance interests that applicants could have engendered by the prior policy and explained why the policy considerations outweighed these interests. The IFR and this final rule reflect a continuation and refinement of DHS’s longstanding focus on security, fraud prevention, and benefit integrity in the employment authorization context.
2016年,国土安全部(DHS)取消了临时版就业许可文件(EAD),代之以180天的自动就业许可延期,并解释称这将支持本部在应对国家所面临诸多威胁、保障国家安全方面的重要使命。因此,国土安全部认定有必要取消90天的EAD处理时限以及临时EAD的签发。35 2016年的这一变化35 2016年11月18日,作为国土安全部实施《2000年二十一世纪美国竞争力法》(AC21)相关工作的一部分,国土安全部公布了一项最终法规,取消了临时EAD,并规定了180天的自动延期期限。见联邦公报81 FR 82398(2016年11月18日)。体现了国土安全部“持续关注安全并致力于改进裁决流程,包括在文件制作方面采用技术进步措施,以减少欺诈并应对国家安全威胁”。见联邦公报81 FR 82398(2016年11月18日)(AC21最终规则)。国土安全部实施就业许可自动延期,是为了在当时的背景下,在不损害国家安全和公共安全的前提下避免就业中断。然而,无论是180天还是540天的就业许可和/或EAD自动延期,都是仅凭续签EAD申请的提交即延长就业许可,而此时美国公民及移民服务局(USCIS)尚未完成全面审查、尚未解决任何不利信息,也尚未确定申请人是否持续符合该福利的资格。如今,有关国家安全、公共安全以及移民福利完整性的担忧已达到高点,总统已通过第14159号和第14161号行政命令指示国土安全部最大限度地对外国人进行审查和甄别,并确保不向无授权的外国人提供就业许可。36 国土安全部指出,法律并未要求USCIS自动延长EAD。
In 2016, DHS eliminated the interim EAD and replaced it with the 180-day automatic extension of employment authorization and explained that it would support the Department’s vital mission of securing the nation from the many threats it faces. Therefore, DHS determined that the elimination of both the 90-day EAD processing timeframe and the issuance of interim EADs was necessary.35 This change in 2016 35 On November 18, 2016, as part of DHS’s efforts to implement the American Competitiveness in the Twenty-first Century Act of 2000 (AC21), DHS published a final regulation that eliminated interim EADs and provided for a 180-day automatic extension period. 81 FR 82398 (Nov. 18, 2016). reflected DHS’s “continued attention to security and commitment to improving adjudication processes, including technological advances in document production, to reduce fraud and address threats to national security.” See 81 FR 82398 (Nov. 18, 2016) (AC21 Final Rule). DHS implemented the automatic extension of employment authorization to avoid gaps in employment without compromising national security and public safety given the context at the time. However, the automatic extension of employment authorization and/or EADs, whether 180 days or 540 days, extends employment authorization based solely on the filing of a renewal EAD application, before USCIS has completed full vetting, resolved any derogatory information, and determined continuing eligibility for the benefit. Today, concerns regarding national security, public safety, and integrity of immigration benefits are at a high point, and the President has directed DHS, through Executive Orders 14159 and 14161, to vet and screen aliens to the maximum extent possible and to ensure that employment authorization is not provided to unauthorized aliens.36 DHS notes there is no statutory requirement for USCIS to automatically extend EADs.
国土安全部已就其政策变更作出合理解释,即相较于以往,其对不同的政策考量赋予了更大的权重。国土安全部得出结论认为,鉴于当前形势及总统的相关指示,自动延期所固有的安全风险和项目完整性风险,必须被赋予比以往推动相关规则出台的各种利益及不同临时情况更高的权重。鉴于在确定外国人是否持续符合资格之前就向其授予移民福利(包括EAD)所固有的国家安全和公共安全隐患,国土安全部认为,废止就业许可和/或EAD的自动延期是必要的补救措施。结束提供自动36 见第14161号行政命令《保护美国免受外国恐怖分子及其他国家安全和公共安全威胁》(2025年1月20日),联邦公报90 FR 8451, 8451(2025年1月30日);另见第14159号行政命令《保护美国人民免受入侵》(2025年1月20日),联邦公报90 FR 8443, 8446(2025年1月29日)。就业许可和/或EAD延期的做法,使国土安全部能够更好地保护公众安全并增强福利完整性。
DHS has provided a reasonable explanation for changing its policy, namely by giving greater weight to different policy considerations than in the past. DHS has concluded that, owing to current conditions and presidential directives, the security and program‑integrity risks inherent in automatic extensions must be given greater weight than the benefits and different temporary circumstances that motivated prior rules. Given the inherent national security and public safety concerns that flow from granting immigration benefits (including EADs) to aliens before determining continued eligibility, DHS believes that rescinding the automatic extension of employment authorization and/or EADs is a necessary remedy. Ending the practice of providing automatic 36 See E.O. 14161, Protecting the United States from Foreign Terrorists and Other National Security and Public Safety Threats (Jan. 20, 2025), 90 FR 8451, 8451 (Jan. 30, 2025); see E.O. 14159, Protecting the American People Against Invasion (Jan. 20, 2025), 90 FR 8443, 8446 (Jan. 29, 2025). extensions of employment authorization and/or EADs enables DHS to better protect the public’s safety and enhance benefit integrity.
b. 缺乏支持安全理由的证据 评论：许多评论者表示，国土安全部（DHS）未提供证据或数据支持其所称的自动延期构成安全风险，或取消自动延期将有意义地加强审查的主张。多位评论者表示，缺乏任何此类证据表明所谓的问题并不存在。若干评论者写道，近十年来，数百万工人在自动延期制度下工作，但临时最终规则（IFR）未提供任何证据表明该制度导致安全风险持续存在，或导致后来基于安全原因的拒绝。 回应：国土安全部不同意评论者的主张。国土安全部近十年前在其《21世纪美国竞争力法》（AC21）最终规则中已说明，其正通过取消临时就业授权文件（EAD）以及强制性的90天EAD处理时限，优先考虑国家安全和欺诈问题。具体而言，国土安全部指出，“在签发就业授权证明之前，无论是以临时方式还是以其他方式，降低资格审查和安全审查的水平，都将既违背其核心使命，又会损害所签发文件的安全性、质量和完整性。”37 与该理由一致，并如临时最终规则所述，国土安全部现在认为，取消就业授权和/或EAD的自动延期，将确保在续签就业授权和/或EAD之前，完成所有身份核实、公共安全和安全相关审查，并已作出申请人继续符合资格且（如适用）值得获得有利裁量权行使的认定。临时最终规则还清楚说明了博尔德事件的相关性及其与第37条的联系。 37 参见81联邦纪事82398页（2016年11月18日）（AC21最终规则）。
b. Lack of Evidence Supporting Security Rationale Comment: Numerous commenters stated that DHS did not present evidence or data supporting the claim that automatic extensions pose security risks or that eliminating extensions would meaningfully enhance screening. Multiple commenters stated that the absence of any such evidence demonstrates that the purported problem does not exist. Several commenters wrote that for nearly 10 years, millions of workers have worked under automatic extensions, but the IFR provided no evidence showing that this system allowed security risks to persist or resulted in later security-based denials. Response: DHS disagrees with the commenters’ assertions. DHS explained in its AC21 Final Rule nearly 10 years ago that it was prioritizing national security and fraud concerns by eliminating the interim EAD and the mandated EAD 90-day processing timeframe. Specifically, DHS noted that “any reduction in the level of eligibility and security vetting before issuing evidence of employment authorization, whether on an interim basis or otherwise, would both be contrary to its core mission and undermine the security, quality, and integrity of the documents issued.”37 Consistent with that rationale, and as explained in the IFR, DHS now believes that eliminating the automatic extension of employment authorization and/or EADs will ensure that all identity verification, public safety and security related checks are completed, and that a determination has been made that the applicant continues to be eligible and merits a favorable exercise of discretion, as applicable, before renewing employment authorization and/or an EAD. The IFR also clearly explained the relevance of the Boulder incident and its connection to the 37 See 81 FR 82398 (Nov. 18, 2016) (AC21 Final Rule).
自动延期，38 以及为何该措施具有正当性。基于EAD续签申请的提交而维持自动延期，将与国土安全部所声明的福利完整性、安全及防欺诈目标不一致。 评论：许多评论者表示，国土安全部依赖发生在科罗拉多州博尔德市的单一个人轶事事件作为证据，不足以证明一项对数十万守法工人及其雇主产生负面影响的政策逆转是合理的。评论者写道，国土安全部未证明该事件涉及自动延期，也未说明取消自动延期将如何本可防止该事件的发生。
automatic extension,38 and why the measure was justified. Maintaining automatic extensions based upon the filing of an EAD renewal application would be inconsistent with DHS’s stated benefit integrity, security and fraud‑prevention objectives. Comment: Many commenters stated that DHS’s reliance on a single anecdotal incident involving an individual in Boulder, Colorado, was insufficient evidence to justify a policy reversal negatively affecting hundreds of thousands of law-abiding workers and their employers. Commenters wrote that DHS did not demonstrate that the incident involved an automatic extension or how eliminating automatic extensions would have prevented this incident.
回应：国土安全部不同意评论者所称临时最终规则依赖的证据不足以证明该规则政策变更合理性的说法。临时最终规则及本最终规则所应对的担忧是，在自动延期框架下，仅凭续签申请的提交，外国人的就业授权和/或EAD有效期即可延长至多540天，而无需对该外国人的资格进行充分审查，包括背景和安全审查。当就业授权在这些审查完成之前即自动延期时，可能已不再符合该福利资格的外国人，或存在公共安全或国家安全隐患的外国人，得以继续保持就业授权状态。这使得可能对公共安全或国家安全构成威胁的外国人，能够利用相关文件进一步获取其他证件，例如驾驶执照，并进而利用该证件从事不法活动。例如，某外国人可利用以自动延期EAD作为身份证明文件所获得的驾驶执照租赁汽车，随后利用该汽车实施恐怖袭击或其他犯罪活动。如临时最终规则及本最终规则通篇所述，国土安全部认定，这些公共安全和国家 38 参见90联邦纪事48799、48808页（2025年10月30日）（说明对续签EAD申请人进行充分审查的必要性，在一名持有自动延期EAD的外国人于2025年6月1日在科罗拉多州博尔德市制造造成多人受伤的恐怖袭击事件后变得显而易见）。
Response: DHS disagrees that the IFR relied on insufficient evidence to justify the IFR’s policy change as alleged by commenters. The concern addressed by the IFR and this final rule is that, under the automatic extension framework, an alien’s employment authorization and/or EAD validity could be extended for up to 540 days based only on the filing of a renewal application and without having fully vetted the alien for eligibility, including background and security checks. When employment authorization is automatically extended before those checks are complete, aliens who may no longer qualify for the benefit, or who present public safety or national security concerns, are able to remain employment‑authorized. This allows aliens who could be public safety or national security threats to use associated documentation to potentially obtain other credentials, such as driver’s licenses, and use such document, in turn, to engage in nefarious activities. For example, an alien could rent a car by using the driver’s license obtained with an automatically extended EAD as an identity document and subsequently use the car to perpetrate a terrorist attack or other criminal activity. As explained in the IFR and throughout this final rule, DHS has found that these public safety and national 38 See 90 FR 48799, 48808 (Oct. 30, 2025) (explaining that the need to fully vet applicants for renewal EADs became clear when an alien with an automatically extended EAD perpetrated a terrorist attack that injured many people in Boulder, Colorado on June 1, 2025).
安全方面的担忧，超过了在美国公民及移民服务局（USCIS）完成重新审查、评估任何新的不利信息，并（如适用）确定该外国人仍符合资格且继续值得获得有利裁量权行使期间，个别外国人就业授权和/或证件可能出现的临时中断所造成的暂时性影响。
security concerns outweigh the interim impacts that may result from a temporary lapse in an individual aliens’ employment authorization and/or documentation while USCIS completes refreshed vetting, evaluates any new derogatory information, and determines that the alien remains eligible and continues to merit a favorable exercise of discretion, if applicable.
该临时最终规则(IFR)引用了一名外国人在科罗拉多州博尔德市针对和平示威者实施的袭击事件,作为持有自动延期的外国人的一个具体实例,该事件凸显了缓解其他公共安全紧急状况的紧迫需要,也凸显了确保外国人在完成裁决审查之前不得被授予工作许可的重要性。对科罗拉多州博尔德市事件负责的外国人是其工作许可自动延期的受益人,因此是一个尚未经过资格或安全问题全面审查即保持工作许可的外国人的实例。像博尔德市这样的袭击事件很难预测,但如果在外国人每次申请移民福利时,各政府机构之间都能进行适当而彻底的审查和情报共享,这类袭击也并非不可能阻止。39 美国国土安全部(DHS)正优先完成审查后再授予移民福利,以确保公共安全和国家安全,并缓解对美国人构成的直接风险。终止仅凭提交工作许可证(EAD)续签申请即可获得的自动延期,解决了这一系统性漏洞。这确保了只有在美国公民及移民服务局(USCIS)完成以下工作后,才会授予持续的工作许可及其证明文件: 39 包括国会议员在内的公众批评了移民和执法当局,包括美国国土安全部(DHS),称其未能发现该外国人的活动,也未能保护公众。他们强调了修复安全漏洞的紧迫性。见众议院国土安全委员会,《主席普弗卢格尔、格林要求国土安全部提供有关博尔德恐怖袭击嫌疑人的文件》(2025年6月4日),https://homeland.house.gov/2025/06/04/chairmen-green-pfluger-request-dhs-documents-on-suspect-in-boulder-terror-attack(“这一悲剧表明美国本土的恐怖主义威胁正在加剧,表明迫切需要加强国土安全措施,特别是针对非法在美滞留的外国公民,据报道索利曼曾滞留逾期未离境的非移民签证。仅在今年,我们国家就已经面临了多起严重的反犹太主义恐怖行为,包括最近在我国首都发生的两名以色列大使馆工作人员遇刺事件。”)完成更新的审查、评估任何新的不利信息,并确定该外国人仍然符合资格,且(如适用)仍然应获得有利的自由裁量处理。正如行政摘要中所述,特朗普总统已指示美国国土安全部(DHS)对所有在美国境内的外国人“尽最大程度进行审查和甄别”,并确保“不得向任何未经授权的外国人提供工作许可”。40 根据总统的优先事项,美国国土安全部(DHS)正优先考虑计划的完整性、公共安全和国家安全,并认为取消工作许可证(EAD)自动延期所带来的益处超过了工作许可可能出现暂时中断的风险。 c. 对续签申请人的既往审查及现有安全措施 评论意见:一位评论者表示,美国国土安全部(DHS)“未提供任何可信证据证明”与工作许可自动延期及/或工作许可证(EAD)相关的安全风险,因此该临时最终规则(IFR)“缺乏任何实质性依据”。其他评论者声称,鉴于申请工作许可证(EAD)续签的外国人已经接受过审查,该规则的安全依据并不充分。一些评论者表示,许多工作许可证(EAD)持有人很可能在美国公民及移民服务局(USCIS)还有其他待决申请,其中包含证明材料、背景资料和身份证件,这些都可供该机构进行额外审查。
The IFR references the attack by an alien against peaceful demonstrators in Boulder, Colorado, as a concrete example of an alien in possession of an automatic extension that highlighted the urgent need to mitigate additional public safety emergencies, as well as the importance of ensuring aliens are not granted employment authorization until adjudicative review has been completed. The alien responsible for the Boulder, Colorado, incident was the beneficiary of an automatic extension of his employment authorization, and is thus one example of an alien who remained employment authorized without having undergone full vetting for eligibility or security concerns. Attacks, such as the one in Boulder, Colorado, are tough to predict, but they may not be impossible to stop with proper and thorough vetting and intelligence sharing among government agencies each time an alien is seeking immigration benefits.39 DHS is prioritizing the completion of vetting prior to granting an immigration benefit to ensure public safety and national security and to mitigate the immediate risks posed to Americans. Ending automatic extensions based on the filing of an EAD renewal application alone addresses that systemic vulnerability. It ensures that continued employment authorization and its documentation is conferred only after USCIS has 39 The public, including members from Congress, criticized immigration and law enforcement authorities, including DHS, for not detecting the alien’s activities and not protecting the public. They emphasized the urgency to fix security vulnerabilities. See House of Representatives Homeland Security Committee, “Chairman Pfluger, Green Request DHS Documents on Suspect in Boulder Terror Attack” (June 4, 2025), https://homeland.house.gov/2025/06/04/chairmen-green-pfluger-request-dhs-documents-on-suspect-in- boulder-terror-attack (“This tragedy is indicative of a heightened terrorism threat on U.S. soil, signaling an urgent need for increased homeland security measures, particularly with respect to foreign nationals who are unlawfully present in the United States, as Soliman reportedly overstayed a nonimmigrant visa. Our nation has now faced several major acts of antisemitic terror this year alone, including the recent assassination of two Israeli embassy staffers in our nation’s capital.”) completed refreshed vetting, evaluated any new derogatory information, and determined that the alien remains eligible and continues to merit a favorable exercise of discretion, if applicable. As discussed in the Executive Summary, President Trump has directed DHS to “vet and screen to the maximum degree possible” all aliens within the United States and ensure that “employment authorization is not provided to any unauthorized alien.”40 In line with presidential priorities, DHS is prioritizing program integrity, public safety, and national security and believes the benefits of eliminating automatic extensions of EADs outweigh the risks of potential temporary lapses in employment authorization. c. Prior Vetting of Renewal Applicants and Existing Security Measures Comment: One commenter stated that DHS “offered no credible evidence of security risks associated with” the automatic extension of employment authorization and/or EADs, and that the IFR therefore “lacks any substantive rationale.” Other commenters claimed the security rationale for the rule is inadequate given that aliens applying for EAD renewals have already been vetted. Some commenters said many EAD holders are also likely to have other pending applications before USCIS with evidentiary submissions, background, and identity documents that allow for additional agency vetting.
答复:国土安全部(DHS)不同意该临时最终规则(IFR)缺乏依据的说法。国土安全部正优先完成适当的审查工作,然后再签发移民福利,在未确定申请人是否继续符合资格,以及在适用情况下是否值得获得酌情有利裁量之前,不能继续延长就业授权或就业许可文件(EAD)的有效期,无论此前针对过去或待决的福利申请是否已进行过任何审查。国土安全部并非根据成文法被要求自动延长就业授权和/或EAD的有效期。相反,国土安全部正在行使其第40号脚注,见第14161号行政命令《保护美国免受外国恐怖分子及其他国家安全和公共安全威胁》(2025年1月20日),《联邦公报》第90卷第8451页,第8451页(2025年1月30日);另见第14159号行政命令《保护美国人民免受入侵》(2025年1月20日),《联邦公报》第90卷第8443页,第8446页(2025年1月29日)所赋予的权力,以构建就业授权程序,确保所提供移民福利的完整性,并更好地促进国家安全、公共安全和项目完整性。
Response: DHS disagrees that the IFR lacks a rationale. DHS is prioritizing the completion of proper vetting before issuing immigration benefits and cannot continue to extend employment authorization or EADs without determining that the applicant continues to be eligible and, when applicable, merits a favorable exercise of discretion, regardless of any prior vetting that may have been conducted in connection with past or pending benefit requests. DHS is not required by statute to provide an automatic extension of employment authorization and/or EADs. Rather, DHS is exercising its 40 See E.O. 14161, Protecting the United States from Foreign Terrorists and Other National Security and Public Safety Threats (Jan. 20, 2025), 90 FR 8451, 8451 (Jan. 30, 2025); see E.O. 14159, Protecting the American People Against Invasion (Jan. 20, 2025), 90 FR 8443, 8446 (Jan. 29, 2025). authority to structure employment authorization processes in a manner that ensures the integrity of the immigration benefit provided, and better advances national security, public safety, and program integrity.
评论意见:有评论者写道,该临时最终规则(IFR)错误地描述了安全审查的运作方式,称所有相关的背景调查程序都独立于工作人员是否持有未过期的EAD而进行。评论者表示,许多安全警示需要后续调查、跨机构协调以及对机密或敏感信息进行全面审查,这些工作可能需要相当长的时间才能完成评估,而且这些评估是在结构化的裁决框架内进行的,而不是在EAD续签申请的机械性、门槛性审查过程中进行的。
Comment: Commenters wrote that the IFR misstates how security vetting works, stating that all relevant background check processes occur independently of whether a worker has an unexpired EAD. The commenter said many security flags require follow up investigation, interagency coordination, and a full review of classified or sensitive information that can take considerable time to evaluate, and these assessments occur within the structured adjudicative framework, not during the mechanical, threshold review of an EAD-renewal request.
答复:国土安全部不同意这些评论意见的前提。虽然某些背景调查和安全审查程序可能正在进行中,但其他一些则是在美国公民及移民服务局(USCIS)收到申请时启动的,并在该裁决过程中完成。关于USCIS进行背景调查和安全审查以确定就业授权资格的权力,并无任何成文法或其他法规上的限制。此外,背景调查和审查可能是一个漫长的过程,可能需要额外的时间才能解决或做出资格认定。若要使USCIS续签某项移民福利,申请人必须符合相关的资格标准,并在适用情况下值得获得酌情有利裁量。所有申请移民福利(包括就业授权申请)的个人,都必须提交并签署申请表,授权释放USCIS为确定其是否符合所申请福利资格而可能需要的其记录中的任何信息。通过向USCIS签署并提交该表格,申请人同意接受USCIS的核实,以及USCIS将相关信息用于确定初始或持续福利资格。鉴于审查程序有时相当漫长,此前的做法允许在USCIS启动或完成背景调查信息核实之前,自动延长原本会被认定不符合资格的外国人的EAD有效期。
Response: DHS disagrees with the premise of these comments. While some background checks and security review processes may be ongoing, others are initiated when USCIS receives a request and are completed in connection with that adjudication. There are no statutory or other regulatory limitations on USCIS’ authority to conduct background and security checks to determine eligibility for employment authorization. Additionally, background checks and vetting can be a lengthy process that may require additional time to resolve or to make an eligibility determination. For USCIS to renew an immigration benefit, the applicant must meet the relevant eligibility standards and merit a favorable exercise of discretion, if applicable. All individuals who apply for immigration benefits, including applications for employment authorization, are required to submit and sign the application authorizing the release of any information from their records that USCIS may need to determine eligibility for the benefit sought. By signing and submitting the form to USCIS, the applicant consents to verification by USCIS, as well as use of information to determine initial or continued benefit eligibility. Given the, at times, lengthy vetting process, the previous practice allowed automatic extension of EADs to aliens that would otherwise be found ineligible after USCIS initiates or resolves background check information.
此前自动将就业授权延长最长540天的做法,将EAD积压案件管理置于项目完整性、公共安全和国家安全之上的优先地位。要求在延长就业授权和/或EAD之前完成裁决审查,能更好地契合国土安全部的审查职责,以及加强项目完整性、公共安全和国家安全的政策优先事项。
The previous practice of automatically extending employment authorization for a period of up to 540 days prioritized EAD backlog management over program integrity, public safety and national security. Requiring completion of adjudicative review before extending employment authorization and/or EADs better aligns with DHS’s vetting responsibilities and the policy priorities of enhancing program integrity, public safety, and national security.
评论意见:多位评论者表示,USCIS在续签审查过程中仍可自由进行持续性审查,并且一旦发现表明存在安全隐患的新信息,即可拒绝续签申请并立即终止就业授权。
Comment: Multiple commenters stated that USCIS remains free to conduct continuous vetting during the renewal process and deny the renewal application and terminate employment authorization immediately should it identify new information indicating a security concern.
一些评论者表示，国土安全部（DHS）已经具备开展持续筛查和审查的能力，例如通过ATLAS系统和持续移民审查（CIV）项目，这些系统能够实现对移民体系内个人信息的持续共享和监测，并指出这些系统可以随时识别安全隐患，而不仅限于续期申请期间。答复：国土安全部持续实施多种策略，以应对公共安全和国家安全方面的关切。这些工作包括但不限于使用持续筛查和审查能力、机构间信息共享，以及对身份和安全核验流程的改进。然而，这些工具并不能免除在延长工作许可之前完成适当审查和筛查的必要性。进行背景调查，无论是持续进行还是按特定间隔进行（例如，当外国人再次申请移民福利时），都只是整体审查和筛查流程中的一部分。
Some commenters stated that DHS already has the capability to perform continuous screening and vetting, such as through ATLAS and the Continuous Immigration Vetting (CIV) program, which allow for ongoing information sharing and monitoring of individuals in the immigration system, stating these systems can identify security concerns at any time, not just during the renewal application process. Response: DHS continues to implement various strategies to address public safety and national security concerns. These efforts include, but are not limited to, the use of continuous screening and vetting capabilities, interagency information‑sharing, and enhancements to identity and security verification processes. However, these tools do not obviate the need to complete appropriate vetting and screening before extending employment authorization. Conducting background checks, whether continuously or at certain intervals (for example, when an alien again applies for an immigration benefit), is only one part of the overall vetting and screening process.
此外，在背景调查过程中发现的不利信息仍必须加以审查和解决。41 国土安全部认为，在授予任何移民福利之前，例如延长工作许可和/或工作许可证（EAD），美国公民及移民服务局（USCIS）应完成审查和筛查流程，并确定该外国人仍然符合该福利的资格，且（如适用）值得给予有利的自由裁量处理。
Furthermore, derogatory information identified during the background check process must still be reviewed and resolved.41 DHS believes that before any immigration benefit is granted, such as an extension of employment authorization and/or an EAD, USCIS should complete the vetting and screening process and determine that the alien continues to be eligible for the benefit and merits a favorable exercise of discretion, as applicable.
评论：一位评论者表示，该规则低估了雇主已经采取的多层审查和监测手段，这些手段用于识别潜在的安全威胁，包括I-9表核验、E-Verify参与以及犯罪背景调查。
Comment: A commenter stated that the rule discounts the multiple layers of vetting and monitoring tools that are already being conducted by employers to identify potential security threats, including Form I-9 verification, E-Verify participation, and criminal background checks.
答复：国土安全部不会将增强项目诚信、更好地保护公共安全和国家安全的职责下放给雇主。不同雇主所进行的不同程度的审查，并不能替代USCIS所开展的审查。USCIS与美国政府内的各机构以及外国合作伙伴之间订有信息共享协议。因此，雇主所进行的审查通常无法与USCIS所能获取的信息及其审查相比拟。此外，I-9表和E-Verify并非用于国家安全和公共安全审查。所有美国雇主在聘用每一名员工时，均须填写I-9表以记录其身份和工作授权。E-Verify是一项自愿性项目，通过电子方式将I-9表上的信息与社会保障总署（SSA）和国土安全部的记录进行比对，以确认其工作资格。42 安全审查和审查的标准取决于所申请的移民福利种类及其他各种因素，每一项福利申请均有其自身的要求和薄弱环节。国土安全部 41 因此，即便有CIV系统，USCIS仍须以人工方式处理不利信息，这需要时间。参见DHS/USCIS/PIA-076《持续移民审查》第8页，https://www.dhs.gov/publication/dhsuscispia-076-continuous-immigration-vetting（最近更新于2023年3月31日）（“尽管自动化有助于识别国家安全隐患，但欺诈检测与国家安全局（FDNS）官员仍须对每一条命中结果进行人工审查，以确认信息的有效性、相关性和准确性，并判断该信息是否具有可处理性，符合国土安全部的授权。若具有可处理性，USCIS的FDNS可开展行政调查，以形成足以为裁决提供依据的调查结论。”）。42 参见E-Verify，1.1背景与概述，https://www.e-verify.gov/e-verify-user-manual-10-introduction/11-background-and-overview（最近更新于2024年5月29日）。
Response: DHS will not delegate to employers its responsibility to enhance program integrity and better protect public safety and national security. The varying levels of vetting by different employers are not a substitute for the vetting that USCIS conducts. USCIS has information-sharing agreements with various agencies within the U.S. government and foreign partners. Therefore, vetting from an employer is typically not comparable to USCIS access and vetting. In addition, Form I-9 and E-Verify are not used for national security and public safety vetting. Form I-9 is required for all U.S. employers to document identity and employment authorization for every worker hired. E- Verify is a voluntary program that electronically compares the information on Form I-9 with SSA and DHS records to confirm employment eligibility.42 Criteria for security checks and vetting depend upon the immigration benefit sought as well as various other factors, and each benefit request has its own requirements and vulnerabilities. DHS does 41 Thus, even with CIV, USCIS is required to manually resolve derogatory information, which needs time. See DHS/USCIS/PIA-076 Continuous Immigration Vetting, at 8, https://www.dhs.gov/publication/dhsuscispia-076-continuous-immigration-vetting (last updated March 31, 2023) (“Although automation assists with the identification of national security concerns, FDNS officers also manually review each hit to confirm the validity, relevancy, and accuracy of the information and to determine if the information is actionable, consistent with DHS authorities. If actionable, USCIS FDNS may perform administrative investigations to produce findings to sufficiently inform adjudications.”). 42 See E-Verify, 1.1 Background and Overview, https://www.e-verify.gov/e-verify-user-manual-10- introduction/11-background-and-overview (last updated May 29, 2024).
不认为续签就业授权应当成为既定政策目标的例外情形，这些政策目标旨在更好地保护公共安全与国家安全，并确保申请人在获得移民福利之前持续符合资格（如适用）且理应获得自由裁量权的有利裁定。美国公民及移民服务局（USCIS）正在行使其全部权力，对可能对美国国家安全和公共安全构成风险的外国人所提交的移民福利申请进行调查，同时遵守《移民与国籍法》（INA）及其他适用法律的规定，具体如国土安全部（DHS）授权令0150.1号及15006号所述。43取消基于续签申请的提交而自动延长就业授权和/或EAD有效期的做法，是这一更广泛努力的组成部分之一，旨在加强项目完整性并保障美国的安全。
not believe that renewing employment authorization should be an exception to the stated policy objectives of better protecting public safety and national security and ensuring continued eligibility and that the applicant warrants a favorable exercise of discretion (as applicable) prior to issuing immigration benefits. USCIS is exercising its full authority to investigate immigration benefit requests filed by aliens who may pose risks to the national security and public safety of the United States while adhering to the provisions of the INA and applicable laws, as outlined in DHS Delegation of Authority 0150.1 and 15006.43 The elimination of automatic extensions of employment authorization and/or an EAD based on the filing of a renewal application is one component of this broader effort to enhance program integrity and safeguard the United States.
d. 该规则不会加强安全性 评论：一位评论者表示，以“国家安全为幌子”为该规则辩护“具有讽刺意味”，因为该规则可能产生的后果是使外国人的生计陷入困境，这可能迫使他们为了生存而违反法律。另一位评论者指出，恶意外国人所造成的大多数公共安全和国家安全风险源于他们在美国的存在，而非他们的工作能力，并鼓励国土安全部研究通过合法就业获得收入所带来的风险与在美国境内存在所带来的风险相比究竟有多大。一位评论者表示，国土安全部隐含地认为失业是潜在犯罪的解药，而常识和经验证据表明事实恰恰相反。
d. Rule Would Not Strengthen Security Comment: One commenter said justifying the rule under “the guise of national security is ironic” because the rule’s likely consequence in derailing the livelihoods of aliens may push them to violate the law for survival. Another commenter stated that most of the public safety and national security risk created by malicious aliens stems from their presence in the United States rather than their ability to work and encouraged DHS to study how significant the risk associated with income generated through lawful employment is compared to the risk associated with presence on U.S. soil. A commenter stated that DHS implicitly assumes that unemployment is the antidote to potential crime when common sense and empirical evidence provides the opposite is true.
43 参见，例如，国土安全部，《授予公民及移民服务局的授权》，授权令第0150.1号（2003年6月5日），可查阅：https://www.hsdl.org/c/view?docid=234775；国土安全部，美国公民及移民服务局，《授予美国公民及移民服务局局长下达快速遣返令及执行移民法律的授权》，授权令第15006号（2025年5月2日），https://www.uscis.gov/sites/default/files/document/legal-docs/Delegation_to_USCIS_for_LE_Authorities_15006_1_1.pdf。国土安全部第0150.1号授权令依据《2002年国土安全法》制定，将《移民与国籍法》下的广泛权力授予公民及移民服务局（BCIS，现为USCIS），以管理相关法律、处理福利申请、调查潜在欺诈行为、提出起诉建议并启动遣返程序。国土安全部第15006号授权令授予USCIS局长签发逮捕令、下令立即遣返外国人、拘留或保释外国人，以及调查移民法律的民事和刑事违法行为的权力。
43 See, e.g., DHS, Delegation to the Bureau of Citizenship and Immigration Services, Delegation No. 0150.1 (June 5, 2003), available at https://www.hsdl.org/c/view?docid=234775; DHS, USCIS, Delegation to Director, United States Citizenship and Immigration Services, to Order Expedited Removal and to Enforce Immigration Laws, Delegation No. 15006 (May 2, 2025), https://www.uscis.gov/sites/default/files/document/legal- docs/Delegation_to_USCIS_for_LE_Authorities_15006_1_1.pdf. DHS Delegation Number 0150.1 is based on the Homeland Security Act of 2002 and delegates broad authority under the INA, to the Bureau of Citizenship and Immigration Services (BCIS), now USCIS, to administer the laws, process benefits, investigate potential fraud, make prosecution recommendations, and initiate removal proceedings. DHS Delegation Number 15006 grants the Director of USCIS the authority to issue warrants, order immediate removal of aliens, to detain or release aliens on bond, and the ability to investigate civil and criminal immigration law violations.
回应：国土安全部不同意该评论所称本规则将导致外国人的生计陷入困境，也不同意其暗示本规则可能迫使个人为求生存而沦为罪犯的说法。
Response: DHS disagrees with the comment that this rule will result in derailing the livelihoods of aliens and the insinuation that it may push individuals to become criminals to survive.
正如临时最终规则（IFR）中所解释的，国土安全部已采取重大措施，以减少前几年美国遭遇的空前非法移民潮之后EAD申请总量的增长。44例如，国土安全部已收紧了假释的使用，并使临时保护身份（TPS）及相关项目更好地符合法定标准。因此，自2025年以来，初次EAD申请已显著下降。USCIS还发现，此前因提交续签EAD申请而有资格获得自动延期的最大几个EAD类别的申请数量也有所下降，如表1所示。表1. 2025年1月至2026年6月期间基于待决庇护申请和身份调整申请提交的I-765表格受理件数（初次申请和续签申请） 月份及年份 C08类别（待决庇护申请）下的I-765表格受理件数 C09类别（待决身份调整申请）下的I-765表格受理件数 2025年1月 240,060 64,106 2025年2月 222,552 69,139 2025年3月 228,104 84,268 2025年4月 199,659 99,790 2025年5月 181,301 81,857 2025年6月 178,422 74,289 2025年7月 181,122 76,554 2025年8月 81,251 63,086 2025年9月 79,825 54,088 2025年10月 85,803 76,572 2025年11月 63,902 51,620 2025年12月 53,893 49,774 2026年1月 42,562 41,169 2026年2月 50,358 44,647 2026年3月 51,049 57,995 2026年4月 49,989 75,254 2026年5月 36,316 41,143 2026年6月 32,472 36,455 数据来源：国土安全部，USCIS，首席数据与分析办公室（CDAO），CLAIMS3与ELIS系统，查询于2026年7月，PAER0022044。
As explained in the IFR, DHS has taken significant steps to reduce the overall EAD filings that followed the unprecedented flood of illegal immigration into the United States during previous years.44 For example, DHS has narrowed the use of parole and better aligned TPS and related programs with statutory criteria. As a result, initial EAD applications have significantly declined since 2025. USCIS has also seen a decline in the number of filings in the largest EAD categories that were formerly eligible for automatic extensions based on the filing of a renewal EAD application, as shown in Table 1. 庇护申请(C08)和待决调整身份申请(C09)。2025年1月至2026年6月底期间,这两个类别合计约占所有I-765申请的80%。表1显示了自本届政府2025年1月上任以来C08和C09申请量的下降情况。美国公民及移民服务局(USCIS)在2025年1月共收到240,060份C08申请,到2026年6月降至32,472份。同样,USCIS在2025年1月共收到64,106份C09申请,到2026年6月降至36,455份。USCIS指出,就业授权文件(EAD)申请率的下降支持了临时最终规则(IFR)中提出的观点,即潜在的中断,尤其是任何中断的持续时间,具有推测性,且可能被自动延期所带来的危害所抵消。
asylum applications (C08) and those with pending adjustment of status applications (C09). Together these categories made up approximately 80% of all I-765 applicants from January 2025 through the end of June 2026.45 Table 1 shows how C08 and C09 filings have decreased since the start of the current administration in January 2025. USCIS received a total of 240,060 C08 filings in January 2025, which decreased to 32,472 in June 2026.46 Similarly, USCIS received a total of 64,106 C09 filings in January 2025, which decreased to 36,455 in June 2026. USCIS notes the decreased EAD filing rates support the point made in the IFR that the potential lapse, and in particular the duration of any lapse, is speculative and could be outweighed by the harm posed by automatic extensions.
最后,国土安全部(DHS)定期发布有关其活动的详细统计数据,47但出于多种原因,拒绝就通过合法就业产生收入的外国人所带来的额外国家安全风险开展研究。例如,将仅由合法就业所产生收入导致的递增风险,与因在美国存在、旅行、交往、未经授权就业或其他活动所产生的风险区分开来并加以量化,将面临重大的方法论和数据局限。相关人群数量很小,敏感的国家安全信息将限制基础案件数据的可获得性和使用,并且难以得出具有统计学意义的结论。
Finally, DHS regularly publishes detailed statistics on its activities,47 but declines to perform a study of the additional risk to national security caused by income aliens generated through lawful employment for multiple reasons. For example, isolating and quantifying the incremental risk attributable solely to income derived from lawful employment—as distinct from the risk associated with presence, travel, associations, unauthorized employment, or other activities—would present significant methodological and data limitations. The relevant population would be small, sensitive national security information would constrain the availability and use of underlying case data, and it would be difficult to draw statistically meaningful conclusions.
DHS承认评论者的观点,即恶意行为者构成的主要公共安全和45尽管数量少得多,但I-765申请在A07、A08、A10、C16、C20、C24和C31类别中也有所下降。DHS,USCIS,政策质量办公室(OPQ),《I-765就业授权申请按资格类别代码、月份和EAD类型统计的收件量》2025-2026财年(截至2026年3月5日)。CLAIMS3,ELIS,查询于2026年3月,PAER0020648;DHS,USCIS,首席数据与分析办公室(CDAO),《I-765就业授权申请按资格类别代码、月份和EAD类型统计的收件量》2025年10月30日至2026年6月30日。CLAIMS3,ELIS,查询于2026年7月,PAER0022044。46 DHS,USCIS,OPQ,《I-765就业授权申请按资格类别代码、月份和EAD类型统计的收件量》2025-2026财年(截至2026年3月5日)。CLAIMS3,ELIS,查询于2026年3月,PAER0020648;DHS,USCIS,首席数据与分析办公室(CDAO),《I-765就业授权申请按资格类别代码、月份和EAD类型统计的收件量》2025年10月30日至2026年6月30日。CLAIMS3,ELIS,查询于2026年7月,PAER0022044。

47 参见国土安全统计办公室,https://ohss.dhs.gov(最后访问日期2026年4月2日)。国家安全风险源于其在美国境内的存在,而非仅仅源于其持续工作的能力。即便如此,DHS认为运用其职权以降低与存在和就业两方面相关的风险既重要又恰当。获得合法就业机会可为恶意行为者提供资金来源、从事活动的常规掩护,以及进入某些工作场所和设施的途径。因此,在未经全面审查的情况下限制具有潜在恶意企图的外国人保留EAD和/或就业授权,并合法工作和创造收入的能力,是DHS维护福利诚信、国家安全和公共安全这一更广泛努力的重要组成部分。基于这些原因,DHS继续认为,采取措施降低恶意行为者获得或依赖就业授权及相关安全文件的可能性是值得的。
47 See Office of Homeland Security Statistics, https://ohss.dhs.gov (last visited Apr. 2, 2026). national security risk posed by malicious actors arises from their presence in the United States and not solely from their continuing ability to work. Even so, DHS considers it important and appropriate to use its authorities to reduce risks associated with both presence and employment. Access to lawful employment can provide malicious actors with financial resources, routine cover for their activities, and access to certain workplaces and facilities. Limiting the ability of aliens with potentially malicious intent to retain EADs and/or employment authorization, and to lawfully work and generate income, without full vetting is therefore a meaningful component of DHS’s broader efforts to safeguard benefit integrity, national security and public safety. For these reasons, DHS continues to view measures that reduce the likelihood that malicious actors can obtain or rely on employment authorization and related secure documentation as worthwhile.
评论:一些评论者指出,如果目标是加强审查的完整性,那么合乎逻辑的解决办法是加快案件处理速度,使所有背景调查和审核能够更快完成。一位评论者还指出,工作人员应能够在接受审查期间保留其就业授权并维持家庭生计。
Comment: Some commenters stated that, if the goal is to strengthen vetting integrity, the logical solution is to expedite case processing so that all background checks and reviews are completed more quickly. One commenter also stated that workers should be able to maintain their employment authorization and support their families while vetting occurs.
答复:国土安全部(DHS)尽一切努力尽快完成案件处理,同时注重对每位申请福利者进行全面审查资格的重要性,包括审查犯罪活动和其他安全风险。国土安全部认为,在批准续签工作许可和/或相关文件之前进行筛查、审查并确定其是否持续符合资格,对于增强项目完整性以及更好地保护公共安全和国家安全至关重要。然而,鉴于美国公民及移民服务局(USCIS)所面对的初次申请和续签EAD申请数量庞大,此类流程必然需要时间。请注意,加急处理某一案件通常意味着USCIS会将该福利申请移至处理队列的最前面,从而使可能更早提交申请的其他申请人受到不利影响,但这并不会改变正确进行筛查、审查并确定其是否持续符合资格所需的时间。就这些评论者建议USCIS应加急处理所有EAD续签申请,从而将EAD续签申请置于处理队列中其他申请之前而言,国土安全部认为这不是一个可行的解决方案,也不会增强项目完整性或更好地保护公共安全和国家安全。
Response: DHS undertakes every effort to complete case processing as expeditiously as possible while being conscious of the importance of vetting every benefit requestor thoroughly for eligibility, including vetting for criminal activity and other security risks. DHS believes screening, vetting, and determining continued eligibility before granting a renewal of employment authorization and/or documentation is essential to enhance program integrity and better protect public safety and national security. Such processes, however, especially given the volume of initial and renewal EAD applicants that USCIS encounters, necessarily take time. Note that expediting a case generally means that USCIS would move the benefit request to the front of the processing queue to the detriment of those who may have filed earlier but does not otherwise change the time needed to properly conduct screening and vetting and determine continued eligibility. To the extent that these commenters suggest that USCIS should expedite all EAD renewal applications and thus put renewal EAD applications in front of other applications in the processing queue, DHS maintains that this would not be a viable solution and would not enhance program integrity or better protect public safety and national security.
评论:多位评论者指出,该规则带来的安全收益是渐进式的,因为续签申请人已经经过了严格的审查,并且在EAD续签裁定之前不会被重新审查,并表示即使在EAD自动延期的情况下,USCIS的这种二次审查也会发生。
Comment: Multiple commenters stated that the security benefit of the rule is incremental because renewal applicants have already been robustly vetted and will not be re-vetted until the EAD renewal is adjudicated, stating that this second vetting by USCIS occurs even if EAD extensions are automatic.
答复:USCIS必须确保所有向USCIS申请移民福利的外国人符合所申请福利的资格,在适用情况下理应获得有利的自由裁量处理,并且不会对国家安全或公共安全构成威胁。在签发移民福利之前,继续调查移民福利申请,并评估申请人是否已确立持续的资格并理应获得有利的自由裁量处理(如适用),以及其是否对美国的国家安全和公共安全构成风险,这是必要的,且属于USCIS的职权范围。这包括工作许可和/或EAD的续签申请,此类申请可能揭示出初次批准时并不存在的新的不利信息或情况变化。
Response: USCIS must ensure that all aliens who request immigration benefits from USCIS are eligible for the benefit sought, merit a favorable exercise of discretion, if applicable, and do not pose a threat to national security or public safety. It is necessary and within USCIS’ authority to continue investigating immigration benefit requests and to assess whether an applicant has established ongoing eligibility and warrants favorable discretion, if applicable, and whether they pose a risk to the national security and public safety of the United States, before issuing immigration benefits. This includes renewal applications for employment authorization and/or EADs, which may reveal new derogatory information or changed circumstances that were not present at the time of the initial grant.
评论:一位评论者指出,该临时最终规则(IFR)将导致国土安全部的资源被用于EAD处理,而相关人员仍滞留在美国境内,等待有关其是否能够继续留美的裁定,并表示该规则“因USCIS未能及时处理申请而惩罚了申请人、家庭、社区和雇主”。另一位评论者同样指出,该规则“并未实质性改善国土安全”,并将转移资源,而非支持安全或运营效率。答复:国土安全部致力于确保所有进入并留在美国境内的外国人不对国家安全或公共安全构成威胁。USCIS在防止恐怖分子和其他不法分子藏匿于美国境内方面发挥着重要作用,为此,筛查和审查应优先于纯粹为求便捷的裁定。国土安全部将继续努力减少无实质内容、欺诈性和无根据的EAD申请,以腾出裁定及其他资源,从而更好地确保国家安全和项目完整性。因此,国土安全部不同意将资源集中用于认真审理EAD续签申请会“转移”安全资源的说法,相反,此类裁定本身就是USCIS的一项核心安全职能。
Comment: One commenter stated that the IFR would cause DHS resources to be spent on EAD processing while people remain in the United States without decisions on their ability to stay, stating that the rule “punishes applicants, families, communities, and employers for USCIS’ failure to timely process applications.” Another commenter similarly stated that the rule “does not meaningfully improve homeland security” and would divert resources rather than supporting security or operational efficiency. Response: DHS is dedicated to ensuring that all aliens who enter and remain in the United States do not present threats to national security or public safety. USCIS plays an instrumental role in preventing terrorists and other bad actors from harboring in the United States, and screening and vetting should be prioritized over purely expedient adjudications to that end. DHS will continue to work to reduce frivolous, fraudulent, and meritless EAD filings to free up adjudicatory and other resources to better ensure national security and program integrity. Therefore, DHS does not agree that focusing resources on careful EAD renewal adjudications “diverts” resources from security; rather, such adjudications are themselves a core security function of USCIS.
e. 关于安全理由的其他评论 评论:一些评论者对国土安全部的理由表示关切,即国土安全部认为不法分子可能从EAD自动延期以及由此产生的用于支持“不法活动”的工作收入中获益,这些评论者称该理由具有推测性、歧视性,并延续了反移民言论。一位评论者表示,这种语言对亚裔美国人社区尤为危险,因为该群体长期面临被视为“永远的外国人”的叙事,这只会助长这种“有害的刻板印象”,并支持关于亚裔是外国政府代理人的无根据猜疑。
e. Other Comments on Security Rationale Comment: Some commenters expressed concern with DHS’s rationale that bad actors could benefit from EADs being automatically extended and the income generated from working to support “nefarious activities,” stating that it is speculative, discriminatory, and perpetuates anti-immigrant rhetoric. A commenter said this is particularly dangerous language for Asian American communities who face a persistent narrative as the “perpetual foreigner” and will only contribute to that “pernicious stereotype” and support unfounded suspicions that Asians are agents of foreign governments.
答复：美国国土安全部（DHS）不同意评论者的主张。国土安全部终止了根据续签EAD申请的提交自动延长就业授权和/或EAD有效期的做法，以确保持续符合资格，并优先对外国人进行适当的审查和甄别，以推进其使命。本规则并非针对亚裔美国人社区或任何其他特定外国人群体。相反，本规则按其文本规定，适用于所有根据前8 CFR 274a.13(d)条款有资格自动延长其就业授权和/或EAD的外国人。如临时最终规则（IFR）和本最终规则所述，国土安全部优先在授予移民利益之前完成甄别工作，以增强项目完整性，更好地保护公共安全和国家安全。临时最终规则中提供的示例说明了一个更广泛的担忧，即在完成甄别之前延长就业授权可能使可能构成公共安全或国家安全风险的外国人保留就业授权。终止基于EAD续签申请提交的自动延长，确保只有在美国公民及移民服务局（USCIS）完成新一轮甄别、评估任何新的不利信息，并确定该外国人仍然符合资格且（如适用）仍值得获得有利的自由裁量之后，才授予继续的就业授权。国土安全部认为，取消EAD自动延长在项目完整性、公共安全和国家安全方面带来的益处，超过了就业授权可能出现暂时中断的风险。评论：评论者指出，尽管国土安全部反复援引所谓的国家安全担忧作为临时最终规则的理由，但该规则“未被充分强调但明确存在”的动机是总体上减少EAD申请数量。他们指出，该规则旨在普遍阻遏移民，特别是针对寻求庇护者，并且国土安全部忽视了这样一个事实，即被庇护者和难民即使其EAD上的到期日已过，仍被授权就业。一位评论者指出，尽管国土安全部声称终止为难民和被庇护者自动续签EAD的做法是为了采取“统一方法”并避免雇主产生“潜在的困惑”，但其并未量化这种潜在困惑的程度，也未说明为何至少不能为凭身份即获得就业授权的难民和被庇护者提供自动续签。
Response: DHS disagrees with the commenters’ assertions. DHS ended the practice of providing automatic extension of employment authorization and/or EADs based on the filing of a renewal EAD application to ensure continued eligibility and prioritizing the proper vetting and screening of aliens in furtherance of its mission. This rule does not target Asian American communities or any other specific group of aliens. Instead, this rule, as written, applies to all aliens who were eligible for the automatic extension of their employment authorization and/or EADs under former 8 CFR 274a.13(d). As explained in the IFR and this final rule, DHS is prioritizing the completion of vetting prior to granting an immigration benefit to enhance program integrity and better protect public safety and national security. The examples provided in the IFR illustrated the broader concern that extending employment authorization before completing vetting can allow aliens who may present public safety or national security risks to retain employment authorization. Ending automatic extensions based on the filing of an EAD renewal application ensures that continued employment authorization is conferred only after USCIS has completed renewed vetting, evaluated any new derogatory information, and determined that the alien remains eligible and continues to merit a favorable exercise of discretion, if applicable. DHS believes that the program integrity, public safety, and national security benefits of eliminating automatic extensions of EADs outweigh the risks of potential temporary lapses in employment authorization. Comment: Commenters stated that, while DHS repeatedly cites purported national security concerns as justification for the IFR, the “underemphasized yet explicit” motivation for the IFR is a reduction in EAD filings overall. They stated that the rule is intended to generally deter immigration, specifically for asylum seekers, and that DHS ignores that asylees and refugees are authorized employment even if the expiration date on their EAD has passed. One commenter stated that, while DHS claims it is ending the practice of automatically renewing EADs for refugees and asylees to take a “uniform approach” and avoid “the potential for confusion” of employers, it does not quantify the potential confusion or justify why it cannot at least provide automatic renewals for refugees and asylees, who are authorized to work incident to their status.
评论者得出结论认为，临时最终规则意在破坏并阻挠那些拥有合法工作授权人士的权益，将造成不必要的工作授权中断，制造不合理的障碍，并援引国土安全部所称的提供“全面政策解决方案”的目标，指出该规则实际上意图不必要地使雇主感到困惑、阻碍移民的工作能力，并将更多人推向“困境”。另一位评论者质疑增强安全是否为该规则的真实意图，并提出其他可能的动机，例如故意导致外国人失去身份并失去工作。
The commenters concluded that the IFR is meant to undermine and frustrate those with legal work authorization and will cause unnecessary lapses in work authorization creating unreasonable obstacles, citing DHS’s referenced goal of providing a “comprehensive policy solution” and stating the rule is actually intended to unnecessarily confuse employers, frustrate immigrants’ ability to work, and push more into “dire straits.” Another commenter questioned enhanced security as the true intent of the rule, suggesting other motives such as intentionally causing aliens to fall out of status and lose their jobs.
答复：国土安全部不同意评论者关于本规则的目标是总体上减少EAD申请、普遍阻遏移民或“不必要地使雇主感到困惑”以及“阻碍移民的工作能力”的说法。如前所述，本规则的目的是通过终止自动延长就业授权和/或EAD有效期的做法，在授予新的就业授权期限和/或新的EAD之前，优先对外国人进行适当的审查和甄别，以确保持续符合资格，并确认申请人（如适用）仍值得获得有利的自由裁量。
Response: DHS disagrees with the commenters that the goal of this rule is to reduce EAD filings overall, generally deter immigration or to “unnecessarily confuse employers” and “frustrate immigrants’ ability to work.” As previously stated, the purpose of this rule is to prioritize the proper vetting and screening of aliens, to ensure continued eligibility, and to confirm that applicants continue to merit favorable exercises of discretion, if applicable, before granting new periods of employment authorization and/or new EADs by ending the practice of automatically extending the validity of employment authorization and/or EADs.
美国国土安全部（DHS）认识到，获得庇护或难民身份的外国人，其就业系基于其庇护或难民身份而获得授权。正如中期最终规则（IFR）所解释的那样，凡因身份而获得就业授权48并且维持其相应移民身份的外国人，例如被庇护者和难民，只要其维持该移民身份，就将继续获得就业授权，无论其就业授权文件（EAD）上所载的到期日期为何。因身份而获得就业授权的外国人仍可选择提交I-765表格以获得EAD，用作身份证明。如中期最终规则所述，美国国土安全部正对所有外国人采取统一做法，终止自动延长就业授权和/或EAD的做法。意见：一位评论者表示，取消自动延长不符合第14159号行政令《保护美国人民免受入侵》的要求，该行政令责令美国国土安全部及其他机构确保不向美国境内任何未获授权的外国人提供就业授权。该评论者表示，取消自动延长对该项要求没有影响，因为此类自动延长的目的在于，在外国人已获批准就业授权的情形下，为美国公民及移民服务局（USCIS）提供充分的时间和资源，以依法依规作出稳妥的认定。48 参见8 CFR 274a.12(a)。
DHS recognizes that aliens who are granted asylum or refugee status are authorized employment incident to their asylum or refugee status. As explained in the IFR, aliens who are authorized employment incident to their status48 and are maintaining their respective immigration status, such as asylees and refugees, will continue to be employment authorized while maintaining their immigration status, regardless of the expiration date on their EAD. Aliens who are authorized employment incident to status will continue to have the choice to file Form I-765 to obtain EADs for proof of identity. As stated in the IFR, DHS is taking a uniform approach for all aliens by ending the practice of providing automatic extensions of employment authorization and/or EADs. Comment: One commenter said that eliminating automatic extensions does not serve E.O. 14159, Protecting the American People Against Invasion, which orders DHS and other agencies to ensure that employment authorization is not provided to any unauthorized alien in the United States. The commenter said eliminating automatic 48 See 8 CFR 274a.12(a).
回应：美国国土安全部坚持认为，此规则符合为其统计和法规义务作出稳妥认定所需时间和资源的目的，即在外国人已获批准就业授权的情形下为美国公民及移民服务局提供此类自动延长的时间和资源。
extensions has no impact on this mandate, as the purpose of such automatic extensions is to afford USCIS the time and resources to make a robust determination in compliance with its statutory and regulatory obligations in cases where aliens were already approved for employment authorization.
回应：美国国土安全部坚持认为，本规则实现了第14159号行政令的要求。该行政令在第16节中责令国土安全部部长会同国务卿和总检察长采取一切适当行动，使各部门活动与总统所制定的政策保持一致，以确保“不向美国境内任何未获授权的外国人提供就业授权”。正如中期最终规则和本规则所述，终止自动延长EAD的做法符合第14159号行政令的该项指示，因为先前获得的就业授权并不意味着申请人将无限期地持续符合就业授权资格，有必要对资格进行审查，以确保外国人的情况未发生变化，从而仍然符合资格。在未经适当审查和充分裁决以确保持续符合资格的情况下给予就业自动延长，既不符合第14159号行政令，也不符合当前的优先事项。
Response: DHS maintains that this rule accomplishes the mandate of E.O. 14159. This executive order directs the Secretary of Homeland Security, in coordination with the Secretary of State and the Attorney General, in Section 16 to take all appropriate action to align any departmental activities with the policies set out by the President, to ensure that “employment authorization is not provided to any unauthorized alien in the United States.” As stated in the IFR and this rule, ending the practice of providing automatic extensions of EADs is consistent with this instruction in E.O. 14159 because a previous grant of employment authorization does not mean that an applicant remains indefinitely eligible for employment authorization, and it is necessary to review eligibility to ensure that an alien’s circumstances have not changed such that the alien is no longer eligible. Granting automatic extensions of employment without proper vetting and full adjudication to ensure continued eligibility is not consistent with E.O. 14159 or with current priorities.
二、美国公民及移民服务局理由的一致性及对长期政策的逆转 意见：多位评论者指出，美国公民及移民服务局表示其预计未来EAD续期处理不会出现延误，但同时又表示现在必须终止EAD自动延长，因为其需要更多时间完成安全审查。评论者表示，这两种说法不可能同时成立。评论者还指出，如果美国国土安全部预计延误将持续存在，那么终止自动延长将仅仅因为政府无法足够快地完成这些案件的裁决，而使雇员失去工作授权。这些评论者表示，当某机构的论证自相矛盾且该机构未能对该不一致之处提供充分理由时，法院会宣告相关规则无效，部分评论者援引了美国联邦通信委员会诉福克斯电视台案（F.C.C. v. Fox Television Stations, Inc.）作为依据。
ii. Consistency in USCIS Rationale and Reversal of Longstanding Policy Comment: Multiple commenters wrote that USCIS says it does not expect delays in processing EAD renewals in the future, but also says it must end automatic extensions on EADs now because it needs more time to finish security checks. Commenters said both cannot be true. Commenters also stated that, if DHS expects continued delays, then ending automatic extensions will push employees out of work authorization simply because the government cannot finish adjudicating these cases fast enough. These commenters said courts invalidate rules when an agency’s reasoning is contradictory and the agency offers no adequate justification for the inconsistency, with some commenters citing F.C.C. v. Fox Television Stations, Inc.
同样针对所称的这一不一致之处，多位评论者指出，美国国土安全部关于EAD申请数量将下降、且更多裁决资源将缩短EAD续期处理时间的结论，忽视了这些相同的资源在初始阶段同样可用于处理任何可疑风险信号。因此，该评论者表示，美国国土安全部所称在续期阶段需要进行更全面调查的必要性，今后将变得更加不必要。该评论者表示，这一说法也无法与该部一贯以来的立场相调和，即该部历来主张，只要资源充足，便能够及时完成充分的调查。
Also referencing this purported inconsistency, multiple commenters stated that DHS’s conclusion that EAD applications will decline and that more adjudicative resources will reduce EAD renewal processing times ignores that those same resources will be available at the initial stage to address any red flags. Thus, the commenter said DHS’s purported need for fuller investigations at the renewal stage will be even less necessary moving forward. The commenter said the claim also cannot be reconciled with the Department’s historic position that it can complete sufficient investigations promptly, given sufficient resources.
多位评论者表示,临时最终规则(IFR)在未给出充分解释、理由或替代性补救措施的情况下,否定并违背了2016年规则和近期2024年规则的政策,而这两项规则的目的均在于减轻因工作许可中断给工人和雇主造成的损害。一位评论者具体指出,临时最终规则未援引任何数据表明续签处理时间已出现持续缩短,未提供任何新的绩效指标,也未作出任何分析以证明2016年及2024年两次确认的长期存在的裁决难题已发生实质性变化。 答复:美国国土安全部(DHS)不认同评论者关于临时最终规则及本最终规则中的论证相互矛盾或缺乏正当理由的主张。DHS指出,临时最终规则并未声明“美国公民及移民服务局(USCIS)预计就业许可文件(EAD)处理不会出现延误”。相反,DHS所阐明的是,其已采取措施应对临时保护身份(TPS)受益人及其他申请人申请量激增的情况,且DHS预计初次申请和续签EAD的整体申请量很可能大幅下降,从而释放出裁决资源以缩短续签EAD的处理时间。49 USCIS定期评估申请量与裁决资源之间的关系,并在适当且可行的情况下重新分配工作量以应对优先事项,包括在49 参见《联邦公报》第90卷第48799页,第48809页(2025年10月30日)。
Multiple commenters said the IFR rejects and contradicts the policies of both the 2016 and the recent 2024 rules, the purpose of which was to mitigate the harm to workers and employers caused by interruptions in work authorization, without adequate explanation, justification, or alternative remedies. One commenter specified that the IFR cites no data showing sustained reductions in renewal processing times, no new performance metrics, and no analysis demonstrating that the long-recognized adjudication challenges identified in 2016 and again in 2024 have materially changed. Response: DHS disagrees with the commenters’ assertion that the reasoning in the IFR, and this final rule, is contradictory or unjustified. DHS notes the IFR does not state that “USCIS does not expect delays in processing EADs.” Instead, DHS explained it has taken measures to address floods of filings from TPS beneficiaries and other applicants, and that DHS expects that overall filing rates for initial and renewal EADs are likely to substantially decline, freeing up adjudicative resources to reduce renewal EAD processing times.49 USCIS routinely evaluates filing volumes relative to adjudicative resources and reallocates workloads to address priorities as appropriate and feasible, including between 49 See 90 FR 48799, 48809 (Oct. 30, 2025).
初次申请和续签EAD申请之间进行分配。临时最终规则阐明,尽管USCIS将持续致力于改善处理时间并减少积压,但DHS已认定自动延期与确保持续符合资格或与当前的安全审查及审查优先事项不符。DHS认为,临时最终规则中所提供的论证理由是恰当的,且临时最终规则的目的已得到充分论证。终止基于提交EAD续签申请而产生的自动延期,可确保只有在USCIS完成重新审查、评估任何新的不利信息,并确定该外国人仍然符合资格且(如适用)仍值得获得有利的自由裁量处理之后,方可授予持续的就业许可。DHS认为,取消EAD自动延期在维护项目完整性、公共安全和国家安全方面所带来的益处,超过了就业许可可能出现暂时中断的风险。
initial and renewal EAD filings. The IFR explains that, while USCIS continues to work to improve processing times and reduce backlogs, DHS has determined that automatic extensions are inconsistent with ensuring continued eligibility or with current security and vetting priorities. DHS believes that the reasoning provided in the IFR is appropriate and that the purpose of the IFR has been adequately justified. Ending automatic extensions based on the filing of an EAD renewal application ensures that continued employment authorization is conferred only after USCIS has completed renewed vetting, evaluated any new derogatory information, and determined that the alien remains eligible and continues to merit a favorable exercise of discretion, if applicable. DHS believes that the program integrity, public safety, and national security benefits of eliminating automatic extensions of EADs outweigh the risks of potential temporary lapses in employment authorization.
正如本序言第IV.C.2.i.a节中对此前一条评论的答复所详细阐述的那样,终止自动延期的决定反映出DHS在权衡运营效率与就业连续性,同另对安全及福利完整性关切之间的考量方式发生了变化,而非DHS在确保及时裁决方面的根本承诺出现了不一致。
As discussed in greater detail in response to a previous comment in Section IV.C.2.i.a. of this preamble, the decision to end automatic extensions reflects a change in how DHS balances operational efficiency and continuity of employment against these security and benefit integrity concerns rather than an inconsistency in DHS’s underlying commitment to timely adjudications.
DHS预计,总体而言,EAD申请量将大幅下降,从而释放出裁决资源以缩短续签EAD的处理时间。处理时间的缩短将减少提交续签EAD申请的外国人依赖EAD自动延期以避免潜在中断的必要性。自临时最终规则于2025年10月30日生效以来,USCIS已观察到EAD整体申请量呈稳步下降趋势。参见本序言第IV.C.2.i.d节表1。USCIS指出,EAD申请量的下降印证了临时最终规则中所提出的观点:外国人出现许可中断的可能性,尤其是任何中断的持续时间,具有推测性,其风险可能被自动延期所带来的危害所超过。
DHS expects that overall, EAD filing rates will substantially decline and free up adjudicative resources to reduce renewal EAD processing times. Decreasing processing times would result in a reduced need for aliens filing renewal EAD applications to rely on an automatic extension of their EAD to avoid potential lapses. Since the IFR became effective on October 30, 2025, USCIS has seen a steady decrease in overall EAD filings. See Table 1 in Section IV.C.2.i.d. of this preamble. USCIS notes that decreased EAD filing rates support the point made in the IFR: that the potential for an alien to have a lapse, and in particular the duration of any lapse, is speculative and could be outweighed by the harm posed by automatic extensions.
iii. 未能承认积压问题及其他实际运作情况 评论：多位评论者指出，临时最终规则（IFR）忽视了积压问题目前仍然存在，且未就减少需要自动延期的积压问题提供任何预测或计划。部分评论者表示，该临时最终规则错误地将积压问题描述为未来的“设想情形”，但实际上处理延误已十分严重。一位评论者表示，外部分析进一步证实，就业授权文件（EAD）裁决的长期延误是一个有记录可查、持续存在的现实情况，尽管有此记录，国土安全部（DHS）在该临时最终规则中并未提供任何实证数据，表明处理时间已有足够改善，以致无需再采用自动延期。一位评论者表示，外部分析进一步证实，就业授权文件裁决的长期延误是一个有记录可查、持续存在的现实情况，尽管有此记录，国土安全部在该临时最终规则中并未提供任何实证数据，表明处理时间已有足够改善，以致无需再采用自动延期。部分评论者援引国土安全部的积压数据，以主张自动延期机制的价值，并指出该规则未能承认积压问题。另一位评论者表示，虽然力求及时裁决是一个值得追求的目标，但这并不能消除对自动延期的需要，美国公民及移民服务局（USCIS）不应彻底取消有关自动延期的法规规定。
iii. Lack of Acknowledgement of the Backlog and Other Operational Realities Comment: Multiple commenters stated that the IFR ignores that backlogs persist today and provides no projections or plans to reduce the backlogs that necessitate automatic extension. Some of these commenters said the IFR incorrectly portrays backlogs as a future “scenario,” but processing delays are significant. A commenter stated that external analyses reinforce the conclusion that long delays in EAD adjudication are a documented, ongoing reality and, despite this record, DHS offers no empirical data showing in the IFR that processing times have improved enough to make automatic extensions unnecessary. A commenter stated that external analyses reinforce the conclusion that long delays in EAD adjudication are a documented, ongoing reality and, despite this record, DHS offers no empirical data showing in the IFR that processing times have improved enough to make automatic extensions unnecessary. Some commenters referenced DHS backlog data in asserting the value of the automatic extension mechanism and the rule’s lack of acknowledgement of the backlog. Another commenter stated that, while striving for timely adjudication is a worthy goal, it does not eliminate the need for automatic extensions, and USCIS should not eliminate the regulatory provision of automatic extensions in its entirety.
答复：国土安全部终止就业授权及就业授权文件（EAD）的自动延期，并非因为美国公民及移民服务局已完成其积压待决案件的处理，而是因为国土安全部正优先确保申请人持续符合资格并接受适当的审查核实。然而，美国公民及移民服务局将继续处理其积压案件，同时不会不必要地向可能不符合资格或对美国公共安全与国家安全构成威胁的人员授予移民福利。
Response: DHS is ending the automatic extension of employment authorization and EADs not because USCIS has completed its backlog of pending cases, but rather because DHS is prioritizing ensuring continued eligibility and proper vetting. However, USCIS will continue to work through its backlog of cases without unnecessarily granting immigration benefits to those who may be ineligible or threaten the public safety and national security of the United States.
国土安全部(DHS)在临时最终规则(IFR)中承认了就业授权文件(EAD)积压问题,指出积压的大部分原因是先前的政策决定导致初次申请和后续续签EAD申请数量激增所致。50 国土安全部在讨论每项规则中当前积压情况的同时,也探讨了未来可能出现的积压情形。具体而言,国土安全部在2024年临时最终规则(TFR)中提供了数据,以预测庇护申请激增及积压对2024年临时最终规则有效期内可能出现的续签EAD申请行为及其影响。参见89 FR 24628(2024年4月8日)。国土安全部在2024年最终规则中讨论了相同的数据。参见89 FR 101208(2024年12月13日)。这些规则试图缓解由各种情形可能导致的未来续签EAD处理积压的影响,国土安全部利用这些数据预测来估算将自动延期期限从最长180天延长至最长540天予以成文化所产生的影响。国土安全部已重新评估了该推测性做法,并确定尽管积压仍在持续,但鉴于相关的安全性与完整性风险,自动延期并非适当的机制。正如本序言其他部分所讨论的,国土安全部已采取重大措施,以减少此前几年前所未有的非法移民涌入美国后随之而来的EAD申请总量。51 自2025年以来,初次EAD申请数量的显著下降凸显了这些变化的影响,这些变化包括缩小假释(parole)的适用范围,以及使临时保护身份(TPS)及相关项目与法定标准保持一致。具体而言,美国公民及移民服务局(USCIS)还发现,在原先因提交续签EAD申请而有资格获得自动延期的最大几个EAD类别中,申请数量有所下降, 50 参见90 FR 48799, 48807(2025年10月30日)(讨论EAD申请的涌入,指出该涌入“持续拖累USCIS的处理时间和裁决资源”,并且“主要是由前任政府的政策所导致,该政策允许大量外国人以假释身份入境并申请庇护和/或临时保护身份,并同时申请就业授权”)。 51 参见90 FR 48799, 48809(2025年10月30日)。另见USCIS,“国土安全部加强国家移民系统的完整性,恢复到常识性的合法移民水平”,https://www.uscis.gov/newsroom/news-releases/dhs-strengthens-integrity-in-nations-immigration-system-returns-to-commonsense-legal-immigration(2025年11月13日);USCIS局长Joseph B. Edlow的证词,《国土安全部监督:移民及海关执法局(ICE)、海关及边境保护局(CBP)与USCIS:众议院国土安全委员会听证会》,https://docs.house.gov/meetings/HM/HM00/20260210/118916/HHRG-119-HM00-Wstate-EdlowJ-20260210.pdf,第6页(2026年2月10日)(“这些关键举措加强了我们合法移民系统的完整性,强化了问责制,并确保USCIS的资源用于合法、妥善提交的案件。”)。
DHS acknowledged the EAD backlog in the IFR, stating that most of the backlog resulted from prior policy decisions that resulted in an overwhelming number of initial and subsequent renewal EAD filings.50 DHS discussed potential future backlog scenarios while also acknowledging the current backlogs in each of those rules. Specifically, DHS provided data in the 2024 TFR to project the impact of the asylum filing surges and backlogs to consider possible renewal EAD application filing behaviors and the impact throughout the effective period of the 2024 TFR. See 89 FR 24628 (Apr. 8, 2024). DHS discussed the same data in the 2024 Final Rule. See 89 FR 101208 (Dec. 13, 2024). These rules attempted to mitigate the impact of potential future renewal EAD processing backlogs that could have been caused by a variety of circumstances, and DHS used those data projections to estimate the impact of codifying the automatic extension period increase from up to 180 days to up to 540 days. DHS has reassessed that conjectural approach and determined that, notwithstanding ongoing backlogs, automatic extensions are not an appropriate mechanism given the associated security and integrity risks. As discussed elsewhere in this preamble, DHS has taken significant steps to reduce the overall EAD filings that followed the unprecedented flood of illegal immigration into the United States during previous years.51 Significant declines in initial EAD applications since 2025 highlight the impact of these changes, which include a narrowed use of parole and a realignment of TPS and related programs with statutory criteria. Specifically, USCIS has also seen a decline in the number of filings in the largest EAD categories that were formerly eligible for automatic extensions based on the filing 50 See 90 FR 48799, 48807 (Oct. 30, 2025) (discussing an influx of EAD applications that “continues to bog down USCIS processing times and adjudicative resources,” and “was largely caused by the prior administration’s policies that allowed a significant number of aliens to enter the country on parole and seek asylum and/or TPS, and alongside such applications, employment authorization”). 51 See 90 FR 48799, 48809 (Oct. 30, 2025). See also USCIS, “DHS Strengthens Integrity in Nation’s Immigration System, Returns to Commonsense Legal Immigration Levels,” https://www.uscis.gov/newsroom/news-releases/dhs-strengthens-integrity-in-nations-immigration-system- returns-to-commonsense-legal-immigration (Nov. 13, 2025); Testimony of USCIS Director Joseph B. Edlow, Oversight of the Department of Homeland Security: ICE, CBP, and USCIS: Hearing Before the House Comm. on Homeland Security, https://docs.house.gov/meetings/HM/HM00/20260210/118916/HHRG-119-HM00-Wstate-EdlowJ- 20260210.pdf, at 6, (Feb. 10, 2026), (“These critical efforts have strengthened the integrity of our legal immigration system, reinforced accountability, and ensured that USCIS resources are directed toward lawful, properly filed cases.”).
如表1所示,续签EAD申请的提交情况。参见本序言第IV.C.2.i.d节。I-765表格申请人中最大的两个类别是庇护申请待决者(C08)和身份调整申请待决者(C09)。自2025年1月至2026年6月底,这两个类别合计约占全部I-765申请人的80%。52 表1显示了自本届政府2025年1月上任以来C08和C09申请数量的下降情况。例如,USCIS在2025年1月共收到240,060件C08申请,到2026年6月降至32,472件。53 同样,USCIS在2025年1月共收到64,104件C09申请,到2026年6月降至36,455件。

The two largest categories of Form I-765 applicants are those with pending asylum applications (C08) and those with pending adjustment of status applications (C09). Together these categories made up approximately 80% of all I-765 applicants from January 2025 through the end of June 2026.52 Table 1 shows how C08 and C09 filings have decreased since the start of the current administration in January 2025. 评论:多位评论者表示,临时最终规则(IFR)忽视了对工作许可持续高需求的现实情况,并认为国土安全部(DHS)错误地预期就业许可文件(EAD)续签申请将“大幅下降”,理由是本届政府曾试图终止古巴、海地、尼加拉瓜和委内瑞拉(CHNV)假释计划以及若干临时保护身份(TPS)指定。许多最初通过CHNV及类似假释计划入境的人员,或此前拥有临时保护身份的人员,现已申请庇护或其他保护,这些申请仍需要办理EAD续签。此外,评论者指出,终止CHNV假释计划和临时保护身份指定均已在法院受到质疑,目前尚无最终判决；如果终止决定被撤销,假释入境者和临时保护身份受益人将继续申请EAD续签。评论者得出结论认为,国土安全部必须为持续的高续签量做好规划。
Comment: Multiple commenters said the IFR ignores the reality of continued high demand for work authorization and that DHS is wrong to expect that EAD renewal filings will “substantially decline” because this Administration attempted to terminate the Cuban, Haitian, Nicaraguan, and Venezuelan (CHNV) parole program and several TPS designations. Many people who first came through CHNV and similar parole programs, or who previously had TPS, have now applied for asylum or other protections that still require EAD renewals. Additionally, the commenters said the termination of the CHNV parole program and the TPS designations have all been challenged in court with no final judgments issued; if terminations are reversed, parolees and TPS beneficiaries will continue applying for EAD renewals. The commenters concluded that DHS must plan for continued high renewal volumes.

回应:国土安全部承认,本次规则制定之外的各项政策变化,例如人道主义假释计划的重大调整,54 临时保护身份指定的终止,55 以及庇护项目所做的变更,56 已经并将继续影响未来对工作许可和/或EAD的需求。国土安全部同样认识到对工作许可和/或EAD持续存在的高需求,并不认同评论者关于IFR忽视了此类需求的说法。相反,正如IFR和本最终规则所阐明的,国土安全部认为,取消EAD自动延期在维护项目诚信、公共安全和国家安全方面所带来的益处,超过了就业授权可能出现暂时性中断的风险。
Response: DHS acknowledges that various policy changes outside of this rulemaking, such as significant changes to humanitarian parole programs,54 the termination of TPS designations,55 and changes made to the asylum program,56 have affected, and will continue to affect, the demand for employment authorization and/or EADs in the future. DHS also recognizes the continued high demand for employment authorization and/or EADs, and DHS disagrees with the commenters’ assertion that the IFR ignored such demand. Rather, as the IFR and this final rule explain, DHS believes the program integrity, public safety, and national security benefits of eliminating automatic extensions of EADs outweigh the risks of potential temporary lapses in employment authorization.

评论意见:有评论者指出,该中期最终规则(IFR)忽视了国土安全部(DHS)近期及未来计划中削减USCIS人员编制所带来的影响。具体而言,今年4月,近20,000名USCIS员工,约占员工总数的80%,收到了敦促其终止在USCIS任职的通知。该评论者表示,该规则并未说明持续进行或计划中的USCIS人员削减将如何影响用于处理续签EAD申请的裁决资源,也未说明该规则在制定削减积压工作计划时是如何考虑这些削减因素的。另一位评论者同样指出,该中期最终规则还忽视了本届政府对联邦雇员队伍的“大幅削减”以及有史以来最长的政府停摆所造成的影响。
Comment: Commenters stated that the IFR ignores the impact of DHS’s recent and planned future reductions in the USCIS workforce. Specifically, in April, nearly 20,000 USCIS staff—about 80 percent of the workforce—received communications urging them to end their employment with USCIS. The commenter said the rule does not explain how ongoing or planned USCIS staffing cuts affect the adjudicative resources available to process renewal EAD applications or how it accounted for these cuts in its planning to reduce backlogs. Another commenter similarly said the IFR also ignores the effects of this administration’s “decimation” of the federal workforce and the longest shutdown of government operations in history.
答复:该中期最终规则(IFR)及本最终规则并未低估或忽视裁决EAD申请所需的处理时间和资源的影响。如前所述,国土安全部(DHS)正优先考虑公共安全、国家安全和福利诚信。
Response: The IFR and this final rule do not undercount or ignore the impact of processing times and resources needed for adjudicating EADs. As stated previously, DHS is prioritizing public safety, national security and benefit integrity.
国土安全部(DHS)指出,USCIS一般由规费自筹资金,因此在政府停摆期间仍能继续其裁决工作。然而,国土安全部指出,政府停摆可能会影响我们与受停摆影响更为直接的联邦执法合作伙伴及其他机构协同工作的能力。
DHS notes that USCIS is generally fee-funded and able to continue its adjudicatory work during government shutdowns. However, DHS notes that government shutdowns could impact our ability to work with federal law enforcement partners and other agencies that are more directly impacted.
关于USCIS员工队伍规模的问题,国土安全部(DHS)同意,充足的人员配置和高效的资源分配对于减少积压工作、将续签EAD申请在现有卡片到期后仍处于待批状态的风险降至最低至关重要。USCIS持续在现有预算和招聘权限范围内采取运营措施,以进一步提高处理效率,包括招聘和重新分配人员、优化工作流程、在适当情况下投资自动化技术 57,并落实近期立法和行政行动所指示的收费 58 及项目变更 59。iv.“适当规划”理由 评论意见:多位评论者指出,国土安全部(DHS)关于续签申请人可通过“适当规划”避免工作授权中断的主张是错误的,忽视了实际运营状况和积压问题,并表示EAD续签申请人无法针对可能影响USCIS处理时间的每一种不可预见情况进行“适当规划”。评论者指出,2016年和2024年最终规则的出台正是为了应对该机构和申请人均无法合理预见的不可预见情况。
Regarding the size of USCIS’ workforce, DHS agrees that adequate staffing and efficient allocation of resources are important to reducing backlogs and minimizing the risk that a renewal EAD application will remain pending past the current card’s expiration. USCIS continuously takes operational steps within existing budgetary and hiring authorities to further enhance processing efficiency, including hiring and reallocating personnel, refining workflows, investing in automation where appropriate,57 and implementing the fee58 and program changes directed by recent legislation and executive actions.59 iv. “Proper Planning” Rationale Comment: Multiple commenters stated that DHS’s assertion that renewal applicants’ “proper planning” could avoid lapses in work authorization is false and ignores operational realities and backlogs, asserting that EAD renewal applicants cannot “properly plan” for every unforeseen circumstance that may affect USCIS processing times. Commenters stated that the 2016 and 2024 Final Rules were issued to address unforeseen circumstances that neither the agency nor applicants could reasonably anticipate.
评论者在陈述遵循该机构关于不要提前180天以上提出申请的建议的申请人将面临不可避免的就业中断时,援引了处理积压的数据。多名评论者表示,许多就业许可文件(EAD)申请,包括延期申请,审理时间超过180天。60 回应:如前所述,国土安全部(DHS)已确定,与通过确保在美国公民及移民服务局(USCIS)彻底审查申请人、解决任何不良记录信息并确定该外国人在适用情况下持续符合移民福利资格且值得获得有利的自由裁量处理结果之前,不批准就业授权和/或EAD,从而更好地保护美国人民安全保障的需要相比,部分外国人可能经历的就业授权和/或EAD有效期出现持续时间不确定的临时中断的潜在影响,是可以被前者的重要性所超越的。57 例如,USCIS已开展多项工作,将人工智能纳入其工作流程,以更高效地向客户提供移民服务。参见国土安全部《美国公民及移民服务局,人工智能使用案例》,https://www.dhs.gov/ai/use-case-inventory/uscis(最后更新于2026年2月12日)。58 参见国土安全部《美国公民及移民服务局收费表及对某些其他移民福利申请要求的修改》,联邦公报89 FR 6194(2024年1月31日)(“这些收费用于支付公平、高效地审理移民福利申请所需的成本,包括为难民、庇护申请人及某些其他申请人或请愿人免费提供的服务”)。USCIS主要是一个以收费为基础运作的机构。参见8 U.S.C. 1356(m)第286(m)条,其经费通常不由国会拨款。59 参见,例如,《USCIS依据H-R.1协调法案调整移民费用及相关程序》,联邦公报91 FR 22952(2026年4月29日)。
