USCIS针对部分表格和受理地点提供案件处理时间,以告知公众处理福利申请通常所需的时间。61 USCIS每月在其网站上更新案件处理时间,采用最新可得数据。处理时间随时间推移而波动,其依据是USCIS在所公布处理时间之前的六个月内完成(即签发批准或拒绝通知)80%已审理案件所需的时间。62 每个案件各不相同,部分案件所需时间可能长于其他案件。处理时间应作为参考依据,而非对案件完成所需时间的绝对衡量标准。某一特定表格类型和类别在第80百分位数上显示的处理时间略长于6个月,并不意味着某一特定申请批准或拒绝将需要超过6个月的时间。由于处理时间所反映的是一个6个月的报告周期,就近期政策变化及相关申请递交率而言,它属于滞后指标。处理时间还基于USCIS批准或拒绝申请所用的时间,而该时间可能因在批准或拒绝申请之前需要请求补充证据所耗费的时间而产生偏差。
USCIS provides case processing times for select forms and locations to inform the public how long it generally takes to process benefit requests.61 USCIS updates case processing times on its website monthly with the latest available data. The processing times fluctuate over time and are based on how long it took USCIS to complete (meaning issue an approval or denial notice) 80% of adjudicated cases over the six months prior to the posted processing times.62 Each case is unique, and some cases may take longer than others. Processing times should be used as a reference point, not an absolute measure of how a case will take to be completed. The fact that a particular form type and category shows a processing time slightly longer than 6 months at the 80th percentile does not mean that a particular application will take longer than 6 months to approve or deny. Processing times, because they represent a 6-month reporting period, are a lagging indicator as it pertains to more recent policy changes and related filing rates. Processing times are also based on the time it took USCIS to approve or deny applications, which can be skewed by the time needed to request additional evidence before an application is approved or denied.
尽管USCIS通常建议申请人不要在其EAD到期前超过180天提出续期申请,但并无规定禁止在到期前某一特定日期之前提交表格。申请人妥善规划应包括尽早提交申请,而非临近EAD到期时才提交,并确保在提交申请时已随申请一并提交USCIS确定资格所需的全部证据。尽管妥善规划在所有情况下未必能避免就业授权和/或相关文件61 参见USCIS《案件处理时间》,https://egov.uscis.gov/processing-times(最后访问于2026年5月8日)。62 参见USCIS《案件处理时间》,https://egov.uscis.gov/processing-times/more-info(最后访问于2026年4月22日)。
Although USCIS generally recommends that applicants not file requests to renew EADs more than 180 days before the expiration of their EADs, there is no prohibition on filing the form before a certain date ahead of expiration. Proper planning by applicants includes filing early, rather than close to the expiration of the EAD, and ensuring that all evidence needed for USCIS to determine eligibility has been submitted with the application at the time of filing. While proper planning might not avoid a temporary lapse 61 See USCIS, Case Processing Times, https://egov.uscis.gov/processing-times (last visited May 8, 2026). 62 See USCIS, Case Processing Times, https://egov.uscis.gov/processing-times/more-info (last visited Apr. 22, 2026).
出现临时中断,但这应有助于降低出现中断的可能性,并在中断确实发生时缩短其持续时间。
in employment authorization and/or documentation in all cases, it should help to reduce the potential of a lapse and shorten the duration if a lapse were to occur.
3. 取消EAD自动延期的理由 评论:有几位评论者反对该规则,但未提供理由。他们支持在不审查或审核外国人申请的情况下自动延长就业授权,并发放就业授权文件。一位评论者称该法规变更“错误”且“不人道”。 回应:正如本序言中其他部分对更具体评论的详细回应所述,尽管部分续签申请人可能面临就业授权和/或证件的暂时失效,国土安全部(DHS)仍认为这种做法是合理的,因为这将增强项目完整性并更好地保护公共安全和国家安全。取消基于提交EAD续签申请而自动延期的做法,确保只有在美国公民及移民服务局(USCIS)完成重新审查、评估任何新的不利信息,并确定该外国人仍符合资格且(如适用)仍值得获得自由裁量的有利处理之后,才会授予继续的就业授权。国土安全部认为,取消EAD自动延期在项目完整性、公共安全和国家安全方面带来的益处,超过了就业授权和/或EAD有效性可能出现暂时失效的风险。
3. Rationale for Eliminating the Automatic Extension of EADs Comment: A couple of commenters opposed the rule without providing their rationale. They supported automatic extension of employment authorization, and the provision of employment authorization documents, without vetting or reviewing aliens’ applications. One called the regulatory change “wrong” and “inhumane.” Response: As discussed in greater detail in response to more specific comments elsewhere in this preamble, to the extent that some renewal applicants might face a temporary lapse in employment authorization and/or documentation, DHS believes this approach is nevertheless justified as it will enhance program integrity and better protect public safety and national security. Ending automatic extensions based on the filing of an EAD renewal application ensures that continued employment authorization is conferred only after USCIS has completed renewed vetting, evaluated any new derogatory information, and determined that the alien remains eligible and continues to merit a favorable exercise of discretion, if applicable. DHS believes that the program integrity, public safety, and national security benefits of eliminating automatic extensions of EADs outweigh the risks of potential temporary lapses in employment authorization and or EAD validity.
除这些制定本规则的独立理由外,国土安全部的行动也符合总统指令。正如执行摘要中所述,第14159号行政命令指示国土安全部部长会同国务卿和司法部长,确保不向未获授权的外国人提供就业授权。63此外,取消就业 63 见行政命令,《保护美国人民免受入侵》,联邦公报第90卷第8443页,第8446页(2025年1月29日)。
In addition to these independent reasons for promulgating this rule, DHS is acting in accord with presidential directives. As discussed in the Executive Summary, E.O. 14159 instructs the Secretary of Homeland Security, alongside the Secretary of State and Attorney General, to ensure that employment authorization is not provided to unauthorized aliens.63 Furthermore, removing automatic extensions of employment 63 See Executive Order, Protecting the American People Against Invasion, 90 FR 8443, 8446 (Jan. 29, 2025).
授权和/或EAD的自动延期,也符合第14161号行政命令的要求,该命令要求国土安全部“最大限度地”对美国境内的所有外国人进行审查和甄别,64以及第10949号总统公告,该公告要求国土安全部确保已在美国境内的外国人不支持外国恐怖分子,也不对美国公民、文化、政府、制度或建国原则怀有敌对态度。65
authorization and/or EADs aligns with Executive Order 14161, which requires DHS to vet and screen all aliens within the United States “to the maximum degree possible,”64 and Presidential Proclamation 10949, which orders DHS to ensure that aliens already within the United States do not support foreign terrorists or bear hostile attitudes toward its citizens, culture, government, institutions, or founding principles.65
4. 对USCIS处理工作的影响 i. 行政负担与效率 评论:多位评论者指出,该临时最终规则(IFR)将增加政府的行政负担,并进一步拖慢裁决流程以及USCIS及时处理EAD续签的能力。一位评论者表示,取消自动延期并不能加快裁决速度,反而“制造了必然的行政失败”,并将导致紧急服务请求、查询和申诉数量的增加。同样,另一位评论者写道,该临时最终规则将造成不必要的行政低效,因为USCIS可能面临过早提交的续签申请增多、申请人因担心就业中断而多次提交申请,以及在申请待决期间雇主核查请求增多等问题。一位评论者认为,对USCIS而言,不实施该临时最终规则比实施它负担更小。
4. Impact on USCIS Processing i. Administrative Burden and Efficiency Comment: Multiple commenters asserted that the IFR would increase the administrative burden on the government and further slow the adjudication process and USCIS’ ability to timely processes EAD renewals. A commenter stated that eliminating the automatic extension does not speed up adjudications, but simply “creates a guaranteed administrative failure” and will lead to increases in emergency service requests, inquiries, and appeals. Similarly, another commenter wrote that the IFR would create unnecessary administrative inefficiencies as USCIS would likely face an increase in prematurely filed renewal applications, multiple submissions from applicants fearing gaps in employment, and additional employer verification requests during pending periods. One commenter reasoned it would be less burdensome for USCIS to not implement the IFR than to implement it.
其他评论者表示,该机构多年来一直未能及时裁决EAD续签申请,而该临时最终规则并未解决当前的实际运作状况以避免就业授权出现空档,因为即使申请人在最早可能的日期提交申请,其现有EAD往往在裁决完成之前很久就已到期,因为USCIS的处理时间有时长达8至11个月。一位评论者指出,积压及由此导致的就业授权中断并非 64 见联邦公报第90卷第8451页,第8451页(2025年1月31日)。
Other commenters stated that the Agency has been unable to timely adjudicate EAD renewals for several years and the IFR does not resolve the current operational reality to avoid gaps in employment authorization, because even when applicants file at the earliest possible date, their current EADs often expire long before adjudication concludes as USCIS processing times are sometimes 8 to 11 months. A commenter remarked that backlogs and resulting lapses in employment authorization are not the fault 64 See 90 FR 8451, 8451 (Jan. 31, 2025).

一位评论者表示,该临时最终规则未考虑国土安全部其他规则的影响,包括近期发布的“身份有效期”拟议规则,66该规则将增加USCIS工作人员的裁决工作量,并危及该机构确保延期能够在现有时限内持续得到处理的能力。
One commenter stated that the IFR does not account for the impact of other DHS rules, including the recent Duration of Status proposed rule,66 which would increase the adjudication workload on USCIS staff and jeopardize the Agency’s ability to ensure extensions can be consistently processed within existing timeframes.
答复：国土安全部（DHS）承认,积压和运营挑战确为2022年和2024年临时最终规则以及2024年最终规则的理由。然而,正如国土安全部在临时最终规则（IFR）中所解释的那样,就业授权文件（EAD）有效期的自动延期,仅通过及时提交续签EAD申请,即赋予延长外国人已过期的就业授权和（或）EAD的利益,而无需先完成裁决审查及相关审查,包括解决审查过程中发现的不利信息。也就是说,该利益在美国公民及移民服务局（USCIS）未先执行以下行为的情况下即被授予：资格认定；完成审查和筛查核验；解决与该外国人相关的潜在不利信息命中项；以及在适用情况下,作出应在自由裁量权行使中授予就业授权的认定。因此,该自动延期构成一项安全漏洞,可能使不良行为者得以继续工作并获取收入,从而为可能对美国公众构成迫在眉睫威胁的邪恶活动提供资金。在未经适当审查和充分裁决的情况下授予利益,有悖于国土安全部的使命,并对美国人民的安全与保障构成威胁。67 国土安全部认为,这一担忧超过了对可能因积压和运营挑战而导致的就业授权和（或）EAD有效期暂时中断的担忧 66 国土安全部,移民与海关执法局,《为非移民学术学生、交流访问者及外国新闻媒体代表设定固定准许入境期限及延长居留程序》,90 FR 42070（2025年8月28日提议公布）。
Response: DHS acknowledges that backlogs and operational challenges were justifications for the 2022 and 2024 Temporary Final Rules and the 2024 Final Rule. However, as DHS explained in the IFR, the automatic extension of the validity of an EAD grants the benefit of extending an alien’s expired employment authorization and/or EAD merely by filing a timely renewal EAD application and without first completing adjudicative review and related vetting, including resolution of derogatory information identified during the vetting process. That is, the benefit is granted without USCIS first performing the following actions: an eligibility determination; completing vetting and screening checks; resolving potential hits of derogatory information in connection with the alien; and a determination that the employment authorization should be granted in the exercise of discretion, if applicable. The automatic extension, therefore, poses a security vulnerability that could allow bad actors to continue to work and generate income to potentially finance nefarious activities that pose an imminent threat to the American public. Granting benefits without proper vetting and full adjudication is contrary to the mission of DHS and poses a threat to the safety and security of the American people.67 DHS believes that this concern outweighs concerns about potential backlogs and operational challenges that may result in temporary lapses of employment authorization 66 DHS, Immigration and Customs Enforcement, Establishing a Fixed Time Period of Admission and an Extension of Stay Procedure for Nonimmigrant Academic Students, Exchange Visitors, and Representatives of Foreign Information Media, 90 FR 42070 (proposed Aug. 28, 2025).
67 参见,例如,配合H.R. 4567的会议报告 [第108-774号报告], 《为截至2004年9月30日财政年度的国土安全部拨款》,第74页（2004年10月9日）,https://www.gpo.gov/fdsys/pkg/CRPT-108hrpt774/pdf/CRPT-108hrpt774.pdf（其中建议,除其他事项外,设立一个机构,在授予移民利益之前对每一名申请人、受益人及申请人进行“执法/背景核查”）。和（或）EAD有效期。
67 See, e.g., Conference Report to accompany H.R. 4567 [Report 108-774], “Making Appropriations for the Department of Homeland Security for the Fiscal Year Ending September 30, 2005,” p. 74 (Oct. 9, 2004), https://www.gpo.gov/fdsys/pkg/CRPT-108hrpt774/pdf/CRPT-108hrpt774.pdf (recommending, among other things, the creation of an organization to conduct “law enforcement/background checks on every applicant, beneficiary, and petitioner prior to granting immigration benefits.”). and/or EAD validity.
正如国土安全部在IFR中所述,本届政府的政策是“对所有不可准入及可被驱逐出境的外国人,尤其是那些对美国人民的安全或保障构成威胁的外国人,忠实执行移民法律”。68 根据该政策,国土安全部部长已与国务卿及司法部长协作,被指示“撤销前届政府导致非法外国人在美国境内人数增加或持续存在的政策决定,并使部内一切活动与本行政命令及移民法律所规定的政策保持一致”,包括“确保《移民与国籍法》（INA）第212(d)(5)条（8 U.S.C. 1182(d)(5)）项下的假释权仅依照该成文法的明文规定,逐案行使”,以及“确保暂准居留身份（TPS）的指定符合INA第244条（8 U.S.C. 1254a）的规定,并确保该等指定在范围上受到适当限制,且仅在满足该成文法文本要求所必需的期限内作出”。69 国土安全部已就前段所述指示采取了若干行动。70 因此,除前文所述有关自动EAD延期的严重关切外,国土安全部正在独立于本规则采取措施,包括限缩假释权的使用以及使TPS指定与法定标准保持一致,这些措施很可能会降低EAD申请提交量并改善续签EAD申请的处理时限。
As DHS stated in the IFR, it is the policy of the Administration “to faithfully execute the immigration laws against all inadmissible and removable aliens, particularly those aliens who threaten the safety or security of the American people.”68 Pursuant to this policy, the Secretary of Homeland Security, in collaboration with the Secretary of State and the Attorney General, has been directed to “rescind the policy decisions of the previous administration that led to the increased or continued presence of illegal aliens in the United States, and align any and all departmental activities with the policies set out by this order and the immigration laws” including by “ensuring that the parole authority under section 212(d)(5) of the INA (8 U.S.C. 1182(d)(5)) is exercised on only a case-by- case basis in accordance with the plain language of the statute” and by “ensuring that designations of TPS are consistent with the provisions of section 244 of the INA (8 U.S.C. 1254a), and that such designations are appropriately limited in scope and made for only so long as may be necessary to fulfill the textual requirements of that statute.”69 DHS has already taken a number of actions in support of the directives described in the preceding paragraph.70 Thus, in addition to the serious concerns relating to automatic EAD extensions discussed previously, DHS is taking steps independent of this rule, including narrowing the use of parole and to align TPS designations with statutory criteria, that are likely to result in lower EAD filing volumes and improve renewal EAD application processing times.

续期方面:美国国土安全部(DHS)于2026年7月17日发布了一项取消“在身份有效期内”(D/S)制度的最终规则。71 DHS同意评论者的意见,即在该最终规则生效日期2026年9月15日之后,USCIS收到的I-539表格(《延长居留期限/变更身份申请表》)的申报量将会增加,因为需要提交此类表格的非移民将开始陆续提交申请。然而,8 CFR 214.1(m)中所述的过渡期规定,为目前以D/S身份在美国境内的F类和J类非移民提供了为期4年的过渡期,以逐步转变为固定的准许停留期限。72 如最终规则所述,这些F类和J类非移民将继续以D/S身份获准停留,直至其离境,或直至指定表格上所载明的项目结束日期,或该规则生效之日起4年后,以先到者为准。73 这将使DHS有时间按需调配资源,以确保日益增加的申请量能够顺利融入现有工作流程。USCIS将继续监测其资源分配情况,并酌情进行调整。
renewals: DHS published a final rule eliminating duration of status (D/S) on July 17, 2026.71 DHS agrees with commenters that there will be an increase in the filing volume of Forms I-539, Application for Extension of Stay/Change of Status, received by USCIS following September 15, 2026, the effective date of the final rule as those nonimmigrants who are required to file such forms begin to do so. The transition period described in 8 CFR 214.1(m), however, provides for a 4-year transition period for F and J nonimmigrants currently in the United States for D/S to gradually shift to a fixed admission period.72 As explained in the final rule, these F and J nonimmigrants will continue to remain admitted for D/S until they depart, or until the program end date specified on designated forms or 4 years after the effective date of the rule, whichever comes first.73 This would allow time for DHS to shift resources as needed to ensure that the larger, increased volumes are integrated into existing workflows. USCIS will continue to monitor its resource allocations and adjust as appropriate.

二、处理时限 评论意见:一位评论者声称,提前申报通常会导致申请被拒收或驳回,并且若因提前申报而被拒收,申报费用不会退还给申请人。另一位评论者认为,由于USCIS的政策仅允许在申请提交365天后进行服务查询,这将限制雇主和雇员解决延误问题的应对方式。
ii. Processing Timelines Comment: One commenter asserted that early filing routinely leads to rejections or denials, and that filing fees are not returned to the applicant if they are rejected for early filing. Another commenter reasoned that since USCIS’ policy only allows service inquiries after 365 days, this would limit employer and employee’s options for addressing delays.
回应：尽管美国公民及移民服务局（USCIS）通常建议申请人不要在其现有就业授权文件（EAD）到期前超过180天提交续签申请，但美国公民及移民服务局不会仅因I-765表格续签申请提交过早而拒收或拒绝该申请，只要该申请在其他方面符合资格要求。美国公民及移民服务局联系中心是一个可用资源，提供具体的客户服务选项，包括在案件被拒收或拒绝时如何获得帮助。75此外，美国公民及移民服务局并未设定申请人必须等待365天才能提交案件查询的固定规则。相反，美国公民及移民服务局在其案件处理时间网页上发布处理时间，该网页包含一个工具，可帮助确定申请人是否可以请求了解其案件的最新情况。根据申请人的收件日期及其他因素，美国公民及移民服务局会提供可提交案件查询的预计日期，或提供提交案件查询的链接。76国土安全部（DHS）认识到，即使外国人因其身份而自动获得就业授权，其仍可能希望获得EAD。因身份而获得就业授权的外国人将继续可以选择提交I-765表格，以获得EAD作为身份证明和就业授权证明。国土安全部还认识到，在没有 75参见美国公民及移民服务局，联系我们（最后访问于2026年2月3日），https://www.uscis.gov/about-us/contact-us。 76参见《关于处理时间的常见问题》，美国公民及移民服务局，https://egov.uscis.gov/processing-times/processing-times-faqs（最后访问于2026年3月23日）。
Response: Although USCIS generally recommends that applicants not file to renew their EAD more than 180 days before the expiration of their current EAD, USCIS will not reject or deny an otherwise eligible Form I-765 renewal solely because it was filed sooner. The USCIS Contact Center is an available resource and provides specific customer service options, including how to obtain assistance if a case is rejected or denied.75 Further, USCIS does not have a set rule that applicants must wait 365 days to submit a service inquiry. Rather, USCIS posts processing times on its Case Processing Times webpage, which includes a tool to help determine whether an applicant can request an update on their case. Depending on the applicant’s receipt date and other factors, USCIS provides an estimated date for when a case inquiry may be submitted or a link to submit a case inquiry.76 DHS recognizes that an alien who is authorized employment incident to their status may still wish to obtain an EAD. Aliens who are authorized employment incident to status will continue to have the choice to file Form I-765 to obtain an EAD as proof of identity and employment authorization. DHS also recognizes that, in the absence of 75 See USCIS, Contact Us (last visited Feb. 3, 2026), https://www.uscis.gov/about-us/contact-us. 76 See Frequently Asked Questions About Processing Times, USCIS, https://egov.uscis.gov/processing- times/processing-times-faqs (last visited Mar. 23, 2026).
自动延期的情况下，一些外国人如果其续签申请在现有EAD到期前未获裁决，可能会经历就业授权和/或EAD有效性的暂时中断。然而，国土安全部坚持认为，在没有更新审查和裁决的情况下自动延长就业授权和/或EAD所带来的安全风险，超过了使用自动延期以确保外国人获得不间断的就业授权和/或EAD所带来的益处。虽然国土安全部不能保证每一项续签申请都能在现有EAD到期前获得裁决，但国土安全部认为，申请人适当的规划，加上申请量的减少以及持续的运营改进，可能会降低中断发生的可能性并缩短中断的持续时间。 三、程序公正性 意见：许多评论者对申请人因美国公民及移民服务局处理时间而丧失工作授权的程序公正性表示担忧。具体而言，一些评论者表示，临时最终规则（IFR）将惩罚那些遵守法律、遵循移民程序，或更广泛地说，“一切都做对了”的人，使他们“并非出于自身过错”而丧失身份。同样，几位评论者指出，现行的自动延期政策有助于防止非移民因其无法控制的延误而受到惩罚。少数评论者表示，在不解决处理延误问题的情况下取消自动延期将是不公正的。
automatic extensions, some aliens may experience temporary lapses in employment authorization and/or EAD validity if their renewal applications are not adjudicated before their current EADs expire. However, DHS maintains that the security risks associated with automatically extending employment authorization and/or EADs without updated vetting and adjudication outweigh the benefits of using automatic extensions to ensure that aliens have uninterrupted employment authorization and/or EADs. While DHS cannot guarantee that every renewal will be adjudicated before the existing EAD expires, DHS believes that proper planning by applicants, combined with reduced filing volumes and ongoing operational improvements, may lessen the likelihood and duration of lapses. iii. Procedural Fairness Comment: Many commenters expressed concerns about the procedural fairness of applicants losing work authorization due to USCIS processing times. Specifically, some commenters said that the IFR would punish people who are following the law, complying with immigration procedures, or, more generally, “have done everything right,” losing status through “no fault of their own.” Likewise, several commenters remarked that the current automatic extension policy helps prevent nonimmigrants from being punished for delays beyond their control. A few commenters said that removing automatic extensions without addressing processing delays would be unjust.
另有几位评论者指出，临时最终规则将与行政公平或公正原则相悖。其他评论者表示，该规则将与国土安全部自身承认的工作授权中断会造成“重大且不必要的损害”这一观点相矛盾。还有两位评论者表示，该规则会因个别极端案例而惩罚守法的申请人。
A few other commenters remarked that the IFR would contradict administrative equity or fairness. Other commenters said that the rule would contradict DHS’s own acknowledgement that interruptions in work authorization cause “substantial and unnecessary harm.” A couple of commenters said that the rule would penalize law abiding applicants due to an extreme individual case.
回应：国土安全部认识到，许多续签申请人勤勉地遵守提交要求，裁决延误可能并非其所能控制。国土安全部还承认，此前的规则制定，包括2016年和2024年的规则，曾试图通过提供就业授权和/或EAD的自动延期，来减轻处理延误对工人和雇主的影响。
Response: DHS recognizes that many renewal applicants diligently comply with filing requirements and that delays in adjudication may not be within their control. DHS also acknowledges that prior rulemakings, including the 2016 and 2024 rules, sought to mitigate the impact of processing delays on workers and employers by providing automatic extensions of employment authorization and/or EADs.
然而，正如临时最终规则中所解释的，国土安全部已重新评估了就业授权连续性与确保移民福利仅在经过适当审查和资格认定后方可授予这一需求之间的平衡。并不存在法定权利要求就业授权必须自动延期。相关类别中的就业授权通常是自由裁量性且有时限的，续签需要进行新的裁决。临时最终规则及本最终规则并未拒绝向符合资格的申请人续签，它们只是要求，继续的就业授权须以已获批准的续签申请为依据，而非仅以提交该申请为依据。
However, as explained in the IFR, DHS has reassessed the balance between continuity of employment authorization and the need to ensure that immigration benefits are granted only after appropriate vetting and eligibility determinations. There is no statutory entitlement to an automatic extension of employment authorization. Employment authorization in the relevant categories is generally discretionary and time-limited, and renewal requires a new adjudication. The IFR and this final rule do not deny renewal to eligible applicants; they simply require that continued employment authorization be based on an approved renewal application rather than on the mere filing of that application.
美国国土安全部（DHS）已确定，在未进行最新审查和裁决的情况下授予自动延期，不符合其使命，也不符合多项行政令的要求，这些行政令指示国土安全部确保不向未获授权的外国人提供工作授权，并确保对美国境内所有外国人进行最大程度的审查和甄别。尽管国土安全部意识到工作授权暂时中断可能带来的困难，但出于保护国家安全和公共安全、提高移民福利诚信度的需要，终止自动延期做法是正当合理的。 iv. 关于美国公民及移民服务局（USCIS）处理程序的综合或混合意见 评论意见：多位评论者就USCIS的运营指标、处理时间、证据要求通知（RFE）的使用，以及因裁决延误而失业的申请人的补救措施提出了问题。一位评论者同意540天的延期时间过长，因为在授予就业授权文件（EAD）和/或工作授权之前应进行彻底的审查。
DHS has determined that granting automatic extensions without updated vetting and adjudication is inconsistent with its mission and with E.O.s directing DHS to ensure that employment authorization is not provided to unauthorized aliens and that all aliens in the United States are vetted and screened to the maximum degree possible. While DHS is mindful of the hardships that may arise from temporary lapses in employment authorization, the need to protect national security and public safety, and to enhance the integrity of immigration benefits, justifies discontinuing automatic extensions. iv. General or Mixed Feedback on USCIS Processing Comment: Several commenters asked questions about USCIS operational metrics, processing times, use of RFEs, and remedial actions for applicants who lose their jobs due to adjudication delays. A commenter agreed that a 540-day extension was too long because there should be thorough vetting before granting EADs and/or employment authorization.
回应：国土安全部认可评论者关于本规则影响的提问。正如《临时最终规则》（IFR）及本序言前文所述，本部已采取措施解决在上届政府期间导致EAD申请数量历史性激增的相关政策问题。国土安全部预计EAD的首次申请和续签申请数量都将大幅下降，77 这将有助于缩短续签EAD的处理时间。
Response: DHS acknowledges commenters’ questions about the rule’s impact. As discussed in the IFR and earlier in this preamble, the department has taken measures to address policies that resulted in the historic spike in EAD applications received during the previous administration. DHS anticipates a substantial decrease in both initial and renewal filings for EADs,77 which would allow for a reduction in processing times for renewal EADs.
USCIS有自由裁量权，可因缺乏所需的初始证据或不符合资格而拒绝福利申请，或要求申请人在规定期限内提交补充证据。78 福利申请人承担证明其资格的举证责任。79 证据要求通知（RFE）虽然可能延长申请的裁决时间，但也为申请人提供了机会，可向USCIS提交补充证据以支持其申请。
USCIS has the discretion to deny benefit requests for lack of required initial evidence or for ineligibility, or to request that additional evidence is submitted within a specified period of time.78 The benefit requestor bears the burden of proof for establishing eligibility.79 A request for evidence (RFE), though it may add time to the adjudication of an application, is an opportunity for the applicant to provide additional evidence to USCIS in support of their application.
国土安全部将不会针对外国人工作授权中断的情况实施补救措施。国土安全部认为，申请人若能周密规划，包括尽早提交申请而非临近EAD到期时才提交，并确保在提交申请时已随申请一并提交USCIS作出资格认定所需的全部证据，有助于降低工作授权可能中断的风险。尽管周密规划未必能在所有情况下避免工作授权和/或证件出现暂时中断，但应有助于降低中断发生的可能性，并在确实发生中断时缩短其持续时间。国土安全部还指出，申请人可请求USCIS加快审理属于USCIS管辖范围内的申请。80 USCIS会根据具体情况逐案审议所有加急申请请求，通常要求提供文件以支持此类请求。81 是否加急审理完全由USCIS自行裁量决定。82 加急处理一个案件 77 见90联邦纪事48814页，脚注157。
DHS will not be implementing remedial actions in case an alien’s employment authorization lapses. DHS believes that careful planning by applicants includes filing early, rather than close to the expiration of the EAD, and ensuring that all evidence needed for USCIS to determine eligibility has been submitted with the application at the time of filing, may help to reduce the risk of a potential lapse in employment authorization. While proper planning might not avoid a temporary lapse in employment authorization and/or documentation in all cases, it should help to reduce the potential of a lapse and shorten the duration if a lapse were to occur. DHS also notes that an applicant may request that USCIS expedite the adjudication of an application that is under USCIS jurisdiction.80 USCIS considers all expedite requests on a case-by-case basis and generally requires documentation to support such requests.81 The decision to expedite is within the sole discretion of USCIS.82 Expediting a case generally means that USCIS 77 See 90 FR 48814, footnote 157.

5. 对个人、雇主、经济及特定EAD类别的综合影响 i. 对外国人、家庭和社区的影响 a. 总体困境、公平性、教育中断及家庭影响 评论意见：许多评论者担忧，《临时最终规则》将导致有效工作授权出现中断，造成失业或工作中断，同时损害外国人的财务稳定性。
5. Combined Impacts on Individuals, Employers, the Economy, and Specific EAD Categories i. Impacts on Aliens, Families, and Communities a. General Hardship, Fairness, Educational Disruptions, and Family Impacts Comment: Many commenters expressed concerns that the IFR would cause lapses in valid work authorization, causing job loss or interruptions of work, while undermining aliens’ financial stability.
评论者表示,工作授权的失效会导致失业、收入损失、住房与食物保障不稳定、医疗保障丧失、社区资源紧张、教育中断,以及对家庭和儿童造成伤害,并指出这对已经遵守所有要求的个人而言是不公平的。许多评论者对因工作授权间断而导致的工资损失后果表示了普遍关切。例如,一些评论者反映,工资损失可能导致非移民在住房和食物方面陷入不稳定状态。多位评论者写道,工作授权的中断可能使非移民面临被驱逐的威胁,另有评论者对无家可归风险的增加表示担忧。评论者指出,许多非移民可能没有家人或朋友可以依靠以获得临时住所,这使得这一群体在收入短缺方面尤为脆弱。一位评论者表示,在当前的就业市场中,如果其因工作授权间断而失业,要再找到另一份工作将极其困难。
Commenters expressed that lapses in work authorization lead to job loss, income loss, housing/food insecurity, loss of health coverage, strain on community resources, educational disruption, and harm to families and children, and that this is unfair to individuals who have complied with all requirements. Many commenters expressed general concerns about the consequences of lost wages due to gaps in employment authorization. For example, commenters shared that lost wages could lead to housing and food insecurity for nonimmigrants. Several commenters wrote that a lapse in employment could make nonimmigrants face the threat of eviction and other commenters shared concerns about increased risk of homelessness. Commenters remarked that many nonimmigrants may not have families or friends to lean on for temporary shelter, making this group particularly vulnerable to income shortages. One commenter remarked that in the current job market, it would be extremely difficult for them to find another job if they were to lose employment due to a lapse in work authorization.
83 同上。
83 See id.
多位评论者对临时最终规则(IFR)可能对家庭造成的负面影响表示关切。一些评论者表示,工作授权中断将导致家庭financial不稳定。部分评论者特别表示担忧,认为该临时最终规则将给依靠双重收入来满足抵押贷款、房租、托儿、医疗、食物、交通及其他生活开支等基本需求的家庭带来严重的经济困难。一位评论者表示,该临时最终规则可能导致家庭分离。一位评论者指出,工作授权的中断将影响非移民旅行以及探望或照顾患病家庭成员的能力。另一位评论者写道,工作授权的间断将对由女性照顾子女、老人及残疾家庭成员的家庭产生不成比例的影响。
Several commenters expressed concern about the negative impact that the IFR would have on families. Some commenters said that employment lapses would result in financial insecurity for households. Some commenters specifically expressed concern that the IFR would cause significant financial hardship for families, including those who depend on dual incomes to meet basic needs such as mortgage payments, rent, childcare, healthcare, food, transportation, and other living expenses. A commenter said that the IFR could force family separation. A commenter remarked that a lapse in employment authorization would affect nonimmigrants’ ability to travel and visit or take care of sick family members. Another commenter wrote that work authorization gaps would disproportionately affect households where women care for children, elders, and disabled family members.
多位评论者对临时最终规则对儿童的具体影响表示关切。例如,评论者反映,工作授权的中断将妨碍他们为有特殊需要的子女提供照料的能力。一位评论者指出,长期面临未满足需求的家庭所承受的压力可能影响儿童的长期健康和发展。同样,另一位评论者写道,如果家庭收入中断,儿童可能“一夜之间”失去稳定获取食物、住所、医疗保健和教育的机会。该评论者推断,因此,父母缺乏稳定工作授权的儿童更有可能遭受整体健康状况不佳的困扰,并且住院、缺课和纪律问题的发生概率也更高。评论者指出,因工资中断而导致的食物不安全将导致高血压和糖尿病等病症,并表示贫困家庭的儿童处于“低于非常良好”健康状况的可能性是富裕家庭儿童的四倍。另一位评论者同样指出,他们预计拥有移民父母的美国公民子女将受到该临时最终规则的特别影响。
Several commenters expressed concern regarding the IFR’s specific impact on children. For example, commenters shared that employment gaps would hinder their ability to provide care for their special needs children. A commenter remarked that families that continually face unmet needs endure stress that can impact the long-term health and development of children. Similarly, another commenter wrote that if household income is interrupted, children could lose their access to stable food, shelter, healthcare, and education “overnight.” The commenter reasoned that, as a result, children whose parents lack stable work authorization are more likely to suffer from poor overall health, and have a greater likelihood of hospitalization, school absenteeism, and disciplinary issues. Commenters remarked that food insecurity from disrupted wages would contribute to conditions such as hypertension and diabetes and said that children from poor families are four times as likely to be in “less than very good health” than children from wealthier families. Another commenter similarly remarked that they anticipate U.S. citizen children with an immigrant parent to be particularly impacted by the IFR.
少数评论者表示,该临时最终规则带来了人道主义方面的困境,并对外国人追求体面工作及实现“美国梦”的权利构成挑战。其他评论者表示,该临时最终规则不人道,并侵蚀了公众对移民体系的信任,还有评论者表示,这向从事工作的外国人传递出他们是“可有可无”的信息。一位评论者表示,许多就业授权文件(EAD)申请人是境外灾难的受害者,不应再面临进一步的困难。同样,多位评论者表示,外国人来到美国是为了逃离冲突或迫害,并将自动工作授权描述为“生命线”。另一位评论者将该临时最终规则取消就业授权文件有效期自动延期的做法描述为一种非法的胁迫形式,称这将使外国人在美国的生活变得无法忍受。少数评论者对该临时最终规则可能在招聘过程中造成歧视表示担忧。例如,有几位评论者担心,由于处理延误可能带来的中断,雇主将不愿雇用依赖工作授权的员工。一位评论者指出,自动延期政策有助于在对抗潜在歧视方面为求职创造更公平的环境。
A few commenters said that the IFR poses humanitarian difficulties and challenges aliens’ rights to pursue dignified work, as well as the American Dream. Other commenters said that the IFR is inhumane and erodes trust in the immigration system, while others stated it sends the message that working aliens are dispensable. One commenter stated that many EAD applicants are victims of disasters abroad and do not need to face further hardship. Similarly, multiple commenters stated that aliens come to the United States fleeing conflict or persecution and described automatic work authorization as “a lifeline.” Another commenter described the IFR’s removal of automatic extensions of EAD validity periods as an unlawful form of coercion, stating that it will make life in the United States unbearable for aliens. A few commenters expressed concerns about the IFR causing potential discrimination in the hiring process. For example, a couple of commenters expressed concern that employers would not hire employees who are dependent on work authorization, due to potential disruptions from processing delays. One commenter remarked that the automatic extension policy helps level the hiring playing field against potential discrimination.
一些评论者指出,许多非移民家庭依靠微薄的工资维持生计,可能没有足够的积蓄来弥补收入中断期间的缺口。一位评论者补充说,由于寻求庇护者没有资格获得联邦福利,这一群体将变得尤为脆弱。
A couple of commenters remarked that many nonimmigrant families live on modest wages and may not have enough saved up to supplement a gap in income. One commenter added that, since asylum seekers are not eligible for federal benefits, this group would be left particularly vulnerable.
一些评论者还表示担心,非移民在工作许可中断期间将失去失业保险和雇主提供的医疗保险等福利。一位评论者表示,许多非移民依靠就业许可文件(EAD)来证明其在医疗保险市场(Healthcare Marketplace)购买医疗保险的资格。多位评论者补充说,如果没有医疗保险,外国人可能更倾向于不治疗健康问题,也不寻求预防性医疗服务。评论者指出,这将因此增加急诊室和紧急护理的使用率,并使非移民群体面临医疗债务的风险。另有评论者指出,2025年税收和预算法,即公法119-21号(Pub. L. 119-21),又称“一个美丽大法案”(One Big Beautiful Bill Act),将进一步限制外国人的医疗保险,并对由此产生的健康状况表示担忧,援引了多篇文章,证明一个人负担医疗保险的能力与其健康状况之间存在相关性。一位评论者还表示,某些医疗保险计划要求未成年子女持有未过期的EAD才能获得保障。
Some commenters also expressed concerns that nonimmigrants would lose benefits such as unemployment insurance and employer sponsored health coverage during employment authorization gaps. One commenter said that many nonimmigrants rely on EADs to demonstrate their eligibility to purchase healthcare on the Healthcare Marketplace. Multiple commenters added that, without health care coverage, aliens may be more likely to avoid treating health conditions and seeking preventive services. Commenters remarked that this would consequently increase use of emergency rooms and urgent care and expose nonimmigrant populations to medical debt. Additional commenters remarked that the 2025 tax and budget law, Pub. L. 119-21, also known as the “One Big Beautiful Bill Act,” will further limit health coverage for aliens, and expressed concern for resulting health outcomes, citing several articles which demonstrated correlation between one’s ability to afford health insurance and their health outcomes. One commenter also remarked that some medical insurance plans require minor children to have an unexpired EAD for coverage.
许多评论者对该临时最终规则(IFR)可能对公共资源造成的影响表示担忧。评论者认为,收入损失将不得不通过增加对公共援助项目、慈善机构以及其他社区或人道主义服务的依赖来弥补。例如,评论者表示,该临时最终规则将增加对食物救济站、紧急避难所、服装或捐赠中心以及社区卫生中心等服务的需求。少数评论者指出,这些资源本已捉襟见肘,尤其是在吸收了联邦援助项目缩减所带来的溢出影响之后。一位评论者指出,缅因州的一些城市已经超出其一般援助资金的额度,无法承受资源方面的进一步压力。该评论者补充说,外国工人在社会服务机构中担任着至关重要的角色,从事翻译、口译、个案管理和文化联络等工作。该评论者认为,失去这些工作人员将加剧这些机构面临的压力,并预测如果外国工人面临EAD有效期中断,他们随后可能会转而向社会服务机构寻求帮助。该评论者补充说,食品银行等地方支持服务在过去一年中需求已经有所上升。
Many commenters expressed concerns about the impact that the IFR would have on public resources. Commenters reasoned that loss of income would have to be supplemented by increased reliance on public assistance programs, charity, and other community or humanitarian services. For example, commenters said that the IFR would increase demand for services like food pantries, emergency shelters, clothing or donation centers, and community health centers. A few commenters remarked that these resources are already strained, particularly after having absorbed the spillover from shrinking federal assistance programs. One commenter remarked that cities in Maine are already exceeding their general assistance funds and cannot afford additional strain on resources. The commenter added that alien workers fill vital roles at social service agencies, working as translators, interpreters, case managers, and cultural brokers. The commenter reasoned that loss of these workers would exacerbate the strain on these agencies, predicting that if the alien workers face a lapse in EAD validity, they may then turn to the social service agency for help. The commenter added that local support services such as food banks have already seen an uptick in demand over the past year.
多位评论者表示担心,非营利法律服务机构将面临服务需求的压倒性增长。同样,一些评论者表示,危机驱动的请求需要及时回应,无法在不牺牲关键法律服务的情况下并入现有案件负荷,而承担更多此类案件将给其机构带来压力,并分散对人道主义案件准备工作的关注。
Several commenters expressed concern that nonprofit legal providers would see an overwhelming increase in demand for services. Similarly, some commenters said that crisis-driven requests require timely responses and cannot be integrated into existing caseloads without sacrificing key legal services, and that shouldering more of these cases would cause stress to their organization and divert attention away from humanitarian case preparation.
回应:国土安全部(DHS)认识到,工作许可的中断可能给受影响的外国人、其家庭、其雇主以及他们所居住的社区带来实际困难。评论者描述了诸如失业、收入减少、住房和食物无保障、医疗保险丧失、社区资源紧张、教育中断以及对儿童和家庭稳定性的负面影响等风险,尤其是对于依靠双收入或微薄工资维持生计的家庭而言。国土安全部也注意到外国人工作许可暂时中断可能带来的其他干扰,包括对于那些可能依赖自动延期来获取符合REAL ID标准的驾驶证和其他形式身份证明文件的人员的影响。
Response: DHS recognizes that lapses in employment authorization can create real hardship for affected aliens, their families, their employers and the communities in which they live. Commenters described risks such as job loss, reduced income, housing and food insecurity, loss of health coverage, strain on community resources, educational disruption, and negative impacts on children and family stability, particularly for households relying on dual incomes or modest wages. DHS is also mindful of other disruptions that a temporary lapse in employment authorization of an alien may bring, including for those who may rely on an automatic extension to obtain REAL ID- compliant driver’s licenses and other forms of identification.
国土安全部(DHS)在发布临时最终规则(IFR)84以及在最终确定本规则时均已考虑了这些问题。然而,国土安全部认为,在准予恢复就业授权并签发该授权的安全证明文件之前,确保进行适当的审查和筛查以及持续符合资格的必要性,超过了部分申请人可能经历就业授权和/或就业授权文件(EAD)有效期暂时中断的风险。国土安全部还认定,在未经更新的裁决和筛查的情况下延长此项福利,与其使命以及行政令85不符,该行政令指示国土安全部确保不向无授权外国人提供就业授权,并确保在美外国人受到最大程度的审查和筛查。
DHS considered these concerns before issuing the IFR84 and again in finalizing this rule. However, DHS has concluded that the need to ensure proper vetting and screening, and continued eligibility, before granting renewed employment authorization and issuing secure evidence of that authorization outweighs the risk that some applicants might experience a temporary lapse in employment authorization and/or EAD validity. DHS also determined that extending this benefit without updated adjudication and screening is inconsistent with its mission and with E.O.s85 directing DHS to ensure that employment authorization is not provided to unauthorized aliens and that aliens in the United States are vetted and screened to the maximum degree possible.
尽管如此,国土安全部强调,本规则并未取消就业授权的资格,也不会阻止符合资格的外国人获得初次或续期EAD。相反,本规则终止的做法是:仅凭提交续期申请,在未完成裁决审查及相关筛查(包括解决任何不利信息)的情况下,自动将就业授权和/或EAD有效期延长最长540天。84 见《联邦公报》第90卷第48799页,第48809页(2025年10月30日)。
Nevertheless, DHS emphasizes that this rule does not eliminate eligibility for employment authorization or prevent eligible aliens from obtaining initial or renewal EADs. Rather, it ends the practice of automatically extending employment authorization 84 See 90 FR 48799, 48809 (Oct. 30, 2025).
85 见第14159号行政令《保护美国人民免受入侵》(2025年1月20日),《联邦公报》第90卷第8443页,第8446页(2025年1月29日);以及第14161号行政令《保护美国免受外国恐怖分子及其他国家安全和公共安全威胁》(2025年1月20日),《联邦公报》第90卷第8451页,第8451页(2025年1月30日)。持续的就业授权现在将取决于美国公民及移民服务局(USCIS)对续期申请的批准,该批准须在USCIS认定该外国人继续符合资格,且(如适用)继续值得获得有利的自由裁量处理之后作出。
85 See E.O. 14159, Protecting the American People Against Invasion (Jan. 20, 2025), 90 FR 8443, 8446 (Jan. 29, 2025) and E.O. 14161, Protecting the United States From Foreign Terrorists and Other National Security and Public Safety Threats (Jan. 20, 2025), 90 FR 8451, 8451 (Jan. 30, 2025). and/or EAD validity for up to 540 days based solely on the filing of a renewal application, without completion of adjudicative review and related vetting, including resolution of any derogatory information. Continued employment authorization will now depend on USCIS’ approval of a renewal application, after USCIS has determined that the alien continues to be eligible and continues to merit a favorable exercise of discretion, if applicable.
本规则也不改变移民福利、公共福利或教育项目的基础资格标准,也不限制外国人寻求其根据联邦或州法律原本有资格获得的援助。某些类别的外国人,例如难民和被批准庇护者,根据现行法规仍是特定公共福利方面的“合格外国人”,这些授权规定不因本规则而改变。同样,本规则也不改变各州、地方政府或私人组织提供慈善或人道主义援助的能力。
This rule also does not change underlying eligibility standards for immigration benefits, public benefits, or educational programs, and it does not restrict aliens from seeking assistance for which they are otherwise eligible under Federal or State law. Certain categories of aliens, such as refugees and asylees, remain “qualified aliens” for specified public benefits under existing statutes, and those authorities are unchanged by this rule. Likewise, this rule does not alter the ability of States, localities, or private organizations to provide charitable or humanitarian assistance.
国土安全部还指出,独立于本规则之外,其已采取措施减少因此前政策导致的异常庞大的EAD申请量,包括缩小假释(parole)的适用范围,以及使临时保护身份(TPS)的指定与法定标准相一致。国土安全部预计,由于这些举措,整体EAD申请率(初次申请和续期申请)将大幅下降,从而释放裁决资源,以缩短续期EAD申请的处理时间。例如,自2025年初以来,此前有资格获得自动延期的最大EAD类别的初次申请和续期申请已显著减少。见本序言第IV.C.2.i.d节中的表1。
DHS also notes that, independent of this rule, it has taken steps to reduce the extraordinary EAD filing volumes driven by prior policies, including narrowing the use of parole and aligning TPS designations with statutory criteria. DHS expects that overall EAD filing rates (initial and renewal) will substantially decline because of those actions, freeing adjudicative resources to reduce renewal EAD application processing times. For example, initial and renewal EAD applications for the largest EAD categories that were formerly eligible for automatic extensions have significantly reduced since the start of 2025. See Table 1 in Section IV.C.2.i.d. of this preamble.
因此,EAD申请率的下降支持了IFR中提出的观点,即有关就业授权和/或EAD中断的数量和持续时间将增加的主张均属推测性质,且其弊端被自动延期所带来的危害所超过。
Accordingly, the decreased EAD filing rates support the point made in the IFR that any claims about an increase in the number and duration of employment authorization and/or EAD lapses are speculative and outweighed by the harm posed by automatic extensions.
USCIS还在其网站上公布了当前的案件处理时间信息。86 申请人进行适当规划,例如监控EAD到期日期、查阅公布的处理时间,并在法规和表格说明允许的最早时间提交续期申请,可以进一步减轻就业授权中断的风险和持续时间。
USCIS also publishes current case processing time information on its website.86 Proper planning by applicants, such as monitoring EAD expiration dates, consulting posted processing times, and filing renewal applications as soon as permitted by regulation and form instructions, can further mitigate the risk and duration of any lapses in employment authorization.
总体而言,国土安全部认识到评论者所描述的困境的严重性,但认为,综合权衡之下,终止自动EAD延期所维护的国家安全、公共安全和福利诚信利益,超过了就业授权和/或EAD有效期暂时中断所带来的潜在不利影响。
Overall, DHS recognizes the seriousness of the hardships described by commenters but concludes that, on balance, the national security, public safety, and benefit integrity interests served by ending automatic EAD extensions outweigh the potential adverse impacts associated with temporary lapses in employment authorization and/or EAD validity.
b. 心理健康与脆弱性,包括被剥削的风险 评论意见:评论者断言,由于缺乏自动延期以及处理延误所导致的就业空档期,将加剧压力、创伤和焦虑,并可能促使部分外国人从事无授权或被剥削性质的工作,或陷入不安全的居住条件。
b. Mental Health and Vulnerability, Including Risk of Exploitation Comment: Commenters asserted that employment gaps due to the lack of automatic extensions and processing delays will exacerbate stress, trauma, and anxiety, and may push some aliens into unauthorized or exploitative employment or unsafe living conditions.
多名评论者表示,该临时最终规则将引发恐惧、压力、抑郁以及其他心理健康方面的担忧。一位评论者断言,部分移民群体已经因移民执法活动而承受着严重的创伤和压力,而该临时最终规则只会加剧此类情绪。
Multiple commenters remarked that the IFR would instill fear, stress, depression, and anxiety, among other mental health concerns. One commenter asserted that some of the immigrant population is already experiencing significant levels of trauma and stress from immigration enforcement activity, and that the IFR would only exacerbate such feelings.
许多评论者对非移民因该临时最终规则取消自动延期而面临雇主和房东加剧的剥削风险,以及人口贩运、性侵害和虐待风险表示担忧。例如,多名评论者表示,丧失工作许可会使86 参见美国公民及移民服务局(USCIS),案件处理时间,https://egov.uscis.gov/processing-times/more-info(最后访问于2026年4月22日)。
Many commenters shared concerns about nonimmigrants’ increased level of vulnerability to exploitation by employers and landlords, as well as human trafficking, sexual harm, and abuse owing to the IFR’s elimination of automatic extensions. For example, multiple commenters said that loss of employment authorization makes 86 See USCIS, Case Processing Times, https://egov.uscis.gov/processing-times/more-info (last visited Apr. 22, 2026).
非移民容易遭受剥削性的工作条件。一些评论者表示,如果外国人失去合法工作的能力,他们可能会寻求地下工作或从事非法雇佣活动。评论者警告称,这使此类人员容易遭受剥削,并解释说,这些工作通常工资较低、劳动保护极少、工作条件恶劣,而由于身份非法,他们可能不敢举报。他们还指出,84.9%的无证移民报告称未获得一倍半的加班工资,37.1%报告称曾遭受最低工资违规行为的侵害。该评论者补充说,许多雇佣无证工人的雇主没有购买工伤赔偿保险,这意味着雇员在因工受伤时可能需要自行承担医疗费用。该评论者还补充说,由于USCIS最近终止了一项保护举报人的计划,无证雇员现在更不太可能举报各类虐待行为。另一位评论者补充说,迫使申请人从事非法雇佣还可能影响其未来获得移民福利的资格,使其长期处于弱势地位。
nonimmigrants vulnerable to exploitative working conditions. Some commenters remarked that if aliens lose their ability to work legally, they may seek under the table work or unlawful employment. Commenters warned that this leaves such individuals vulnerable to exploitation, explaining that these jobs often have lower wages, minimal labor protections, and abusive conditions which they may be afraid to report due to unlawful status. They also indicated that 84.9 percent of unauthorized immigrants reported not being paid time and a half for overtime labor, and 37.1 percent reported being victims of minimum wage violations. The commenter added that many employers of unauthorized workers do not carry workers compensation insurance, meaning that employees may be responsible for covering their own medical costs in the case of work- related injury. The commenter added that since USCIS recently ended a program that protected whistleblowers, unauthorized employees are now less likely to report various types of abuse. A different commenter added that forcing applicants into unlawful employment may also impact their eligibility for future immigration benefits, putting them in a long-term position of vulnerability.
答复:国土安全部(DHS)认识到,工作许可的不确定性以及潜在的收入中断可能导致压力、焦虑及其他心理健康问题,对于那些可能已经经历过创伤或不稳定生活的人而言尤其如此。DHS还认识到,经济不稳定和缺乏合法就业机会可能增加遭受不良雇主、房东或其他人剥削的风险,并可能加剧人口贩运、虐待或其他伤害的风险。该临时最终规则及本最终规则并未剥夺符合条件的外国人获得工作许可或其所符合条件的相关移民福利的机会。相反,经本最终规则确认的该临时最终规则仅取消了此前允许外国人仅凭待审的续签申请即可在长达540天内持续保有工作许可,而无需完成更新的审查和裁决的自动延期机制。正如本序言通篇所述,DHS已认定,此类自动延期在USCIS完成审查和甄别、解决任何不利信息、确定申请人是否继续符合资格,以及(如适用)确定申请人是否应获得有利的自由裁量处理之前即授予重大利益,构成不可接受的安全和项目完整性风险。
Response: DHS recognizes that uncertainty about employment authorization and potential lapses in income can contribute to stress, anxiety, and other mental health challenges, particularly for individuals who may already have experienced trauma or instability. DHS also recognizes that economic insecurity and lack of lawful employment opportunities can increase vulnerability to exploitation by unscrupulous employers, landlords, or others, and may heighten risks of trafficking, abuse, or other harm. The IFR and this final rule do not deny eligible aliens access to employment authorization or to the underlying immigration benefits for which they qualify. Rather the IFR, affirmed by this final rule, removes only the automatic extension mechanism that previously allowed aliens to remain employment authorized for up to 540 days based solely on a pending renewal application, without completion of updated vetting and adjudication. As described throughout this preamble, DHS has determined that such automatic extensions present an unacceptable security and program integrity risk because they confer significant benefits before USCIS has completed vetting and screening, resolved any derogatory information, determined continued eligibility and that the applicant merits a favorable exercise of discretion, if applicable.
DHS不能在其监管框架中假定外国人和/或雇主会以从事非法雇佣或其他违法行为来应对工作许可的暂时中断。《移民与国籍法》(INA)禁止明知而雇佣无工作许可的外国人,并对违反上述规定的雇主和外国人规定了相应后果。尽管DHS认识到,一旦出现中断,部分外国人可能面临艰难抉择,但DHS并不纵容非法雇佣行为,也不认为这是应对工作许可暂时中断的恰当方式。
DHS cannot structure its regulatory framework on the assumption that aliens and/or employers will respond to temporary lapses in employment authorization by engaging in unauthorized employment or other unlawful conduct. The INA prohibits the knowing employment of unauthorized aliens and establishes consequences for both employers and aliens who violate those provisions. While DHS recognizes that some aliens may face difficult choices if a lapse occurs, DHS does not condone unauthorized employment or believe that it is an appropriate response to a temporary lapse in employment authorization.
美国国土安全部(DHS)与评论者一样,对剥削和虐待问题表示关切。国土安全部,包括通过美国移民及海关执法局(ICE)和美国公民及移民服务局(USCIS)的反欺诈和受害者保护项目等部门,将继续执行与人口贩运、强迫劳动及其他形式剥削相关的法律,并与劳工部及其他机构协调执法工作。针对人口贩运、犯罪和虐待受害者的现有保护措施(包括T类和U类非移民身份以及《针对妇女暴力行为法》(VAWA))不受本规则影响。本规则中的任何内容均不会削弱国土安全部识别和协助受害者,以及支持对贩运者和其他违法者进行调查和起诉的承诺。国土安全部认识到,如果一些外国人的工作许可暂时失效,他们可能会经历更大的压力或处于更脆弱的境地。然而,国土安全部认为,根据当前的国家安全和公共安全优先事项,确保工作许可仅在经过适当审查和裁决后予以延期的必要性,足以证明终止自动延期的合理性。国土安全部还预计,整体工作许可文件(EAD)申请量的减少可能有助于限制失效情况发生的频率和持续时间,并且申请人及时提交申请和提前规划可以进一步减少不确定性。关于申请量减少的情况,请参见本序言第IV.C.2.i.d节中的表1。
DHS shares commenters’ concerns about exploitation and abuse. DHS, including through components such as ICE and USCIS’ fraud detection and victim protection programs, will continue to enforce laws related to human trafficking, forced labor, and other forms of exploitation, and to coordinate with the Department of Labor and other agencies on enforcement. Existing protections for victims of trafficking, crime, and abuse (including T and U nonimmigrant classifications and VAWA) are unaffected by this rule. Nothing in this rule diminishes DHS’s commitment to identify and assist victims or to support the investigation and prosecution of traffickers and other offenders. DHS recognizes that some aliens may experience heightened stress or vulnerability if their employment authorization temporarily lapses. However, DHS has concluded that the need to ensure that employment authorization is extended only after appropriate vetting and adjudication, consistent with current national security and public safety priorities, justifies ending automatic extensions. DHS also expects that reductions in overall EAD filing volumes may help limit the frequency and length of lapses and that timely filing and planning by applicants can further reduce uncertainty. For reduced filing volumes, see Table 1 in Section IV.C.2.i.d. of this preamble.
c. 身份证明、驾驶执照及服务获取 评论意见:多位评论者表示担忧,工作许可失效以及由此导致的相关工作许可文件使用问题,将妨碍非移民获得或更新驾驶执照、开立和使用银行账户、获得或更新健康保险、注册教育项目和培训,以及与执法部门打交道的能力。评论者指出,缺乏驾驶执照可能干扰非移民的关键日常活动,例如送孩子上学、通勤上班,或前往就医或出席移民听证会。少数评论者表示,在许多情况下,非移民需要驾驶执照才能履行其工作职责,如果没有有效的驾驶执照,非移民可能被迫无证驾驶,从而危及道路安全。
c. Identification, Driver’s Licenses, and Access to Services Comment: Several commenters expressed concerns that lapses in employment authorization and the associated use of employment authorization documents would hinder nonimmigrants’ abilities to obtain or renew their driver’s licenses, open and access bank accounts, obtain or renew health insurance, enroll in educational programs and training, and interact with law enforcement. Commenters remarked that lack of a driver’s license could interfere with critical daily activities for nonimmigrants, such as taking children to school, commuting to work, or attending doctors’ appointments or immigration hearings. A few commenters shared that in many cases, nonimmigrants require driver’s licenses to perform their job duties and that, without a valid driver’s license, nonimmigrants may be pushed to drive without a license, compromising road safety.
回应:国土安全部承认,外国人通常将工作许可文件用作身份证明文件,在某些情况下还用作州颁发驾驶执照和身份证件、金融服务及其他用途所需的身份状态文件。根据此前的自动延期框架,表面上已过期的工作许可文件,若附有I-797C表格收据通知,可在某些用途中被视为未过期,包括用于获取驾驶执照。正如临时最终规则(IFR)中所述,国土安全部已确定,继续这种做法以及继续使用自动延期的工作许可文件不再适当。
Response: DHS acknowledges that EADs are commonly used by aliens as identity and, in some contexts, status documents for State-issued driver’s licenses and identification cards, financial services, and other purposes. Under the prior automatic extension framework, an EAD that was facially expired could be treated as unexpired for certain purposes if accompanied by a Form I-797C receipt notice, including obtaining a driver’s license. As explained in the IFR, DHS determined that continuing that practice and the use of automatically extended EADs is no longer appropriate.
本规则不改变各州决定哪些文件可接受用于签发驾驶执照或其他州身份证件的权力,也不修订本部关于签发《真实身份法》(REAL ID Act)所界定的、可用于联邦官方用途的驾驶执照或身份证件的《真实身份》相关法规。但本规则明确规定,对于在2025年10月30日或之后提交的续签申请,待决的I-765表格及相关收据通知将不再根据国土安全部法规自动延长工作许可文件的有效期。表面上已过期的工作许可文件,除非符合根据2025年10月30日之前提交的续签申请而依据8 CFR 274a.13(d)获得自动延期的条件,或依法或根据适用的《联邦公报》通知(例如某些与临时保护身份(TPS)相关的文件)另行延期,否则将不被视为出于就业或移民目的而未过期。
This rule does not change State authority to determine which documents are acceptable for issuance of driver’s licenses or other State identification, nor does it amend the Department’s REAL ID regulations for issuance of driver’s licenses or identification cards acceptable for federal official purposes defined in the REAL ID Act. It does, however, make clear that, for renewal applications filed on or after October 30, 2025, a pending Form I-765 and associated receipt notice will no longer operate to automatically extend EAD validity under DHS regulations. An EAD that is facially expired will not be treated as unexpired for employment or immigration purposes unless it qualifies for an automatic extension under 8 CFR 274a.13(d) based on a renewal application filed before October 30, 2025, or is otherwise extended by law or an applicable Federal Register notice (for example, certain TPS-related documents).
本规则不妨碍符合条件的外国人申请或续签工作许可文件,也不禁止各州或其他实体在法律允许的情况下接受其他形式的身份证明或文件。
The rule does not prevent eligible aliens from seeking or renewing EADs, nor does it prohibit States or other entities from accepting alternative forms of identification or documentation where permitted by law.
国土安全部还预计,随着工作许可文件整体申请量的下降以及续签处理时间的改善,失效发生的风险和持续时间将有所减少。美国公民及移民服务局已经观察到,曾符合自动延期资格的主要工作许可文件类别的申请率出现下降。请参见本序言第IV.C.2.i.d节中的表1。
DHS also expects that, as overall EAD filing volumes decline and renewal processing times improve, the risk and duration of lapses will be reduced. USCIS has already seen a decline in filing rates for the main EAD categories that were eligible for automatic extensions. See Table 1 in Section IV.C.2.i.d. of this preamble.
美国国土安全部(DHS)认为,确保作为身份和工作授权证明的EAD能够反映最近一次裁决和已完成的审查所带来的安全性和福利完整性方面的收益,超过了取消自动延期可能带来的风险。
DHS concludes that the security and benefit integrity gains from ensuring that EADs used as evidence of identity and work authorization reflect a recent adjudication and completed vetting outweigh the potential risks associated with discontinuing automatic extensions.
二、就业、雇主与经济影响 a. 雇主负担、业务连续性及特定行业问题 评论:多位评论者认为,取消自动延期将增加I-9表格合规的复杂性,导致人员流动和生产力损失,加剧医疗保健、教育、农业、制造业等领域现有的用工短缺,并对小型雇主造成特别沉重的负担。
ii. Employment, Employers, and Economic Impacts a. Employer Burdens, Business Continuity, and Sector Specific Concerns Comment: Multiple commenters argued that loss of automatic extensions will increase Form I-9 compliance complexity, cause turnover and productivity losses, exacerbate existing shortages in health care, education, agriculture, manufacturing, etc., and impose particular burdens on small employers.
评论者表示反对取消自动延期,称此项变更将对雇主造成重大干扰,包括运营效率低下(生产力损失和延误)、行政负担、人员配置缺口、人力资本成本与人员留任问题、民事处罚,以及在员工等待续签决定期间因缺乏工作授权而产生的合规负担。少数评论者表示,由于授权状态不明确,雇主可能违反I-9表格合规规则。多位评论者断言,该临时最终规则(IFR)将加剧现有的劳动力短缺,并预计劳动力参与率至少到2030年将持续下降,警告称该临时最终规则可能因阻止寻求庇护者在EAD审理期间工作以及取消已通过审查的工作人员的资格,而使此类短缺进一步恶化。一位评论者表示,劳动力短缺将对美国经济产生负面影响,阻碍经济增长并加剧通货膨胀压力。另一位评论者表示,2019年至2021年间移民劳动力减少了200万,导致各行业出现劳动力短缺,其中建筑业的劳动力短缺价值达108亿美元,并据此推断该临时最终规则将在每个行业重演此类劳动力短缺。一位评论者表示,在公共和私营部门的努力能够充分满足行业需求之前,美国将需要额外的劳动力来填补国内劳动力留下的空缺职位,并担忧取消EAD延期可能进一步扰乱制造商的劳动力稳定性和业务连续性。许多评论者表示,取消自动延期将对依赖外国劳动力的美国经济关键行业和部门产生负面影响,包括农业、科技、能源、制造业、物流、运输、酒店业、教育和建筑业。多位评论者对医疗保健及医疗保健工作者所受影响表示担忧,指出即使工作授权出现短暂中断,也会扰乱患者护理、诊所运营和社区医疗服务。一位评论者谈到该临时最终规则将如何对雇用EAD工作者的教学医院和学术医疗系统产生不利影响。
Commenters expressed opposition to removing automatic extensions, stating that such a change would cause significant disruption to employers, including operational inefficiencies (loss of productivity and delays), administrative burdens, staffing gaps, human capital costs and retention, civil penalties, and compliance burdens as employees await renewal decisions without work authorization. A few commenters stated that employers could violate Form I-9 compliance rules due to unclear authorization statuses. Multiple commenters asserted that the IFR would exacerbate existing labor shortages and projected a continued decrease in labor force participation rates through at least 2030, cautioning that the IFR could worsen such shortages by preventing asylum seekers from working during EAD processing and removing vetted workers. One commenter stated that labor shortages would negatively impact the U.S. economy, hinder growth and contribute to inflationary pressures. Another commenter stated that a decline of 2 million immigrant workers between 2019 and 2021 contributed to labor shortages across sectors, including a labor shortage in the construction industry valued at $10.8 billion, reasoning that the IFR would replicate such labor shortages across every industry. A commenter stated that until public and private sector efforts could fully meet industry demand, the United States would need additional workers to fill jobs left vacant by the domestic workforce, expressing concern that ending the EAD extension could further disrupt workforce stability and business continuity for manufacturers. Many commenters stated that the removal of automatic extensions would negatively impact critical industries and sectors of the U.S. economy that rely on foreign workers, including agriculture, technology, energy, manufacturing, logistics, transportation, hospitality, education, and construction. Several commenters expressed concern about the impact on healthcare and healthcare workers, noting that even a brief lapse in employment authorization disrupts patient care, clinic operations, and community health access. One commenter remarked about how the IFR will adversely affect teaching hospitals and academic health systems that employ EAD workers.
其他评论者担忧,该临时最终规则将通过扰乱包含持有EAD的移民工作者的谈判单位,削弱工会、集体谈判及劳动力稳定性,并指出工人突然失业风险的增加将使工会在执行工作场所标准方面的能力承受压力,迫使其投入更多资源来保护弱势工作者并应对该临时最终规则所造成的不稳定局面。
Other commenters expressed concerns that the IFR would undermine labor unions, collective bargaining, and workforce stability by disrupting bargaining units that include immigrant workers with EADs, stating that increased risk of sudden job loss among workers would strain unions’ ability to enforce workplace standards, forcing them to devote more resources to protecting vulnerable workers and addressing instability caused by the IFR.
多位评论者表示,在政府处理持续延误的背景下,自动延期对业务连续性至关重要,并称该临时最终规则将施加不必要的负担和成本,尤其是对没有内部法律部门的小型公司而言。一些评论者表示,该规则将迫使雇主进行大量应急规划和劳动力审查,以应对工作授权可能出现的中断。具体而言,一位评论者表示,诸如获庇护者等因身份而自动获得工作授权的群体,仍可能被要求向雇主出示EAD,并进一步推断该临时最终规则仍可能在这些群体中造成雇主困惑和失业问题。其他评论者表示,取消EAD自动延期将导致企业流失工作人员,并援引美国公民及移民服务局(USCIS)的一项分析,该分析确定此前的EAD延期通过避免劳动力流失为美国雇主节省了约35亿美元。
Multiple commenters expressed the importance of automatic extensions for business continuity amid persistent government processing delays and stated that the IFR would impose unnecessary burdens and costs, especially on smaller companies without in-house legal departments. Some commenters stated that the rule would force employers to conduct extensive contingency planning and workforce audits to prepare for potential lapses in work authorization. Specifically, a commenter stated that groups such as asylees, who are authorized to work incident to status, may still be asked to show an EAD to their employer, and further reasoned that the IFR could still cause employer confusion and job loss among these groups. Other commenters stated that eliminating automatic EAD extensions would cause businesses to lose workers and cited to a USCIS analysis establishing that EAD extensions previously saved U.S. employers an estimated $3.5 billion by avoiding labor turnover.
许多评论者对雇主可能因行政处理延迟导致工作许可文件(EAD)续签受阻而被迫解雇员工表示担忧。评论者表示,此前工作许可规则的突然反转已给雇主造成困惑,并导致他们因对不断变化的联邦法规产生误解而做出草率决定,从而导致错误解雇,即持有有效工作许可文件的员工仅因雇主不了解如何遵守现行规则而被解雇。一位评论者表示,此类影响将加剧当前持续存在的教师短缺危机。
Numerous commenters expressed concern that employers would be forced to terminate employees due to administrative delays in processing EAD renewals. Commenters stated that previous sudden reversals of work permit rules have created confusion for employers and caused them to make rash decisions based on misinformation about changing Federal regulations, leading to erroneous layoffs where workers with valid EADs were dismissed because employers did not understand how to comply with current rules. A commenter said that such impacts would exacerbate the ongoing teacher shortage crisis.
回应:国土安全部(DHS)认识到,包括医疗保健、教育、制造业、农业、酒店业和科技业在内的某些行业,以及已工会化的工作场所,可能对劳动力连续性的中断特别敏感。
Response: DHS recognizes that some sectors, including healthcare, education, manufacturing, agriculture, hospitality, and technology, and unionized workplaces may be particularly sensitive to interruptions in workforce continuity.
本规则不改变雇主根据《移民与国籍法》(INA)第274A条、《美国法典》第8编第1324a条以及《联邦法规汇编》第8编第274a部分所承担的基本义务,即仅雇佣获授权在美国工作的个人、核实就业资格,以及在现有文件到期时重新核实就业授权。
This rule does not change employers’ underlying obligations under section 274A of the INA, 8 U.S.C. 1324a, and 8 CFR part 274a to hire only individuals who are authorized to work in the United States, to verify employment eligibility, and to reverify employment authorization when existing documentation expires.
通过终止对2025年10月30日或之后提交的续签申请的自动延期,本规则确保就业授权将以经过裁定后签发的安全、未过期文件作为证明。这降低了雇主依赖过时或无效文件的风险,并符合国土安全部确保就业授权和/或工作许可文件仅在美国公民及移民服务局(USCIS)完成适当审查并确定其持续符合资格后方可发放的职责。
By ending automatic extensions for renewal applications filed on or after October 30, 2025, this rule ensures that employment authorization will be evidenced by a secure, unexpired document issued following adjudication. This reduces the risk that employers will rely on outdated or invalid documentation, and it aligns with DHS’s responsibility to ensure that employment authorization and/or an EAD is provided only after USCIS has completed appropriate vetting and determined continued eligibility.
为确保工作场所和企业清楚了解临时最终规则(IFR)的各项要求,国土安全部已更新《雇主手册》(M-274)及相关指导文件,以协助雇主理解这些变更并正确填写I-9表格。雇主有责任确保其所有员工的I-9合规性,包括I-9核验流程的更新。87国土安全部承认,在现有工作许可文件到期前续签裁定尚未完成的情况下,部分雇主可能面临临时的人员短缺及相关成本。这些成本可能包括生产力损失、留任员工的加班费用、临时或替代员工的招聘与培训费用,以及与规划、审计和合规相关的内部行政成本。国土安全部已在临时最终规则及本规则所附的经济分析中讨论了这些类别的影响,并认识到87参见国土安全部、美国公民及移民服务局,《雇主手册》M-274,第5.0节“特定情形下就业授权和/或工作许可文件(EAD)的自动延期”(最后更新于2025年10月30日),https://www.uscis.gov/i-9-central/form-i-9-resources/handbook-for-employers-m- 274/50-automatic-extensions-of-employment-authorization-andor-employment-authorization-documents- eads-in。
To ensure that workplaces and businesses have a clear understanding of the requirements of the IFR, DHS has updated the Handbook for Employers (M-274) and related guidance to assist employers in understanding these changes and properly completing Form I-9. Employers are responsible for ensuring I-9 compliance for all of their employees, including updates to the I-9 verification process. 87 DHS acknowledges that, where renewal adjudications are not completed before current EADs expire, some employers may face temporary staffing gaps and associated costs. These can include lost productivity, overtime for remaining staff, recruitment and training for temporary or replacement workers, and internal administrative costs associated with planning, auditing, and compliance. DHS discussed these categories of impacts in the IFR and in the economic analysis accompanying this rule and recognizes 87 See DHS, USCIS, Handbook for Employers M-274, 5.0 Automatic Extensions of Employment Authorization and/or Employment Authorization Documents (EADs) in Certain Circumstances (last updated Oct. 30, 2025), https://www.uscis.gov/i-9-central/form-i-9-resources/handbook-for-employers-m- 274/50-automatic-extensions-of-employment-authorization-andor-employment-authorization-documents- eads-in.
部分雇主可能因此受到负面影响。88国土安全部已在本序言第V.B.1节中概述了估算将失效的工作许可文件数量所面临的困难。然而,在本最终规则中,国土安全部已对经济影响作出估算,并强调由于处理时间的动态变化、申请数量及申请行为等因素,影响很可能存在差异。因此,国土安全部提供了一个较宽的估算区间。
there may be negative impacts for some employers.88 DHS outlined the difficulties in estimating the number of EADs that would lapse in the Section V.B.1. of this preamble. However, in this final rule DHS has made estimates of economic impacts and emphasizes that there is likely to be variation in the impacts, due to factors such as dynamic process times, filing volumes, and filing behavior. For this reason, DHS provides a wide range for the estimates.
国土安全部已确定,这些运营方面的负担,相较于与自动工作许可文件延期相关的国家安全、公共安全及福利完整性方面的考量,属于可接受的代价。自动工作许可文件延期允许就业授权和/或工作许可文件的有效期仅凭一份待决的续签申请即可延续长达540天,而此时美国公民及移民服务局尚未完成更新的背景审查和裁定。鉴于当前的安全优先事项以及要求加强对已在美国境内的外国人进行审查的行政命令,国土安全部已得出结论,继续依赖自动延期并非管理裁定积压的可接受方式。独立于本规则之外,国土安全部已采取预期将减少初次及续签工作许可文件申请数量的措施,具体做法包括缩小假释(parole)的使用范围,以及使临时保护身份(TPS)和相关政策与制定法文本保持一致。如先前在对评论的回应中所讨论,国土安全部预计工作许可文件申请总量将下降,从而使裁定能力能够日益集中用于及时处理续签裁定。
DHS has determined that these operational burdens are outweighed by the national security, public safety, and benefit integrity concerns associated with automatic EAD extensions. Automatic EAD extensions allow employment authorization and/or EAD validity to continue for up to 540 days based solely on a pending renewal application, before USCIS has completed updated background checks and adjudication. Considering current security priorities and E.O.s directing enhanced vetting of aliens already present in the United States, DHS has concluded that continued reliance on automatic extensions is not an acceptable means of managing adjudication backlogs. Independent of this rule, DHS has taken steps expected to reduce initial and renewal EAD filing volumes by narrowing the use of parole and aligning TPS and related policies with statutory text. As discussed previously in response to comments, DHS anticipates that overall EAD filings will decline and that adjudicative capacity can increasingly be focused on timely renewal adjudications.
美国国土安全部(DHS)指出，申请人可以要求美国公民及移民服务局(USCIS)加快审理属于USCIS管辖范围内的申请。89 USCIS根据具体情况逐案审议所有加急请求，通常要求提供文件以支持此类请求。90 是否加急处理的决定完全由USCIS自行裁量。91 加急处理某一案件 88 参见90 FR 48799, 48817 (2025年10月30日)。另见本序言第五节。89 参见USCIS，加急请求，https://www.uscis.gov/forms/filing-guidance/expedite-requests（最后更新于2026年9月8日）。
DHS notes that an applicant may request that USCIS expedite the adjudication of an application that is under USCIS jurisdiction.89 USCIS considers all expedite requests on a case-by-case basis and generally requires documentation to support such requests.90 The decision to expedite is within the sole discretion of USCIS.91 Expediting a case 88 See 90 FR 48799, 48817 (Oct. 30, 2025). See also Section V of this preamble. 89 See USCIS, Expedite Requests, https://www.uscis.gov/forms/filing-guidance/expedite-requests (last updated Sep. 8, 2026).
