通常意味着USCIS将在其他申请（包括可能更早提交的申请）之前审理某一福利申请，因此USCIS会仔细权衡每一项加急请求的紧迫性和合理性。92 DHS认识到雇主、工会和行业团体所提出的关切，并将继续监测实施情况和处理时间。尽管如此，DHS认为，确保仅在完成全面审查和裁决后才授予续期的就业授权，对于更好地保护国家安全和公共安全、增强就业核实系统的完整性是必要的。坚持一个优先考虑国家安全、公共安全和福利完整性的制度，其重要性超过了申请人及其雇主因就业授权可能出现的暂时性中断而面临的潜在风险和额外负担。
generally means that USCIS would adjudicate a benefit ahead of others, including those who may have filed earlier, so USCIS carefully weighs the urgency and merit of each expedite request.92 DHS recognizes the concerns raised by employers, unions, and industry groups and will continue to monitor implementation and processing times. Nonetheless, DHS concludes that ensuring that renewed employment authorization is granted only after complete vetting and adjudication is necessary to better protect national security and public safety and to enhance the integrity of the employment verification system. Adhering to a system that prioritizes national security, public safety, and benefit integrity outweighs the potential risks and additional burdens that applicants and their employers may face by a potential temporary lapse in employment authorization.
b. 移民对经济的贡献以及该规则对宏观经济和地方经济的影响 评论：众多评论者指出，该临时最终规则(IFR)忽视了移民对经济的重要贡献，以及就业授权作为成功将外国人融入美国社会并为经济作出贡献的关键工具这一实际运作情况。评论者还表示，该临时最终规则正在扰乱雇用这些外国人的企业，从而使移民的经济贡献受到损失。例如，一些评论者讨论了庇护申请人和难民对美国经济的积极影响，并指出这一群体为美国政府带来的税收收入超过了其所产生的支出。另一位评论者指出，2023年纽约州的移民人口约占总人口的23%，缴纳了748亿美元的税款，这表明移民是劳动力队伍中富有生产力的成员。该评论者还表示，就业授权的中断可能意味着他们将失去住房、面临饥饿、向州和联邦政府缴纳的税款减少 92 同上。
b. Immigrant Contributions to the Economy and Macroeconomic and Local Economic Impacts of the Rule Comment: Numerous commenters stated that the IFR ignores the vital economic contributions of immigrants, and operational realities that the work authorization is a critical tool to successfully integrate aliens into the United States and contribute to the economy. Commenters also stated that the IFR is disrupting businesses that employ these aliens and thereby losing the economic contribution of immigrants. For example, commenters discussed the positive impacts that asylees and refugees make to the U.S. economy and stated that this population contributed more revenue than they cost in expenditures to the U.S. government. Another commenter said that in 2023, New York State’s immigrant population represented about 23 percent of the total population and paid $74.8 billion in taxes, thus demonstrating that immigrants are productive members of the workforce. The commenter also stated that a lapse in employment authorization could mean that they would lose their homes, face hunger, contribute less taxes to state 92 See id.
或在其他方面变得脆弱（例如遭受负面健康影响）。另一位评论者表示，移民工人是纽约市经济的关键驱动力，占该市人口的36%，占工资、薪金和营业收入总额的37%；并且在2021年，为该市和该州贡献了数百亿至数千亿美元的税收和消费支出。另一位评论者指出，包括寻求庇护者和其他移民在内的劳动力迁入，是为数不多的能够抵消老年劳动力快速退出趋势的积极因素之一，并强调许多经济发展战略依赖于移民持续获得就业授权的能力。其他评论者强调了对经济的潜在影响，指出寻求庇护者和难民在面临短缺的行业工作，缴纳数十亿美元的税款，并且在未来十年内移民将为经济增加7万亿美元。
and the federal government or would otherwise become vulnerable (such as incurring negative health effects). Another commenter said that immigrant workers are a key driver of New York City’s economy, making up 36 percent of the population; accounting for 37 percent of total wage, salary, and business income; and in 2021, contributed tens to hundreds of billions of dollars in taxes and spending to the city and state. An additional commenter identified in-migration of workers, including asylum seekers and other immigrants, as one of the few positive trends offsetting the rapid exit of older workers and emphasized that many economic development strategies depend on immigrants’ continuous access to work authorization. Other commenters highlighted potential impacts on the economy, pointing out that asylum seekers and refugees work in sectors that face shortages and pay billions in taxes, and that immigrants will add $7 trillion to the economy over the next decade.
评论者对取消就业授权文件(EAD)自动延期可能产生的宏观经济影响表示担忧，例如通过迫使劳动者陷入暂时性失业，从而削弱美国的经济竞争力、创新能力和国家利益。其他评论者预测，该临时最终规则将导致负面经济影响，包括税收减少、纳税人成本增加、供应链中断、消费者支出减少、经济衰退风险以及失业问题。一些评论者普遍担心，鉴于美国每五名劳动者中就有一名是依赖就业授权文件的移民，该临时最终规则将降低经济生产力，并使劳动力市场和经济陷入不稳定状态。
Commenters raised concerns about the macroeconomic impacts of eliminating automatic extensions of EADs, such as undermining U.S. economic competitiveness, innovation capacity, and national interests by forcing workers into temporary unemployment. Other commenters predicted that the IFR would lead to negative economic impacts, including reduced tax revenue, costs to taxpayers, supply chain disruptions, reduced consumer spending, the risk of a recession, and job losses. Some commenters expressed general concern that the IFR will reduce economic productivity and destabilize the workforce and the economy, considering that one in five U.S. workers is an immigrant who relies on EADs.
评论者表示,该临时最终规则(IFR)将通过提高移民中无保险人群的比例,显著增加各州的医疗保健成本,这些移民本已面临获得医疗补助(Medicaid)和《平价医疗法案》(Affordable Care Act)保险的有限机会。评论者指出,由于工作许可中断导致雇主提供的保险失效,各州可能需承担近2800亿美元的未补偿医疗费用。评论者还表示,该临时最终规则将对各州执行本州法律的能力产生不利影响,其理由是,工作许可出现中断的合法劳工将越来越多地进入地下经济,并因此更不愿意举报劳工权益和公民权利方面的违法行为,评论者还讨论了这将如何妨碍各州执行其劳工法和公民权利法的能力,因为有效执法依赖于雇员举报违法行为的能力和意愿。
Commenters said that the IFR would significantly increase States’ healthcare costs by driving up the uninsured rate among immigrants, who already face limited access to Medicaid and Affordable Care Act coverage. The commenters remarked that with lapses in work authorization eliminating employer-sponsored insurance, States could bear nearly $280 billion in uncompensated care costs. The commenters also stated that the IFR would adversely impact the States’ ability to enforce their laws, reasoning that legal workers with lapses in work authorization would increasingly enter the underground economy and become less likely to report labor and civil rights violations and discussed how this would interfere with the States’ ability to enforce their labor and civil rights laws, because effective enforcement relies on employees’ ability and willingness to report violations.
一位评论者表示,该临时最终规则将对联邦预算以及美国国税局(IRS)和劳工部(DOL)等联邦机构产生重大的负面影响,因为当申请人面临长期的工作许可中断时,该规则将减少向国税局申报的应税收入金额,从而削减联邦收入并使联邦财政预测复杂化。该评论者进一步主张,中断等待续期的外国人的工作许可,将迫使成千上万的工人从事未经授权的工作,或者完全退出劳动力市场,从而增加劳工部的调查负担。
A commenter said the IFR would have significant negative consequences on the federal budget and federal agencies such as the Internal Revenue Service (IRS) and Department of Labor (DOL) because the IFR would reduce the amount of taxable income reported to the IRS when applicants face prolonged lapses in employment authorization, diminishing federal revenue and complicating federal fiscal projections. The commenter further asserted that cutting off work authorization for aliens awaiting renewal would push thousands of workers into unauthorized employment or out of the workforce entirely and consequently increase DOL’s investigatory burdens.
此外,评论者还强调了一系列对地方和州经济的潜在负面影响,例如扰乱劳动力队伍并妨碍外国人维持稳定就业的能力,而这反过来又可能直接影响基础设施项目、医疗保健和公共安全等基本社区服务。一位评论者强调,移民在创业方面发挥着重要作用。另一位评论者指出,如果移民工人失去工作许可,缅因州将失去其目前正在经历的经济繁荣。
Furthermore, commenters highlighted a range of potential negative impacts on the local and state economy, such as disrupting the workforce and hindering an alien’s ability to maintain consistent employment, which, in turn, could directly affect essential community services, including infrastructure projects, healthcare, and public safety. A commenter highlighted that immigrants play a major role in entrepreneurship. Another commenter remarked that Maine stands to lose an economic boom they are currently experiencing if immigrant workers lose work authorization.
在表达对该临时最终规则监管变化将对经济产生影响的担忧的同时,多位评论者要求恢复此前允许就业许可文件(EAD)有效期自动延期的做法。
While expressing concerns about the impacts the IFR’s regulatory changes would have on the economy, multiple commenters requested a return to previous practices allowing automatic extension of EAD validity periods.
答复:国土安全部(DHS)同意,包括那些有资格获得最长540天工作许可和/或就业许可文件自动延期的外国人在内的移民,通过其劳动、创业、纳税和消费支出,为国家和地方经济作出了重要贡献;并且持续合法就业可以使工人、雇主和社区受益。国土安全部还承认评论者援引的大量研究、数据和经验,以支持其关于这些贡献的主张。
Response: DHS agrees that immigrants, including aliens who were eligible for the automatic extension of their employment authorization and/or EAD for up to 540 days, make important contributions to the national and local economies through their labor, entrepreneurship, tax payments, and consumer spending; and that continuity of lawful employment can benefit workers, employers, and communities. DHS also acknowledges the many cited studies, data points, and experiences to support their claims about these contributions.
正如评论者所指出的,国土安全部此前的规则制定,包括2022年和2024年的临时最终规则以及2024年的最终规则,均强调自动延期作为缓解就业许可文件处理延误影响的一项工具所具有的经济和运作效益。这些规则估计,自动延期可以减少受影响工人的工资损失,避免雇主的人员流动成本,并维持原本可能因工作许可中断而受到影响的税收贡献。在采纳这些措施时,国土安全部高度重视就业连续性所带来的经济和劳动力市场优势。
As the commenters noted, prior DHS rulemakings, including the 2022 and 2024 temporary final rules and the 2024 final rule, emphasized the economic and operational benefits of automatic extensions as a tool to mitigate the effects of EAD processing delays. Those rules estimated that automatic extensions could reduce wage losses for affected workers, avoid turnover costs for employers, and maintain tax contributions that might otherwise be impacted by lapses in employment authorization. In adopting those measures, DHS gave significant weight to the economic and labor market advantages of continuity of employment.
与此相反,临时最终规则和本最终规则反映了对该政策的重新评估,以及如何在这些经济考量与国土安全部(DHS)在国家安全、公共安全和福利诚信方面的核心职责之间进行平衡。DHS已认定,继续使用自动延期,即仅凭续签申请的提交,就允许就业授权及相关安全证件的使用再持续长达540天,而无需美国公民及移民服务局(USCIS)先完成更新的审查、解决任何不利信息并认定该外国人仍然符合资格且(如适用)仍值得获得有利的自由裁量,这种做法与DHS当前的政策优先事项不符,此类政策优先事项尤其体现在第1415993号和第14161.94号行政命令之中。这些行政命令指示DHS对已经身处美国境内的外国人进行审查和甄别,达到93参见第14159号行政命令《保护美国人民免受入侵》,《联邦纪事》第90卷第8443页(2025年1月29日)94参见第14161号行政命令《保护美国免受恐怖主义和其他国家安全及公共安全威胁》,《联邦纪事》第90卷第8451页(2025年1月31日)。另参见第10949号总统公告《限制外国国民入境以保护美国免受外国恐怖分子和其他国家安全及公共安全威胁》,《联邦纪事》第90卷第24497页(2025年6月10日)。
The IFR and this final rule in contrast reflect a reassessment of that policy and how to balance those economic considerations against DHS’s core responsibilities for national security, public safety, and benefit integrity. DHS has determined that continued use of automatic extensions, which allow employment authorization and the related use of secure documentation to continue for up to 540 days based solely on the filing of a renewal application, before USCIS has completed updated vetting, resolved any derogatory information, and determined that the alien remains eligible and continues to merit a favorable exercise of discretion, if applicable, is not consistent with DHS’s current policy priorities reflected in, among others, E.O. 1415993 and 14161.94 Those E.O.s direct DHS to vet and screen aliens already present in the United States to the 93 See Executive Order 14159 “Protecting the American People Against Invasion,” 90 FR 8443 (Jan. 29, 2025) 94 See Executive Order 14161, “Protecting the United States from Terrorist and Other National Security and Public Safety Threats,” 90 FR 8451 (Jan. 31, 2025). See also Presidential Proclamation 10949, “Restricting the Entry of Foreign Nationals To Protect the United States From Foreign Terrorists and Other National Security and Public Safety Threats,” 90 FR 24497 (June 10, 2025).
最大可能的程度,以确保就业授权不会提供给未获授权的外国人,并查明可用于确保所有已经身处美国境内的外国人都得到最大程度审查和甄别的所有资源。
maximum degree possible, to ensure that employment authorization is not provided to unauthorized aliens, and to identify all resources that may be used to ensure that all aliens who are already in the United States, are vetted and screened to the maximum degree possible.
因此,终止自动延长就业授权文件的做法,能够提升就业授权申请裁决中的福利诚信,并通过确保外国人在获得就业授权之前已得到妥善审查,并被认定仍然符合资格(且如适用,仍值得获得有利的自由裁量),从而更好地保护公共安全和国家安全。DHS强调,其依据《移民与国籍法》第274A条、8 U.S.C. 1324a的规定,负有打击未经授权就业的职责,国会制定该条款的原因之一,是未经授权就业和非法移民对美国及其经济造成的不利负面影响。95鉴于上述指令,DHS现在更加重视安全和福利诚信方面的考量。
Accordingly, ending the practice of providing automatic extension of employment authorization documents enhances the benefit integrity in adjudications of employment authorization requests and better protects public safety and national security by ensuring that aliens are properly vetted and determined to continue to be eligible (and merit a favorable exercise of discretion, if applicable) for employment authorization before such authorization is provided to the alien. DHS emphasizes that it is charged with combating unauthorized employment in accordance with section 274A of the Act, 8 U.S.C. 1324a, which Congress implemented, among other reasons, because of the detrimental negative effect that unauthorized employment and illegal immigration has on the United States and the economy.95 In light of these directives, DHS is now placing greater weight on security and benefit integrity considerations.
DHS认识到,终止自动延期可能导致部分续签申请人的就业授权和/或EAD有效期出现暂时中断,如果其续签EAD申请的裁决未能在现有EAD到期前完成。此类中断可能导致受影响劳工工资损失、雇主产出损失及人员更替成本增加,并在中断期间造成相应的工资税和所得税缴纳减少。DHS还承认,此类影响可能更为95《移民与国籍法》第274A条、8 U.S.C. 1324a系作为1986年《移民改革与控制法》(Pub. L. 99-603)的一部分予以编纂,其目的包括通过对明知雇用无证件外国人的雇主设定处罚,以及禁止雇用因非法入境或所持移民身份不允许就业而无授权在美国工作的外国人,来控制美国的非法移民问题。参见H.R. REP. 99-682, 第46页, 1986 U.S.C.C.A.N. 5649, 第5649-5650页。立法历史显示,国会除其他事项外,还考虑了此前研究中关于非法移民不利影响的证据,这些证据表明有必要立法保护美国劳工和经济。参见,例如,H.R. REP. 99-682, 第46至52页, 1986 U.S.C.C.A.N. 5649, 第5649-5656页。
DHS recognizes that ending automatic extensions may result in temporary lapses in employment authorization and/or EAD validity for some renewal applicants if the adjudication of their renewal EAD application is not completed before their current EAD expires. Those lapses can lead to lost wages for affected workers, lost output and turnover costs for employers, and associated reductions in payroll and income tax contributions for the duration of the lapse. DHS also acknowledges that such impacts may be more 95 Section 274A of the Act, 8 U.S.C. 1324a was codified as part of the Immigration Reform and Control Act of 1986, Pub. L. 99-603, for the purpose of controlling illegal immigration to the United States by, among other things, establishing penalties for employers who knowingly hire undocumented aliens, and prohibit the employment of aliens who are unauthorized to work in the United States either because they entered the country illegally or are in an immigration status which does not permit employment. See H.R. REP. 99-682, 46, 1986 U.S.C.C.A.N. 5649, 5649-5650. The legislative history reflects that Congress considered, among other things, evidence of prior studies demonstrating the adverse impact of illegal immigration that warranted legislation to protect U.S. labor and the economy. See, e.g., H.R. REP. 99-682, 46 – 52, 1986 U.S.C.C.A.N. 5649, 5649 - 5656.
显著,特别是在EAD持有人占劳动力比例较大的某些地区、行业或社区,并且雇主和政府在适应这一变化的过程中可能面临过渡性挑战。
pronounced in certain localities, sectors, or communities where EAD holders represent a substantial share of the workforce, and that employers and governments may face transitional challenges as they adjust to this change.
尽管美国国土安全部（DHS）在预测未来受影响人群的规模及由此产生的宏观经济影响程度方面面临不确定性，但在若干假设条件下，DHS已对特定时间段内的受影响人群和成本影响进行了估算。如临时最终规则（IFR）及本序言前文所述，近年来，由于申请量变化、项目政策、资源水平及法院命令等因素的影响，就业授权文件（EAD）的处理时间一直在波动。DHS还实施了若干重大政策变更，例如缩小假释（parole）的适用范围，并使临时保护身份（TPS）及相关项目更好地符合法定标准，预计这些变更将随着时间推移大幅减少初次申请和续签EAD的数量。鉴于这一不断变化的环境，DHS对在缺乏自动延期的情况下可能出现工作许可中断的续签申请人数量的估计，以及由此得出的影响估计，很可能存在显著差异。因此，我们明确说明了用于作出上述估计所依据的假设和输入数据，并为其提供了数值范围。
While DHS faces uncertainty in projecting the size of any future affected population and the magnitude of resulting macroeconomic impacts, under several assumptions DHS has estimated the impacted population and cost-impacts for a specific window of time. As explained in the IFR and earlier in this preamble, EAD processing times have fluctuated over recent years due to changes in application volumes, programmatic policies, resource levels, and court orders. DHS has also undertaken significant policy changes, such as narrowing the use of parole and better aligning TPS and related programs with statutory criteria, that are expected to materially reduce initial and renewal EAD filings over time. Given this evolving environment, DHS estimates of the number of renewal applicants who might experience lapses in the absence of automatic extensions population, and the resultant impact estimates, likely face significant variation. For this reason, we clearly articulate the assumptions and inputs relied upon to make the estimates and provide ranges for them. .
关于评论者对税收减少和无偿医疗护理增加的担忧，DHS承认，在部分劳动者失业期间，其劳动所得减少可能会减少某些税款缴纳以及对雇主提供的医疗保险计划的供款。然而，税收总额和医疗成本取决于更广泛经济体系中的众多因素，DHS无法以足够的精确度将此类总体影响归因于本规则或对其进行预测，以支持针对本规则的量化估计。
With respect to commenters’ concerns about reduced tax revenues and increased uncompensated care, DHS acknowledges that lower earned income during periods when some workers are not employed could reduce certain tax payments and contributions toward employer-sponsored health coverage for those individuals. At the same time, total tax receipts and healthcare costs depend on numerous factors across the broader economy, and DHS cannot attribute or forecast such aggregate effects with sufficient precision to support a quantitative estimate specific to this rule.
DHS同样不认同终止自动延期将导致或要求大量工人转为未经授权就业的观点。96 尽管DHS承认未经授权的就业可能形成地下劳动力市场，在该市场中剥削行为更可能发生，执法也更为困难，但本规则并不要求也不纵容未经授权的就业。《移民与国籍法》（INA）禁止明知雇用未经授权外国人的行为，并对从事此类行为的雇主和外国人均规定了相应后果。参见INA第274A条，8 U.S.C. 1324a；8 CFR第274a部分。DHS将继续执行上述规定，并与劳工部及其他机构协调开展劳工权益执法和工人保护工作，不论其移民身份如何。本规则不改变现行的工人保护权限，也不改变联邦、州或地方实体调查和纠正劳动法违规行为的能力。
DHS also does not agree that ending automatic extensions will necessitate or require a shift of large numbers of workers into unauthorized employment.96 While DHS acknowledges that unauthorized work can create an underground labor market where exploitation is more likely and enforcement more difficult, this rule does not require or condone unauthorized employment. The INA prohibits the knowing employment of unauthorized aliens and establishes consequences for both employers and aliens who engage in such conduct. See INA sec. 274A, 8 U.S.C. 1324a; 8 CFR part 274a. DHS will continue to enforce those provisions, as well as coordinate with the Department of Labor and other agencies on labor-rights enforcement and protection of workers, regardless of immigration status. Nothing in this rule alters existing worker-protection authorities or the ability of Federal, State, or local entities to investigate and remedy labor law violations.
DHS进一步指出，本规则不改变就业授权的基本资格标准，也不妨碍续签申请人获得就业授权及/或EAD。符合条件的外国人仍可申请并获得续签的EAD，一旦该续签获得批准，便可通过合法就业继续为经济作出贡献。DHS预计，DHS已作出的其他政策变更将减少EAD申请量，再加上美国公民及移民服务局（USCIS）持续推进的流程改进和审慎的资源配置，将降低未来就业授权及/或EAD出现中断的频率和持续时间。97 DHS观察到，自2025年10月以来，I-765表格的申请数量呈下降趋势。参见本序言第IV.C.2.i.d节表1。
DHS further notes that this rule does not change underlying eligibility criteria for employment authorization or prevent renewal applicants from obtaining employment authorization and/or an EAD. Eligible aliens remain able to apply for and receive renewed EADs, and, once those renewals are approved, continue contributing economically through lawful employment. DHS expects that other policy changes that DHS has made will reduce EAD filing volumes, and when combined with ongoing process improvements and careful resource allocation within USCIS, will lessen the frequency and duration of any future lapses in employment authorization and/or EADs.97 DHS has seen falling numbers of I-765 applications since October 2025. See Table 1 in Section IV.C.2.i.d. of this preamble.
续签申请人可以进一步降低其出现临时中断的风险。96 鉴于USCIS当时的运营实际情况，将最长540天的自动延期期限永久化的最终规则所依据的是推测性假设。参见89 FR 101208, 101245（其中指出“本最终规则的目的是提供一项长期解决方案，以缓解因不可预测情况导致续签EAD申请处理时间大幅增加、从而需要未来采取紧急行动的可能性”）。
Renewal applicants can further reduce their risk of a temporary lapse in 96 The final rule making the up to 540-day automatic extension period permanent was based on speculative assumptions given the operational realities at USCIS at the time. See 89 FR 101208, 101245 (noting “the purpose of this final rule is to provide a long-term solution to mitigate the potential for unpredictable circumstances to significantly increase renewal EAD application processing times that would require future urgent action”).
97 关于2025年1月至2026年6月期间，最大的两个资格类别C08和C09的I-765表格申请人数量，参见本序言第IV.C.2.i.d节表1。
97 See Table 1 in Section IV.C.2.i.d. of this preamble for numbers of I-765 applicants from the largest eligibility categories, the C08 and C09, from January 2025 to June 2026.
尽量在适用法规和表格说明允许的最早时间提交就业授权申请,确保其申请中包含证明其持续符合资格所需的全部证据,从而使处理过程不会因补充证据请求(RFE)而延误,并随时了解美国公民及移民服务局(USCIS)发布的最新处理时间信息。98申请人的妥善规划,连同预期中整体就业授权文件(EAD)需求的减少,可能有助于减轻任何临时性中断的影响程度。
employment authorization by filing as early as permitted under applicable regulations and form instructions, ensuring that their application includes all evidence needed to establish continued eligibility so that processing is not delayed by a Request for Evidence, and by keeping informed of current processing-time information posted by USCIS.98 Proper planning by applicants, together with anticipated reductions in overall EAD demand, may help mitigate the extent of any temporary disruptions.
如上文所述,国土安全部(DHS)认为,取消自动延期所带来的公共安全、国家安全和福利诚信方面的益处,超过了评论者所指出的潜在宏观经济和地方经济成本。终止自动延长就业授权文件的做法,增强了就业授权申请裁决中的福利诚信,并通过确保外国人在获得就业授权之前经过适当审查并被认定持续符合资格(如适用,还需获得自由裁量权的有利行使),更好地保护了公共安全和国家安全。
As discussed above, DHS believes the public safety, national security and benefit-integrity benefits of eliminating automatic extensions outweigh the potential macroeconomic and local economic costs identified by commenters. Ending the practice of providing automatic extension of employment authorization documents enhances the benefit integrity in adjudications of employment authorization requests and better protects public safety and national security by ensuring that aliens are properly vetted and determined to continue to be eligible (and merit a favorable exercise of discretion, if applicable) for employment authorization before such authorization is provided to the alien.
iii. 对特定移民类别的救济 a. 对难民、庇护获得者、庇护申请人、无人陪伴外国儿童(UAC)及其他人道主义类别的影响 评论意见:多位评论者表示,难民、庇护获得者、临时保护身份(TPS)受益人、《反对妇女暴力法》(VAWA)自我申请人及无人陪伴儿童将因工作授权中断而受到不成比例的伤害,原因在于他们的脆弱性、获得公共福利的渠道有限(这使得工作授权对其福祉至关重要),以及他们依赖工作来维持生计、安全和融入社会。一位评论者指出,临时最终规则(IFR)不仅会破坏劳动力队伍的稳定,还会给难民、庇护获得者(包括那些申请尚未裁决者)及其他98 USCIS在USCIS.gov上公布案件处理时间,并定期更新。参见USCIS,案件处理时间,https://egov.uscis.gov/processing-times(最近访问日期为2026年5月8日)。人道主义入境者及其家庭造成不应有的痛苦和持久伤害,而这一切仅仅是由于政府的处理延误以及该群体因就业中断所受到的影响所致。一些评论者表示担忧,特定国籍的庇护申请人,包括海地人、乌克兰人、亚裔美国人及太平洋岛民以及阿富汗人,受临时最终规则的影响尤为不成比例。此外,其中一些评论者指出,这些外国人并非通过未知或非正式途径入境,而是通过已知的、经过审查的人道主义项目入境,他们是遵纪守法、具有生产力的个人,曾使政府和经济受益。因此,尽管同时承认该部在福利诚信、安全审查方面的利益,以及使政策与近期强调执法和严格审查的行政指令保持一致的利益,一位评论者仍主张采取一种狭义的、基于风险的方法,在保留临时最终规则核心逻辑的同时,避免对经过审查的人道主义群体造成“全有或全无”的结果。
iii. Relief for Specific Immigration Categories a. Impact on Refugees, Asylees, Asylum Seekers, Unaccompanied Alien Children (UACs), and Other Humanitarian Categories Comment: Multiple commenters stated that refugees, asylees, TPS beneficiaries, VAWA self-petitioners, and unaccompanied children will be disproportionately harmed by gaps in work authorization because of their vulnerability, limited access to public benefits, which makes work authorization crucial to their wellbeing, and reliance on work to support themselves, safety, and integration. A commenter indicated that the IFR would not only destabilize the workforce, but would inflict unconscionable distress and cause lasting harm to refugees, asylees (including those with pending claims) and other 98 USCIS publishes case processing times on USCIS.gov that are regularly updated. See USCIS, Case Processing Times, https://egov.uscis.gov/processing-times (last visited May 8, 2026). humanitarian entrants and their families, solely on account of the government’s processing delay and the impact of lapses in employment on this population. Some commenters expressed concern that asylum seekers of specific nationalities including Haitians, Ukrainians, Asian Americans and Pacific Islanders, and Afghans were disproportionally affected by the IFR. Additionally, some of these commenters indicated that these aliens did not enter through an unknown or informal process but known, vetted and humanitarian programs, and are law-abiding and productive individuals that benefited the government and the economy. For that reason – while at the same time acknowledging the Department’s interest in benefit integrity, security vetting, and aligning policies with recent executive directives emphasizing enforcement and careful screening, a commenter advocated for a narrow, risk-sensitive approach that preserves the core logic of the interim final rule while avoiding an “all or nothing” outcome for the vetted humanitarian population.
另一位评论者指出,取消EAD自动延期将对通过《反对妇女暴力法》(VAWA)获得身份的人员,尤其是亚裔美国人及太平洋岛民女性,造成严重后果,这些女性尤其容易受到此类伤害,因为工作中断可能迫使她们陷入不安全的处境或依赖施虐伴侣。其他评论者表示,获得就业授权为性别暴力幸存者提供了安全感、稳定性和更好的生活质量。一位评论者解释说,施虐者通常滥用移民制度,例如以扣留、撤回或破坏移民申请作为威胁手段,以此作为控制和恐吓的工具,并预测该规则将加剧这一问题。该意见提请注意EAD续签申请裁决中存在的积压问题,并预测由于就业中断,幸存者及其家庭将面临困境。
Another commenter remarked that the removal of the automatic extension of EADs would have severe consequences for those obtaining status through the Violence Against Women Act (VAWA), in particular Asian American and Pacific Islander women, who are particularly vulnerable to these harms, as work interruptions can force them into unsafe situations or dependence on abusive partners. Others indicated that access to employment authorization offers safety, stability, and a better quality of life for survivors of gender-based violence. One commenter explained that abusers commonly misuse the immigration system, for example, by threatening to withhold, withdraw, or sabotage immigration filings, as a means of control and intimidation, and predicted the rule would exacerbate this problem. This submission called attention to backlogs in adjudicating EAD renewal applications and predicted hardships for survivors and families due to lapses in employment.
一位评论者表示担忧,称无人陪伴儿童和青少年将受到负面影响。该评论者指出,年轻的寻求保护者,即使是那些尚不具备工作资格的人,也经常依赖就业authorization文件(EAD)作为一种政府身份证明形式,以获取心理、教育和住房服务等基本社会服务和照护。该评论者还表示,EAD“为年龄较大的青少年获得关键的就业和培训机会提供了便利”。答复:国土安全部(DHS)理解,难民、庇护获准者、庇护申请人、临时保护身份(TPS)受益人、《针对妇女暴力行为法》(VAWA)自我申请人、无人陪伴儿童以及其他人道主义群体,如果经历就业授权和(或)EAD有效期中断,可能特别容易遭受困难。正如临时最终规则(IFR)中就所有申请人(包括人道主义项目中的申请人)所认识到的那样,国土安全部深知就业授权和(或)EAD有效期的中断可能对个人外侨及其家庭产生影响。许多在美国的申请人可能曾逃离迫害、暴力或灾难,获得公共福利的渠道可能有限,并且往往高度依赖就业授权来维持自身及家人的生活,并融入其所在社区。
A commenter expressed concern that unaccompanied children and youth would be negatively impacted. The commenter said that young protection seekers, even those not yet eligible to work, often rely on EADs as a form of government identification to access essential social services and care such as psychological, education, and housing services. The commenter also said that EADs “facilitate critical access to jobs and training opportunities for older youth.” Response: DHS understands that refugees, asylees, asylum applicants, TPS beneficiaries, VAWA self-petitioners, unaccompanied children, and other humanitarian populations may be particularly vulnerable to hardship if they experience lapses in employment authorization and/or EAD validity. As recognized in the IFR for purposes of all applicants, including those in humanitarian programs, DHS is cognizant that a lapse in employment authorization and/or EAD validity may impact individual aliens, and their families. Many applicants in the United States may have fled persecution, violence, or disaster, may have limited access to public benefits, and often rely heavily on employment authorization to support themselves and their families and to integrate into their communities.
本规则并未取消上述任何类别人员获得就业授权的资格。难民和庇护获准者(不包括庇护申请人)根据8 CFR 274a.12(a)条款,凭借其身份即可获得就业授权。只要其维持该身份,无论作为该授权证明所签发的任何EAD上标明的有效期届满日期如何,他们均可继续工作。同样,拥有待决庇护申请的外侨可根据8 CFR第208部分和8 CFR第274a部分的规定申请就业授权。TPS受益人同样根据《移民与国籍法》第244条(8 U.S.C. 1254a)及其实施细则(8 CFR第244部分)获得就业授权,与TPS相关的证明文件延期仍可依照法律或《联邦公报》通知继续提供。本规则不改变这些基本的资格认定框架。
This rule does not eliminate access to employment authorization for any of these categories. Refugees and asylees (not including asylum applicants) remain employment authorized incident to their status under 8 CFR 274a.12(a); they may continue to work so long as they maintain that status, regardless of the expiration date on any EAD issued as evidence of that authorization. Similarly, aliens with a pending asylum application may apply for employment authorization in accordance with the regulations provided at 8 CFR part 208 and 8 CFR part 274a. TPS beneficiaries likewise receive employment authorization consistent with section 244 of the Act, 8 U.S.C. 1254a, and its implementing regulations at 8 CFR 244, and TPS-related extensions of documentation may continue to be provided by law or Federal Register notice. This rule does not change those underlying eligibility frameworks.
IFR与本最终规则仅仅终止了这样一种做法,即仅凭2025年10月30日或之后提交的续签申请,就自动延长EAD的有效期,以及对于并非凭借身份即获授权的类别,自动延长随附的就业授权,但法律或《联邦公报》通知另行规定延期的情形除外(例如某些与TPS相关的文件)。国土安全部已确定,自动延长就业授权和(或)EAD,即便是针对弱势群体,也存在相同的根本性问题:即在美国公民及移民服务局(USCIS)就新信息(包括潜在的犯罪记录或其他不利信息)确定申请人是否持续符合资格、以及(如适用)是否应获得有利裁量权之前,这种做法仅凭一份待决的续签申请,便允许当事人继续获得就业授权并使用安全证件长达540天之久。
The IFR and this final rule simply end the practice of automatically extending the validity of EADs and, for categories not authorized incident to status, attendant employment authorization based solely on the filing of a renewal application on or after October 30, 2025, except where extensions are provided by law or Federal Register notice (such as certain TPS-related documents). DHS has determined that automatic extensions of employment authorization and/or EADs, even for vulnerable populations, present the same underlying concern: they allow continued employment authorization and use of secure documentation for up to 540 days based solely on a pending renewal application, before USCIS has determined continued eligibility and that the applicant merits a favorable exercise of discretion (if applicable) in light of any new information, including potential criminal or other derogatory information.
与第99号行政命令及本部的使命保持一致,国土安全部(DHS)认为,在解决这一核心安全与福利诚信问题的同时,为特定类别申请人维持单独的自动延期制度是不合理的。即便按照某评论者建议的方式,针对人道主义项目采取基于风险的做法,也无法解决以下核心问题,即一些外国人可能在未经更新的审查和裁决的情况下继续获得就业许可,并有可能利用相关文件获取其他福利。本部因此选择了一种统一的做法,将相同原则适用于所有受影响的类别。国土安全部承认,如果续签裁决未能在申请人现有就业许可文件(EAD)到期前完成,部分庇护申请人和其他人道主义申请人的就业许可可能会出现暂时性中断。尤其是庇护申请人,根据现行法规,其获得公共福利的渠道可能有限,收入中断可能难以承受。99 见第14159号行政命令《保护美国人民免受入侵》,联邦公报第90卷第8443页(2025年1月29日);第14161号行政命令《保护美国免受恐怖主义及其他国家安全与公共安全威胁》,联邦公报第90卷第8451页(2025年1月31日);第10949号总统公告《限制外国国民入境以保护美国免受外国恐怖分子及其他国家安全与公共安全威胁》,联邦公报第90卷第24497页(2025年6月10日)。
Consistent with Executive Orders99 and the Department’s mission, DHS does not believe that it is reasonable to maintain a separate automatic extension regime for particular categories of applicants, while at the same time addressing this core security and benefit integrity issue. Even a risk-based approach for humanitarian programs, as a commenter suggested, would fail to address the core concern that some aliens could continue employment authorization, and potentially use associated documents to obtain other benefits, without updated vetting and adjudication. The Department has instead chosen a uniform approach that applies the same principle across affected categories. DHS acknowledges that some asylum applicants and other humanitarian applicants may experience temporary lapses in employment authorization if renewal adjudications are not completed before their current EADs expire. Asylum applicants, in particular, may have limited access to public benefits under existing statutes, and gaps in 99 See Executive Order 14159 “Protecting the American People Against Invasion,” 90 FR 8443 (Jan. 29, 2025); Executive Order 14161, “Protecting the United States from Terrorist and Other National Security and Public Safety Threats,” 90 FR 8451 (Jan. 31, 2025); Presidential Proclamation 10949, “Restricting the Entry of Foreign Nationals To Protect the United States From Foreign Terrorists and Other National Security and Public Safety Threats,” 90 FR 24497 (June 10, 2025).
国土安全部考虑了这些因素,但认为这些因素并不足以超越以下必要性,即确保每次就业许可和/或就业许可文件(EAD)的续签,只有在美国公民及移民服务局(USCIS)完成适当审查,并确定申请人持续符合资格、且(如适用)继续值得给予有利的自由裁量后,方可予以批准。
income may be difficult to absorb. DHS considered these factors but concluded that they did not outweigh the need to ensure that each renewal of employment authorization and/or an EAD is granted only after USCIS has completed appropriate vetting and determined that the applicant continues to be eligible and continues to merit a favorable exercise of discretion, if applicable.
国土安全部指出,与本规则无关的是,根据现行法律,庇护身份和难民身份可赋予或可能导致获得某些公共福利的资格,且某些其他弱势群体也可能有资格获得专项支持项目。100 本规则并未改变这些制度框架。
DHS notes that, separate from this rule, asylum and refugee status confers or may lead to eligibility for certain public benefits under existing law, and certain other vulnerable groups may be eligible for specialized support programs.100 Those frameworks are not altered by this rule.
此外,国土安全部已采取措施,减少可能延误善意人道主义申请人裁决的无实质内容或无依据申请的激励因素,从而寻求改善具有正当诉求申请人的办理程序。例如,在过去四年中,美国公民及移民服务局(USCIS)发现了令人担忧的新申请趋势,包括《反对妇女暴力法》(VAWA)自我申请申请总量增长了360%。101 当不合格的外国人滥用VAWA项目时,会造成重大的办理延误,损害具有合法诉求的幸存者的利益。102 2025年12月22日,美国公民及移民服务局(USCIS)采取措施解决这些问题,更新了《USCIS政策手册》中的指导意见,以简化和明确所提交证据的要求,并减少对证据请求(RFE)和拟拒绝通知(NOID)的需求,以及其他澄清性修改。103 关于无人陪伴儿童和青少年,本规则不改变任何100 参见美国卫生与公众服务部《家庭暴力事实清单》(讨论移民家庭暴力幸存者获得卫生与公众服务部资助服务的情况),https://www.hhs.gov/civil-rights/for-individuals/special-topics/national-origin/domestic-violence/index.html(最近一次修订于2012年8月22日)。
In addition, DHS has taken steps to reduce incentives for frivolous or non-meritorious filings that might delay adjudications for bona fide humanitarian applicants, thereby seeking to improve processing for those with meritorious claims. For example, in the past four years, USCIS has seen alarming new filing trends, including a 360% overall increase in VAWA self-petition filings.101 When unqualified aliens misuse the VAWA program, it causes significant processing delays, harming survivors with legitimate claims.102 On December 22, 2025, USCIS took steps to address these issues by updating guidance in the USCIS Policy Manual to streamline and clarify the expectations of submitted evidence and reduce the need for Requests for Evidence and Notices of Intent to Deny, among other clarifying changes.103 With respect to unaccompanied children and youth, this rule does not change any 100 See, e.g., U.S. Department of Health and Human Services, Domestic Violence Fact Sheet (discussing access to HHS funded services for immigrant survivors of domestic violence), https://www.hhs.gov/civil- rights/for-individuals/special-topics/national-origin/domestic-violence/index.html (last revised Aug. 22, 2012).
101 参见美国公民及移民服务局(USCIS),《USCIS在发现普遍欺诈后恢复VAWA家庭虐待项目的诚信》,https://www.uscis.gov/newsroom/alerts/uscis-restores-integrity-to-the-vawa-domestic-abuse-program-after-finding-rampant-fraud(最近一次更新于2025年12月22日)。

适用于该群体的法定保护或程序性保障措施。104 就有资格获得就业许可的无人陪伴儿童和青少年为参加有效就业目的的职业培训机会而申请就业许可文件而言,他们仍然有资格提出申请,并在获批后依照现行法规开展工作。该临时最终规则及本最终规则均不影响或阻止将有效的就业许可文件用作身份证件,它们只是终止了对在2025年10月30日或之后提交的某些就业许可类别中,已及时提交延期申请的外国人自动延长其就业许可文件有效期的做法。国土安全部(DHS)认识到人道主义群体的高度脆弱性,并将继续依照法定保护措施和人道主义目标管理这些项目。然而,国土安全部认为,本规则所依据的核心原则,即在未完全完成裁决审查及相关审查程序(包括解决审查过程中发现的任何不利信息)之前,不应自动延长更新的就业许可,必须在受影响的各类别中一致适用,以履行国土安全部在国家安全、公共安全和福利诚信方面的职责。正如临时最终规则中详细说明的那样,在未完全完成包括适当审查在内的裁决审查的情况下授予福利,违背了国土安全部的法定使命,并对美国人民的安全与安保构成威胁。105 b. 对以就业为基础的非移民、其受抚养人及以就业为基础的绿卡申请人的影响 评论:多位评论者对取消H-4受抚养配偶就业许可文件自动延期表示关切。部分评论者表示,104 参见,例如《2008年威廉·威尔伯福斯反人口贩运保护重新授权法案》,公法110-457号(2008年12月23日)。
statutory protections or procedural safeguards applicable to that population.104 To the extent that employment-eligible unaccompanied children and youth seek EADs to participate in job training opportunities for valid employment purposes, they remain eligible to apply and, if approved, to work consistent with existing regulations. The IFR and this final rule do not impact or prevent the use of valid EADs as identity documents; they simply end the practice of automatically extending the validity of employment authorization documents for aliens who have timely filed an application to renew their EAD in certain employment authorization categories filed on or after October 30, 2025. DHS recognizes the heightened vulnerability of humanitarian populations and will continue to administer those programs consistent with statutory protections and humanitarian objectives. However, DHS concludes that the core principle underlying this rule, namely that renewed employment authorization should not be automatically extended without fully completing the adjudicative review and the related vetting, including resolution of any derogatory information identified during the vetting process -- must be applied consistently across affected categories to fulfill DHS’s national security, public safety, and benefit integrity responsibilities. As explained in detail in the IFR, granting benefits without fully completing the adjudicative review, which includes proper vetting, is contrary to the statutory mission of DHS and poses a threat to the safety and security of the American people.105 b. Impact on Employment-Based Nonimmigrants, Their Dependents, and Employment-Based Green Card Applicants Comment: Multiple commenters expressed concern about the removal of automatic extensions of EADs for H-4 dependent spouses. Some commenters said that 104 See, e.g., William Wilberforce Trafficking Victims Protection Reauthorization Act of 2008, Pub. L. 110- 457 (Dec. 23, 2008).
105 参见,例如《众议院第4567号法案会议报告》[第108-774号报告],《为截至2005年9月30日财政年度拨付国土安全部经费》,第74页(2004年10月9日),https://www.gpo.gov/fdsys/pkg/CRPT-108hrpt774/pdf/CRPT-108hrpt774.pdf (建议除其他事项外,设立一个机构,在授予移民福利之前对每位申请人、受益人及申请人进行“执法/背景审查”)。取消就业许可文件自动续期不成比例地损害了女性的利益,因为女性占H-4就业许可持有人的大多数。多位评论者担心H-4非移民依赖其配偶的H-1B身份,这造成了脆弱性。
105 See, e.g., Conference Report to accompany H.R. 4567 [Report 108-774], “Making Appropriations for the Department of Homeland Security for the Fiscal Year Ending September 30, 2005,” p. 74 (Oct. 9, 2004), https://www.gpo.gov/fdsys/pkg/CRPT-108hrpt774/pdf/CRPT-108hrpt774.pdf (recommending, among other things, the creation of an organization to conduct “law enforcement/background checks on every applicant, beneficiary, and petitioner prior to granting immigration benefits.”). eliminating automatic EAD renewal disproportionately harms women, who make up most H-4 EAD holders. Multiple commenters raised concerns that H-4 nonimmigrants are dependent on their spouses’ H-1B status, creating vulnerabilities.
多位评论者指出,目前H-4就业许可申请的处理时间经常超过建议的在现有就业许可到期前180天提交申请的期限,并认为取消自动延期将导致许多外国人失去就业许可,面临不可避免的工作中断。评论者表示,无论是劳工部还是国土安全部在H-1B处理上的延误,都可能对H-4非移民的身份及其就业许可申请产生不利影响,使及时提交申请复杂化。
A number of commenters remarked that current processing times for H-4 EAD applications frequently exceed the recommended filing time of 180 days prior to the current EAD’s expiration and argued the removal of automatic extensions would result in many aliens losing employment authorization and facing unavoidable work gaps. Commenters said that delays in H-1B processing, from either the Department of Labor or DHS, could adversely affect an H-4 nonimmigrant’s status and application for an EAD, complicating timely filing.
其他评论者认为,取消自动延期将显著降低H-4非移民的可雇佣性,并将造成包括家庭收入和储蓄中断、配偶及子女医疗保险终止、可能被驱逐或无法履行抵押贷款义务、心理压力和医疗困境、雇主管理I-9重新核实的行政负担以及工作中断等不利影响。
Other commenters suggested that removing the automatic extension would significantly reduce the employability of H-4 nonimmigrants and will cause adverse effects including family income and savings disruption, termination of health insurance for spouses and children, possible eviction or inability to meet mortgage obligations, psychological stress and medical hardship, administrative burden on employers managing I-9 reverifications, and work interruptions.
其他评论者表示,H-4就业许可持有人通过其专业工作和纳税为经济做出了重大贡献,已经过美国公民及移民服务局(USCIS)的全面审查,并积极为其所在社区做出贡献。评论者指出,对于一个已获得充分审查和证明的合法居民群体,缺乏合理的延期期限不利于稳定移民体系这一目标的实现。因此,一位评论者敦促国土安全部重新考虑该临时最终规则,或恢复此前的自动延期期限,或将其延长至至少240天,以与当前USCIS的处理时限保持一致,或将C26等已经过多次审查的合法居民类别予以豁免。
Other commenters said that H-4 EAD holders contribute significantly to the economy through their specialized work and through taxes; have been thoroughly vetted by USCIS; and are actively contributing to their communities. The commenters indicated that the lack of a reasonable extension period for a fully vetted and documented population of legal residents is counterproductive to the goals of a stable immigration system. Therefore, one commenter urged DHS to reconsider this IFR, or revert to the previous duration of the automatic extension or extend it to at least 240 days, to align with the current USCIS processing timelines or exempt certain categories like C26 and other legal resident categories who are already vetted multiple times.
其他评论人员强调了临时最终规则（IFR）对企业的影响，因为企业依赖稳定且获得授权的劳动力队伍来履行客户承诺、推动创新并支持美国经济增长。该评论人员总结认为，临时最终规则将主要影响H-4和L-2受抚养人，其工作许可文件（EAD）续签本已面临严重的处理延误，这反过来会扰乱项目进度、增加行政负担，并给雇员及其家庭造成重大的经济和情感困扰。许多人还认为他们可能失去工作授权，从而影响他们本人以及雇用他们的美国公民。许多人敦促美国公民及移民服务局（USCIS）保留自动延期政策，以减少积压、改善裁决、减少对企业（包括小型企业）的干扰，并提升美国的全球竞争力。 回应：本规则不改变H-4、L-2或其他基于雇佣关系的受抚养人申请工作授权和/或EAD的基本资格。本最终规则也不改变基于雇佣的调整身份申请人在其I-485表格申请待决期间申请EAD的资格。对于那些工作授权附随于其身份、且不一定以EAD证明的非移民（例如，某些在I-94表格上有适当注记的L-2配偶），只要其基础非移民身份持续有效，该授权即持续有效，与是否单独持有EAD无关。对于根据8 CFR 274a.12(c)条款获得工作授权的人员，该授权的续签仍属自由裁量且有时间限制。
Other commenters highlighted the effects of the IFR on business as they are relying on a stable and authorized workforce to meet client commitments, drive innovation, and support U.S. economic growth. This commenter concluded that the IFR would primarily affect H-4 and L-2 dependents whose EAD renewals already face significant processing delays, which in turn disrupts projects, increases administrative burdens and cause significant financial and emotional distress to the employees and their families. Many also believed that they could lose their authorization to work, impacting them and the U.S. citizens they employ. Many urged USCIS to retain the automatic extensions to reduce backlogs and improve adjudication, reduce disruptions to businesses, including small-businesses, and increase America’s global competitiveness. Response: This rule does not change the underlying eligibility of H-4, L-2, or other employment-based dependents to apply for employment authorization and/or EAD. This final rule also does not alter the eligibility of employment-based adjustment applicants to seek EADs while their Form I-485 applications are pending. For nonimmigrants whose employment authorization is incident to status and not necessarily evidenced by an EAD (for example, certain L–2 spouses with appropriate notation on their Form I–94), that authorization continues for so long as the underlying nonimmigrant status remains valid, irrespective of any separate EAD. For those whose employment authorization is granted under 8 CFR 274a.12(c), renewal of that authorization remains discretionary and time limited.
与所有在临时最终规则生效前有资格获得工作许可文件有效期自动延长的非移民类别一样，本规则仅取消了一项做法，即仅基于在2025年10月30日或之后及时提交的EAD续签申请，而自动延长EAD有效期以及（如适用）相应工作授权的做法。国土安全部（DHS）承认，鉴于目前和历史上某些受抚养人类别和基于雇佣类别EAD的处理时间，以及完成申请的全面裁决和审查所需的步骤，存在美国公民及移民服务局可能无法在旧卡到期前完成每份续签申请裁决的风险。在此类情况下，受影响的受抚养人和身份调整申请人可能会经历工作授权的暂时中断，并对收入、福利和职业连续性产生相关影响。雇主如果无法在续签待决期间留用这些雇员，也可能面临运营中断。
As is the case for all nonimmigrant classifications who were eligible to receive an automatic extension of the validity of employment authorization documents prior to the IFR, this rule eliminates only the practice of providing an automatic extension of the EAD validity and, where applicable, attendant employment authorization based solely on a timely filed renewal EAD application on or after October 30, 2025. DHS acknowledges that, given current and historical processing times for some dependent and employment- based EAD categories, as well as the steps needed to fully adjudicate and vet an application, there may be a risk that USCIS may not complete adjudication of every renewal application before the prior card expires. In such cases, affected dependents and adjustment applicants may experience temporary lapses in employment authorization and associated impacts on income, benefits, and career continuity. Employers may also face disruptions if they are unable to retain those employees pending renewal.
国土安全部已审慎权衡上述影响与其自身职责，即确保只有在美国公民及移民服务局完成适当审查，并确定申请人持续符合资格且（如适用）值得获得自由裁量的有利处理之后，方可授予续签的工作授权。H-4、L-2以及基于雇佣的身份调整申请人的自动延期，与其他类别的自动延期存在相同的根本性安全和项目完整性问题：在美国公民及移民服务局依据任何新信息（包括可能存在的工作授权不合格情形，或潜在的犯罪记录或其他不利信息）确定申请人是否持续符合资格之前，这些延期仅凭一份待决申请，就允许申请人在长达540天的时间内继续获得工作授权，并使用表面上已过期的安全证件。基于上述原因，国土安全部拒绝为任何一个子类别的EAD续签申请人（包括基于雇佣关系的受抚养人或身份调整申请人）保留任何期限的自动延期。106 设立例外情形将延续这一核心问题，即某些外国人可能在未经更新审查和裁决的情况下继续获得工作授权，并可能利用相关证件获取其他福利。国土安全部因此选择了统一的处理方式，对所有受影响类别适用相同的原则。
DHS has carefully weighed these impacts against its responsibility to ensure that renewed employment authorization is granted only after USCIS has completed appropriate vetting and determined that the applicant continues to be eligible and merit a favorable exercise of discretion, if applicable. Automatic extensions for H-4, L-2, and employment-based adjustment applicants present the same underlying security and program integrity concerns as automatic extensions for other categories: they allow continued employment authorization and use of facially expired secure documentation for up to 540 days based solely on a pending application, before USCIS has determined continued eligibility in light of any new information, including potential ineligibility for employment authorization or potential criminal or other derogatory information. For these reasons, DHS declines to preserve automatic extensions of any length for any subset of renewal EAD applicants, including employment-based- dependents or adjustment applicants.106 Providing carve outs would perpetuate the core concern that some aliens could continue employment authorization, and potentially use associated documents to obtain other benefits, without updated vetting and adjudication. The Department has instead chosen a uniform approach that applies the same principle across affected categories.
美国国土安全部（DHS）指出，许多基于就业的就业许可文件（EAD）与其他福利申请相关联（例如，主申请人非移民身份延期或调整身份申请），106 参见90 FR 48799, 48810（讨论DHS对替代方案的考虑，例如EAD的较短自动延期，并阐述了否决替代方案所依据的安全与资格方面的考量）。并且美国公民及移民服务局（USCIS）正在努力在可操作的情况下简化和协调处理流程。USCIS将继续探索流程改进措施，以进一步减少未来出现积压的可能性，包括在法律授权的情况下扩大使用加急处理或其他工具的可能性，同时确保安全性和诚信性不受损害。
DHS notes that many employment-based EADs are linked to other benefit requests (for example, principal nonimmigrant extensions or adjustment applications) and 106 See 90 FR 48799, 48810 (discussing DHS consideration of alternatives, such as shorter automatic extensions of EADs and outlining the security and eligibility concerns underlying rejection of alternatives). that USCIS is engaged in efforts to streamline and coordinate processing where operationally feasible. USCIS will continue to explore process improvements to further reduce the potential for future backlogs, including the potential for expanded use of premium processing or other tools where authorized by statute, while ensuring that security and integrity are not compromised.
DHS认识到基于就业的非移民及其家属以及身份调整申请人所作出的重要经济和个人贡献，也认识到就业授权中断可能给这些外国人、其雇主，或这些外国人凭借其EAD所开办和经营的企业带来的影响。然而，DHS认为，有必要确保仅在对继续符合资格进行全面裁决审查（包括在适用情况下是否应获得有利的自由裁量权行使）并经过审查核实之后，才延长更新的就业授权，这与当前的国家安全和公共安全优先事项相一致，因此有理由对这些类别也终止自动延期，并且这一理由超过了就业授权暂时中断可能带来的影响。
DHS recognizes the important economic and personal contributions of employment-based nonimmigrants, their families, and adjustment applicants, and the disruption that lapses in employment authorization may cause for these aliens, their employers, or to the businesses that these aliens may have opened and operated based on their EAD. Nevertheless, DHS concludes that the need to ensure that renewed employment authorization is extended only after full adjudicative review for continued eligibility (including whether the applicant merits a favorable exercise of discretion, if applicable) and vetting, consistent with current national security and public safety priorities, justifies ending automatic extensions for these categories as well and outweighs the potential impacts of temporary lapses in employment authorization.
6. 澄清请求意见：一位评论者要求USCIS确认，递交申请的日期决定自动延期的资格。具体而言，该评论者寻求确认，所有在2025年10月30日之前及时递交申请的人，无论其现有EAD何时到期，都将获得自动延期。
6. Clarification Request Comment: A commenter requested that USCIS confirm that the filing date governs eligibility for automatic extensions. Specifically, the commenter sought to confirm that all timely filers before October 30, 2025, would receive automatic extensions, regardless of when their current EAD expired.
回应：临时最终规则（IFR）107 以及本最终规则，均不影响在2025年10月30日之前已自动延期的EAD的效力，也不影响依法律或联邦公报通知另行自动延期的EAD的效力。根据2025年10月30日前有效的法规，要获得自动延期，符合条件的续签申请必须满足以下条件：
Response: The IFR,107 and this final rule, do not impact the validity of EADs that were automatically extended prior to October 30, 2025, or which are otherwise automatically extended by law or Federal Register notice. To have received an automatic extension under the regulation in effect prior to October 30, 2025, an eligible renewal application must have met the following conditions:
107 参见90 FR 48799, 48799（2025年10月30日）。
107 See 90 FR 48799, 48799 (Oct. 30, 2025).
•该外国人必须在其EAD到期前及时提交就业授权和/或EAD续签申请。参见8 CFR 274a.13(d)(1)(i)；108 •该外国人的就业授权和/或EAD续签申请必须已于2025年10月30日之前正式提交，109 无论EAD何时到期；
•The alien must have timely filed an application to renew the employment authorization and/or EAD before the EAD expires. See 8 CFR 274a.13(d)(1)(i);108 •The alien’s application to renew the employment authorization and/or EAD must have been properly filed before October 30, 2025,109 irrespective of when the EAD expired;
•续签EAD申请所依据的就业授权类别，必须与即将到期的EAD正面所列类别相同，或者，对于获批临时保护身份（TPS）的外国人，其EAD系根据8 CFR 274a.12(a)(12)或(c)(19)签发；并且•该外国人申请就业授权的资格在EAD到期后仍持续有效，且该资格所依据的就业授权类别不要求在裁决续签申请之前先裁决相关的基础申请或请愿书，该情形可能已在USCIS网站上公布。110 对于符合上述条件的外国人，表面上已过期（“表面过期”）的EAD，在与USCIS邮寄给申请人的《行动通知》（I-797C表）结合使用时，自EAD正面所列到期日起最长540天内，仍视为未过期，前提是该通知表明基于同一108 TPS受益人必须在适用的联邦公报通知规定的重新登记期内提交申请；参见81 FR 82398, 82455（2016年11月18日）。就业授权类别的续签申请已及时提交（即USCIS签发的I-765表收据通知上的收件日期早于EAD到期日，且在2025年10月30日之前）。
•The renewal EAD application is based on the same employment authorization category shown on the front of the expiring EAD or, for an alien approved for TPS, whose EAD was issued pursuant to either 8 CFR 274a.12(a)(12) or (c)(19); and •The alien’s eligibility to apply for employment authorization continued notwithstanding the expiration of the EAD and was based on an employment authorization category that does not require the adjudication of an underlying application or petition before the adjudication of the renewal application, as may have been announced on the USCIS website.110 For aliens who meet these requirements, an EAD that appears on its face to be expired (“facially expired”) is considered unexpired for up to 540 days from the expiration date on the front of the EAD when combined with a Notice of Action (Form I- 797C) that USCIS mails to an applicant, indicating a timely filing (i.e., the receipt notice for the Form I-765 issued by USCIS has a receipt date that is prior to the expiration date on the EAD and before October 30, 2025) of the renewal application based on the same 108 TPS beneficiaries must have filed during the re-registration period in the Applicable Federal Register notice; see 81 FR 82398, 82455 (Nov. 18, 2016).
109 若要在2025年10月30日之前妥善提交,外国人必须已提交其申请,使美国公民及移民服务局(USCIS)在2025年10月29日或之前实际收到经妥善签署的申请及所需费用。参见8 CFR 103.2(a)(7)(i)。因此,在2025年10月30日之前已提交但尚未被收到的申请,其外国人不符合自动延期的资格。 110 名为“自动就业授权文件(EAD)延期”的网页鉴于本临时最终规则(IFR)已被存档,但该网页仍可在以下网址查看:https://www.uscis.gov/archive/automatic-employment-authorization-document-ead-extension(最后更新于2025年10月29日)。

See 8 CFR 103.2(a)(7)(i). Accordingly, an alien whose application was submitted but not yet received before October 30, 2025, is ineligible for the automatic extension. 110 The website entitled “Automatic Employment Authorization Document (EAD) Extension” has since been archived in light of the IFR but the webpage is still available for viewing at https://www.uscis.gov/archive/automatic-employment-authorization-document-ead-extension (last updated Oct. 29, 2025).
表面已过期的EAD上所载明的就业资格类别(或在EAD及I-797C通知书包含A12或C19类别代码的情形下,此类别代码无需相符)。在此情形下,外国人表面已过期的EAD自该EAD签发之日起最长540天内视为未过期,即使该EAD于2025年10月30日或之后到期亦然。
employment eligibility category as stated on the facially expired EAD (or in case of an EAD and I-797C notice that contains either A12 or C19 category code – the category code need not match for these categories. In those cases, the alien’s facially expired EAD is considered unexpired for up to 540 days from the date of the EAD, even if the EAD expires on or after October 30, 2025.
7. 关于自动延期及EAD有效期的建议 多位评论者鼓励国土安全部(DHS)探索能够更好地平衡国家安全、经济稳定与人的尊严的替代解决方案。
7. Recommendations for the Automatic Extension and EAD Validity Period Several commenters encouraged DHS to explore alternative solutions that better balance national security, economic stability, and human dignity.
一、维持自动延期,包括延期期间的各种时长 评论意见:许多评论者敦促国土安全部撤回本临时最终规则,转而维持自动延期政策。部分评论者指出,维持自动延期政策对于保护劳工及其家庭免于因审理延误而丧失就业连续性,以及维护美国的经济与战略利益,是必要的。多位评论者认为,在EAD续签审理达到可靠且稳定的基准(例如少于90天)之前,应保留自动延期。
i. Maintain the Automatic Extension, including various lengths of the Extension Period Comment: Many commenters urged DHS to withdraw the IFR and instead maintain the automatic extension policy. Some commenters stated that keeping the automatic extension policy is necessary to protect workers and their families from losing continuity of employment caused by processing delays and protecting the economic and strategic interests of the United States. Multiple commenters reasoned that the automatic extension should be retained until reliable EAD renewal processing benchmarks (for example, less than 90 days) are consistently met.
众多评论者表示,国土安全部至少应恢复最长180天的自动延期期限。其他评论者则建议采用各种不同的延期期限,包括最长120天、最长180天或最长240天,而非完全取消延期期限,以便更好地与当前USCIS的审理时限相适应,并确保对劳动力的干扰降至最低。
Numerous commenters said DHS should at a minimum restore the up-to-180-day automatic extension period. Other commenters recommended various extension periods, including up to 120 days, up to 180 days, or up to 240 days, rather than removing extension periods entirely to better align with the current USCIS processing timelines and ensure minimal workforce disruption.
回应:国土安全部不采纳评论者的建议。保留缩短后的自动延期期限将与国土安全部的首要关切相冲突,即:在外国人未经妥善审查并确定其持续符合资格(如适用,亦未确定是否值得获得自由裁量权的有利行使)的情况下,基于完整的裁决审查之前便授予其就业授权福利,这与国土安全部旨在增强福利诚信、维护公共安全与国家安全的使命相悖。111 正如本临时最终规则所述,112 若国土安全部采纳这些方案,则在背景审查过程中被标记存在不良信息的外国人,仍将获得某种时长(例如180天或240天)的自动延期。这些延期构成安全隐患,并损害福利诚信。与最长540天的自动延期一样,任何时长的自动延期均无法消除以下风险,即希望为非法活动提供资金的外国人可能继续合法工作并赚取资金。这些外国人同样可能利用其自动延长的就业授权及/或EAD获取有效身份证件,从而更易于从事有害于美国的行为。
Response: DHS declines to adopt the commenters’ suggestions. Keeping a reduced automatic extension period would conflict with DHS’s overriding concern that granting employment authorization benefits to aliens without those aliens being properly vetted and determined to continue to be eligible (and merit a favorable exercise of discretion, if applicable) based on a complete adjudicative review is contrary to the DHS mission to enhance benefit integrity and protect public safety and national security.111 As explained in the IFR,112 if DHS pursued these options, aliens with derogatory information flagged during the background check process would nevertheless still obtain an automatic extension of some lengths (such as 180 or 240 days). These extensions pose a security vulnerability and undermine benefit integrity. As with the up to 540-day automatic extension, any automatic extension duration would not eliminate the risk that an alien who wished to fund nefarious activities could continue to work lawfully and generate money. These same aliens could use their automatically extended employment authorization and/or EADs to obtain valid identity documents, which would make it easier to commit conduct detrimental to the United States.
二、连续的EAD有效期 评论意见:众多评论者建议实行连续的EAD有效期,以便申请人可以提前提交申请,而不致丧失工作授权、时间与金钱。评论者认为,在现行制度下,当外国人提前较长时间提交申请时,USCIS通常会签发相互重叠的有效期,而非在现有授权期结束时起算新的批准期,这导致提前申请者丧失了本可使用的工作授权时间。部分评论者得出结论认为,连续的EAD有效期将使外国人能够提前很长时间申请,为USCIS提供更长的裁决窗口期,并保留完整的授权就业期限。
ii. Consecutive EAD Validity Periods Comment: Numerous commenters recommended implementing consecutive EAD validity periods so that applicants could file early without losing work authorization, time, and money. Commenters reasoned that under the current system, when aliens file well in advance of expiration, USCIS routinely issues overlapping validity periods rather than starting the new approval period at the end of the existing authorization period resulting in early filers losing usable work authorization time. Some commenters concluded that consecutive EAD validity periods would allow aliens to apply far in advance, provide USCIS with a longer adjudication window, and preserve the full period of authorized employment.
同样,另一位评论者认为,应延长工作许可文件(EAD)的有效期,以减少续签的频率。该评论者指出,一年期的工作许可文件(EAD)实际上仅有六个月有效,因为续签审批通常需要 111 参见例如,配合众议院第4567号法案的会议报告[第108-774号报告],《为截至2004年9月30日财政年度国土安全部拨款法》,第74页(2004年10月9日),https://www.gpo.gov/fdsys/pkg/CRPT-108hrpt774/pdf/CRPT-108hrpt774.pdf (建议除其他事项外,设立一个机构,在授予移民福利之前对每位申请人、受益人和申请人进行“执法/背景调查”)。112 参见90 FR 48810。
Similarly, another commenter reasoned that EADs should be issued for longer validity periods to reduce the frequency of renewals, stating that a 1-year EAD is, in practice, effective for only 6 months as renewal adjudication typically requires 111 See, e.g., Conference Report to accompany H.R. 4567 [Report 108-774], “Making Appropriations for the Department of Homeland Security for the Fiscal Year Ending September 30, 2005,” p. 74 (Oct. 9, 2004), https://www.gpo.gov/fdsys/pkg/CRPT-108hrpt774/pdf/CRPT-108hrpt774.pdf (recommending, among other things, the creation of an organization to conduct “law enforcement/background checks on every applicant, beneficiary, and petitioner prior to granting immigration benefits.”). 112 See 90 FR 48810.
大约六个月。该评论者写道,临时最终规则(IFR)忽视了实际操作中的情况:尽管美国公民及移民服务局(USCIS)可能接受在到期前180天以上提交的工作许可文件(EAD)续签申请,但该机构发放的有效期是重叠的,而非连续的,这实际上缩短了卡片的可用时间,迫使申请人不得不将续签周期提前再提前。
approximately 6 months. The commenter wrote that the IFR ignores the operational realities, that, although USCIS may accept EAD renewals filed more than 180 days before expiration, the agency issues overlapping—not consecutive—validity periods, effectively cutting into the card’s usable time and forcing applicants into an ever earlier renewal cycle.
回应:国土安全部(DHS)考虑了评论者提出的替代方案,但不会采纳。国土安全部的首要任务是对我国境内所有外国人进行严格审查,以更好地保护美国工人和公众的安全。临时最终规则(IFR)以及本最终规则将通过确保在发放续签工作许可文件(EAD,一项重要福利)之前进行适当审查来加强公共安全,并提高项目完整性。国土安全部正在加强其审查和筛查工作,提高发现具有潜在危害意图的外国人的能力,遏制欺诈行为,并将应被驱逐的外国人置于遣返程序之中。美国公民及移民服务局(USCIS)在法律允许的范围内,充分利用法律规定的各项条款,拒绝向对公共安全和国家安全构成风险的人员提供福利。这包括更频繁地对外国人进行审查,包括更频繁地判定已获得工作许可文件(EAD)的外国人是否继续符合就业授权资格,以及在适用情况下,是否继续值得获得自由裁量权的有利行使。国土安全部认为,如果美国公民及移民服务局(USCIS)同时或在相近时间内向同一外国人发放多份具有连续有效期的工作许可文件(EAD),或按评论者建议发放有效期更长的工作许可文件(EAD),将与上述政策目标不符。基于同时或相近时间提交的多份续签申请而发放的连续工作许可文件(EAD),实际上等同于发放一份有效期更长的工作许可文件(EAD)。这些替代方案的结果将是审查和资格判定的频率降低,这与国土安全部在临时最终规则(IFR)和本最终规则中所述的政策目标直接相悖。
Response: DHS considered the alternatives suggested by the commenters but will not adopt them. DHS’s top priority is the robust vetting of all aliens in our country to better protect the safety of American workers and the public at large. The IFR, and this final rule, will enhance public safety by ensuring proper vetting before issuing renewal EADs, which are important benefits, and improve program integrity. DHS is enhancing its vetting and screening efforts, increasing its ability to detect aliens with potentially harmful intent, deter fraud, and place removable aliens into proceedings. USCIS uses all provisions under the law, to the extent permissible under the law, to deny benefits to those who are a risk to public safety and national security. This includes more frequent vetting of aliens, including more frequent determinations that an alien granted an EAD continues to remain eligible for employment authorization and, as applicable, continues to merit a favorable exercise of discretion. DHS believes that it would be inconsistent with these policy goals for USCIS to issue, concurrently or in close proximity, multiple EADs to the same alien with consecutive validity periods or to issue EADs with longer validity periods, as the commenters suggest. Consecutive EADs based on the filing of multiple renewal applications, concurrently or in close proximity, would be tantamount to one EAD with a longer validity period. The result of these alternatives would be less frequent vetting and eligibility determinations, which would be directly contrary to DHS’s stated policy goal of the IFR and this final rule.
iii. 允许在180天之前提前提交申请 评论:一些评论者建议将工作许可文件(EAD)续签的申请窗口从目前到期前180天延长,以降低审理风险。少数评论者表示,美国公民及移民服务局(USCIS)应允许在到期前12个月提交工作许可文件(EAD)续签申请。
iii. Allowing Application Filing Earlier than 180 days Comment: Some commenters recommended extending the filing window for EAD renewals beyond the current 180 days prior to expiration to mitigate processing risks. A few commenters said USCIS should allow EAD renewals to be filed 12 months prior to expiration.
回应:尽管美国公民及移民服务局(USCIS)通常建议申请人不要在其工作许可文件(EAD)到期前180天以上提交续签申请,但并无禁止在到期前某一特定日期之前提交表格的规定。然而,美国公民及移民服务局(USCIS)不会将续签的工作许可文件(EAD)的生效日期相对于申请人当前工作许可文件(EAD)有效期进行推后处理。因此,提前更早在当前工作许可文件(EAD)到期之前提交申请并获得审批的申请人,产生就业授权中断的风险可能较小,但其合并后的就业授权有效期也可能较短。
Response: Although USCIS generally recommends that applicants not file requests to renew EADs more than 180 days before the expiration of their EADs, there is no prohibition on filing the form before a certain date ahead of expiration. However, USCIS does not postdate the renewal EAD in relation to an applicant’s current EAD’s validity period. Therefore, applicants who file and have their EAD applications adjudicated further ahead of the expiration of their current EADs may have less of a risk of incurring gaps in their employment authorization but may also have a shorter combined employment authorization validity period.
美国公民及移民服务局（USCIS）致力于继续探索提高处理效率的途径，包括可能扩大加急处理服务，使其适用于部分或全部受影响群体。 iv. 加强审查 评论：一些评论者表示，国土安全部（DHS）应实施加强审查措施，例如对申请人进行持续的安全审查，而不取消就业许可文件（EAD）的自动延期。其他评论者建议国土安全部在续期申请审理期间，继续将EAD自动延期最长维持540天，但以“个案审查”的方式开展审查，并指出美国公民及移民服务局可以运用其自由裁量权，在出现不良信息或其他问题的个案中取消延期。 回应：为服务于国土安全部的使命，并确保国土安全部仅在结合续期申请背景进行全面审查，对持续资格（包括，视情况而定，申请人是否值得获得有利的自由裁量处理）进行完整审理之后才授予福利，国土安全部拒绝采纳这些建议。国土安全部已确定，即便在使用加强审查工具的环境下，自动延期也与其当前的安全和福利诚信优先事项不相容。国土安全部只有在美国公民及移民服务局审查完整的申请、完成相应的审查、解决任何不良信息，并确定申请人持续符合资格，且（如适用）继续值得获得有利的自由裁量处理之后，才会批准续期的就业许可。即使国土安全部如评论者所建议的那样在个案中取消延期，该外国人仍将已经在未经适当审查和完整资格审理的情况下，哪怕只是短期地，获得了续期EAD所带来的好处，这与国土安全部增强福利诚信、更好地保护公共安全和国家安全的使命相悖。任何仅基于提交续期申请而给予的就业许可和（或）EAD有效期的自动延期，即便随后被取消或终止，也无法消除以下风险：即希望为恶意活动提供资金的外国人可能继续合法工作并赚取资金，即便该外国人可能本来就不符合续期EAD的资格。因此，国土安全部认为，在未完全彻底完成审理程序（包括审查）的情况下授予福利，将使从事恶意活动的外国人更容易实施有损美国利益的行为，因此不会采纳评论者的建议。
USCIS is committed to continuing to explore processing efficiencies, including possible expansion of premium processing for some or all affected populations. iv. Enhanced Vetting Comment: Some commenters said DHS should implement enhanced vetting, such as ongoing security checks for applicants, without eliminating automatic extension of EADs. Other commenters recommended DHS maintain the automatic extension of EADs for up to 540 days while renewal applications are adjudicated, but conduct vetting on a “particularized basis,” noting that USCIS can use its discretionary authority to cancel an extension in individual cases when and if derogatory information or other concerns arise. Response: In service of the DHS mission, and to ensure that DHS grants benefits only after having thoroughly vetted in the context of the renewal application and full adjudicative review for continued eligibility (including, as applicable, whether the applicant merits a favorable exercise of discretion), DHS declines to adopt these recommendations. DHS has determined that automatic extensions are not compatible with its current security and benefit integrity priorities, even in an environment where enhanced vetting tools are used. DHS will grant renewed employment authorization only after USCIS has reviewed a completed application, conducted the appropriate checks, resolved any derogatory information, and determined that the applicant continues to be eligible and continues to merit a favorable exercise of discretion, if applicable. Even if DHS were to cancel the extension in individual cases, as the commenter suggests, the alien would still have had the benefit of a renewed EAD, even if for a short time, without proper vetting and a full eligibility adjudication, which is contrary to the DHS mission to enhance benefit integrity and better protect public safety and national security. Any automatic extension of employment authorization and/or EAD validity based solely on the filing of a renewal application, even if subsequently cancelled or terminated, would not eliminate the risk that an alien who wished to fund nefarious activities could continue to work lawfully and generate money, even though the alien may not be eligible for the renewal EAD in the first place. Thus, DHS believes granting a benefit without completely and fully finishing the adjudicatory process (including vetting) would make it easier for aliens engaged in nefarious activity to commit conduct detrimental to the United States and will not adopt the commenter’s suggestion.
8. 处理能力与时间安排 i. 续期申请的及时处理 评论：少数评论者表示支持本临时最终规则（IFR），但敦促美国公民及移民服务局确保及时处理续期申请，以免工人和雇主经历意外的就业许可中断。一位评论者补充说，如果该程序无法在申请提交后180天内完成，那么本临时最终规则就是“非常不公平的”。许多评论者表示，应要求美国公民及移民服务局更快地审理和处理所有EAD申请。多位评论者建议设定一个具体的时间框架（例如，90天以内或6个月/180天以内）用于续期申请，以确保及时处理。一位评论者表示，高效、及时的处理是唯一能够真正同时改善安全与经济稳定的措施。
8. Processing Capacity and Timelines i. Timely Processing of Renewals Comment: A few commenters stated support for the IFR but urged USCIS to ensure timely processing of renewals, so workers and employers do not experience unintended lapses in employment authorization. A commenter added that if the process cannot be completed within 180 days of application, then the IFR is “very unfair.” Many commenters said USCIS should be required to adjudicate and process all EAD applications more quickly. Multiple commenters recommended a specific timeframe (for example, under 90 days or under 6 months/180 days) be set for renewals to ensure timely processing. A commenter stated that efficient, timely processing is the only measure that genuinely improves both security and economic stability.
回应：国土安全部仍致力于在不降低安全与审查标准或福利诚信的前提下，及时处理申请。国土安全部理解及时处理的重要性，并力求尽可能高效地审理申请。然而，多种因素可能影响处理时间，例如安全审查要求以及待审申请的总量。美国公民及移民服务局持续监测其资源分配情况，并在适当且可行的情况下进行调整，以确保客户服务。然而，鉴于需要对每份申请进行全面审查，以确保美国公民及移民服务局仅在对申请人进行充分审查和甄别（包括解决任何不良信息），并确定该外国人持续符合就业许可和（或）EAD资格，且（如适用）值得获得有利的自由裁量处理之后，才授予就业许可，国土安全部无法承诺在所有情况下都设定最长处理时间。
Response: DHS remains committed to timely processing applications without lowering security and vetting standards or benefit integrity. DHS understands the importance of timely processing and strives to adjudicate applications as efficiently as possible. However, a variety of factors can affect processing times, such as security vetting requirements and the total number of applications pending review. USCIS continually monitors its resource allocations and makes adjustments, as appropriate and feasible to ensure customer service. However, DHS cannot commit to a maximum processing time in all cases given the need for thorough review of each application to ensure that USCIS only grants employment authorization after full vetting and screening of the applicant, including resolution of any derogatory information, and determining that the alien continues to be eligible for employment authorization and/or an EAD and to merit a favorable exercise of discretion, if applicable.
美国国土安全部(DHS)指出，鉴于DHS已采取行动使政策与法律文本保持一致，我们预计整体就业授权文件(EAD)申请率(包括初次申请和续签申请)将大幅下降。DHS预计，这一下降将释放审裁资源，从而缩短续签EAD的处理时间，并在更长期内减少续签EAD申请人为避免因处理延误导致就业授权和/或EAD有效性出现中断而依赖EAD自动延期的必要性。113 因此，寻求续签其就业授权和/或EAD的外国人可能不太可能经历就业授权和/或EAD有效性的中断。ii. 增加资源和人员配备 评论：众多评论者敦促美国公民及移民服务局(USCIS)专注于减少积压、提高处理能力并实现处理系统现代化。一些评论者讨论了临时最终规则(IFR)并未提供替代解决方案或针对处理时间的改进建议。113 见上文第IV(C)(2)(i)(c)节。
DHS notes that we expect overall EAD filing rates (initials and renewals) are likely to substantially decline given actions DHS has taken to align policy with statutory text. DHS anticipates that this decline will free up adjudicative resources to reduce renewal EAD processing times and the need for renewal EAD applicants in the longer term to rely on an automatic extension of their EAD to avoid lapses in employment authorization and/or EAD validity due to processing delays.113 Therefore, aliens seeking renewal of their employment authorization and/or EADs may not be likely to experience lapses in employment authorization and/or EAD validity. ii. Increase Resources and Staffing Comment: Numerous commenters urged USCIS to focus on reducing backlogs, improving processing capacity, and modernizing processing systems. Some commenters discussed how the IFR offers no alternative solutions or proposed improvements to 113 See section IV(C)(2)(i)(c), supra.
一名评论者敦促DHS与国会合作，批准拨款以加强USCIS持续及时进行审裁的能力。一些评论者表示，USCIS加强人员配备和资源分配，以确保在EAD到期前完成批准，这一点至关重要。一名评论者表示，USCIS应考虑加倍投入资源和人员配备，以减少积压并最大限度地减少有害影响。
processing times. A commenter urged DHS to work with Congress to approve appropriations to strengthen USCIS’ capacity to consistently facilitate adjudications in a timely manner. Some commenters said it was crucial for USCIS to enhance staffing and resource allocation to ensure that approvals are completed before expiration of EADs. A commenter stated that USCIS should consider redoubling resources and staffing to reduce backlogs and minimize harmful impacts.
回应：DHS同意减少积压、提高处理能力、实现案件处理系统现代化以及确保充足的人员配备是重要目标，USCIS正在其现有法定收费权限和更广泛的举措范围内推进此类努力。
Response: DHS agrees that reducing backlogs, improving processing capacity, and modernizing case‑processing systems, as well as ensuring sufficient staffing, are important objectives, and USCIS is pursuing such efforts as permissible within its existing statutory fee authority and broader initiatives.
例如，USCIS持续在现有预算和招聘权限范围内采取措施，以进一步提高处理效率、招聘新员工、114 重新分配人员、优化工作流程、投资自动化、115 并落实近期立法和行政行动所指示的收费116 和项目变更。117 如本序言其他部分所述，DHS已采取重大措施，以减少前几年美国遭遇前所未有的非法移民潮之后随之而来的整体EAD申请量。118 因此，DHS也预计 114 参见，例如，USCIS，“USCIS收到35,000份国土卫士申请，为该局历史上单一职位申请人数最多的一次”，https://www.uscis.gov/newsroom/news-releases/uscis-received-35000-applications-for-homeland-defenders-most-for-any-job-in-agency-history (2025年11月6日)。
For example, USCIS continuously takes steps within existing budgetary and hiring authorities to further enhance processing efficiency, hire new staff,114 reallocate personnel, refine workflows, invest in automation,115 and implement the fee116 and program changes directed by recent legislation and executive actions.117 As discussed elsewhere in this preamble, DHS has taken significant steps to reduce the overall EAD filings that followed the unprecedented flood of illegal immigration into the United States during previous years.118 As such, DHS also expects 114 See, e.g., USICS, “USCIS Received 35,000 Applications for Homeland Defenders, Most for Any Job in Agency History,” https://www.uscis.gov/newsroom/news-releases/uscis-received-35000-applications-for- homeland-defenders-most-for-any-job-in-agency-history (Nov. 6, 2025).

USCIS is primarily a fee-based agency. 118 见 90 FR 48799, 48809（2025年10月30日）。另见 美国公民及移民服务局（USCIS），《国土安全部加强国家移民系统的诚信，恢复常识性合法移民水平》，https://www.uscis.gov/newsroom/news-releases/dhs-strengthens-integrity-in-nations-immigration-system-returns-to-commonsense-legal-immigration（2025年11月13日）；USCIS局长Joseph B. Edlow的证词，《国土安全部监督：移民及海关执法局（ICE）、海关及边境保护局（CBP）与USCIS》：整体工作许可文件（EAD，Employment Authorization Document）申请率（包括首次申请和续签申请）将因USCIS采取的其他措施而随时间下降，例如缩小假释的使用范围，以及使临时保护身份（TPS）及相关项目更好地符合法定标准。事实上，正如本序言上文第IV.C.2.i.d节表1所示，自2025年1月以来，USCIS已经看到EAD申请量的减少。USCIS指出，EAD申请率的下降支持了临时最终规则（IFR）中提出的观点，即潜在的中断，尤其是任何中断的持续时间，具有推测性，并可能被自动延期所带来的危害所抵消。国土安全部（DHS）预计，这一申请量的减少将释放审理资源，USCIS将继续监测其工作量，并酌情调整人员配置和资源分配。
118 See 90 FR 48799, 48809 (Oct. 30, 2025). See also USCIS, “DHS Strengthens Integrity in Nation’s Immigration System, Returns to Commonsense Legal Immigration Levels,” https://www.uscis.gov/newsroom/news-releases/dhs-strengthens-integrity-in-nations-immigration-system- returns-to-commonsense-legal-immigration (Nov. 13, 2025); Testimony of USCIS Director Joseph B. Edlow, Oversight of the Department of Homeland Security: ICE, CBP, and USCIS: Hearing Before the that overall EAD filing rates (both initials and renewals) will decline over time due to other actions taken by USCIS, such as narrowing the use of parole and better aligning TPS and related programs with statutory criteria. In fact, USCIS has already seen a reduction in EAD filings since January 2025 as evidenced by Table 1 in Section IV.C.2.i.d. of this preamble above. USCIS notes the decreased EAD filing rates support the point made in the IFR, that the potential lapse, and in particular the duration of any lapse, is speculative and could be outweighed by the harm posed by automatic extensions. DHS expects that this reduction in volume will free adjudicative resources, and USCIS will continue to monitor its workloads and adjust staffing and resource allocations as appropriate.
然而，本最终规则并非一项积压管理措施。它所解决的根本问题是，自动延期仅凭续签申请的提交，即可使工作许可和（或）EAD有效期延续长达540天，而此时USCIS尚未完成全面审查、尚未解决任何不利信息，且尚未确定该外国人是否仍然符合资格，并在适用情况下是否仍然值得获得有利的自由裁量。即使增加额外资源能够进一步改善处理时间，也无法消除在未对申请进行全面审理的情况下授予自动延期所固有的核心安全及项目诚信风险。
This final rule, however, is not a backlog‑management measure. It addresses the fundamental concern that automatic extensions allow employment authorization and/or EAD validity to continue for up to 540 days based solely on the filing of a renewal application, before USCIS has fully completed vetting, resolved any derogatory information, and determined that the alien remains eligible and continues to merit a favorable exercise of discretion, if applicable. Even if additional resources were to further improve processing times, they would not eliminate the core security and program‑integrity risk inherent in granting automatic extensions without a full adjudication of the application.
评论：多位评论者建议为EAD续签实行加急处理（premium processing）选项。一位评论者指出，由于许多申请耗时超过6个月，在终止自动延期之前，应当将加急处理作为一种可选方案。一些评论者指出，这一替代方案可使雇主和（或）众议院国土安全委员会（House Comm. on Homeland Security），https://docs.house.gov/meetings/HM/HM00/20260210/118916/HHRG-119-HM00-Wstate-EdlowJ-20260210.pdf，第6页，（2026年2月10日），（“这些关键努力加强了我国合法移民系统的诚信，强化了问责制，并确保USCIS的资源被用于依法、经适当提交的案件。”）。

三、优先审理初次EAD申请 评论：一位评论者认为，DHS的资源应优先用于初次EAD审查（即对新进入者进行评估），而不应对此前已通过审查的续签申请人重复进行审查工作。
iii. Prioritize Initial EAD Reviews Comment: A commenter reasoned that DHS resources should prioritize initial EAD screenings, where new entrants are evaluated, rather than duplicating review efforts for previously cleared renewal applicants.
回应：尽管续签EAD申请人在其初次EAD申请审理过程中已经接受过审查，但DHS不认同在后续阶段（包括提交续签EAD申请时）进行审查属于重复工作这一观点。例如，外国人可能在入境后从事犯罪活动，而新的不利信息（包括此前已发生但新近获悉的信息）可能在任何阶段出现。DHS将继续在每一次福利申请提交时优先进行审查，并将继续推进本规则的最终确定工作，以终止自动延长就业授权和/或EAD的做法，从而确保申请人在未经结合申请进行全面、完整审查并确认其持续符合资格之前，不会获得相关福利。
Response: Although renewal EAD applicants will already have been vetted during the adjudication of their initial EAD applications, DHS disagrees that review at subsequent stages, including upon the filing of a renewal EAD application, is duplicative. Aliens may engage in criminal activity after entry, for example, and new derogatory information, including that which is newly obtained despite having occurred previously, may appear at any stage. DHS will continue to prioritize vetting with the filing of every benefit request, and DHS will proceed with finalizing this rule to end the practice of automatically extending employment authorization and/or EADs to ensure that applicants do not receive benefits without full and complete vetting in conjunction with the application and a determination that they continue to be eligible.
9. 协调、指导与宣传 一、补充指导与沟通 评论：一位评论者表示，USCIS应提供明确的沟通和文件，说明本临时最终规则（IFR）的影响，并提供相关信息，帮助处境相似的专业人士有效应对这一变化。同样，其他评论者建议：
9. Coordination, Guidance, and Outreach i. Additional Guidance and Communication Comment: A commenter said USCIS should provide clear communication and documentation outlining the impacts of the IFR and information on how professionals in similar situations can navigate the change effectively. Similarly, other commenters recommended:
•为雇主提供明确指导（例如更新的I-9表格填写说明及宣传材料）；
•Clear employer guidance (such as updated I-9 instructions and outreach);
•基于SAVE系统的核验机制，以减少雇主对自我申报信息的依赖；
•SAVE-based verification so that employers are less dependent on self- reported information;
●一个电子通知系统,用于续签申请受理回执和裁决状态通知,供雇主在重新核实身份时参考使用;以及 ●针对医疗保健行业的具体指导,承认医院、实验室和急救服务提供者在人员配置方面的特殊义务。
•An electronic notification system for renewal filing receipt and adjudication status that employers can rely upon for reverification purposes; and •Health-sector-specific guidance recognizing the unique staffing obligations of hospitals, laboratories, and emergency care providers.
回应:2025年10月30日,美国公民及移民服务局(USCIS)发布了对《雇主手册》(M-274)第5节的更新,该手册为雇主提供有关I-9表格(就业资格核实表)的指导。此次更新强调2025年10月30日为确定外国人是否符合2024年最终规则所宣布的自动延期资格的相关日期。125 USCIS进一步更新了手册第5.1节,详细说明了符合自动延期条件的外国人所适用的证据类别和来源。126 USCIS定期更新M-274手册,此外还在机构网站上为雇主提供指导,每月为雇主和雇员举办多场关于I-9表格的网络研讨会,并设有I-9表格联系中心,提供电话号码和电子邮件地址。127 125 参见USCIS,《雇主手册》M-274,第5.0节“在特定情形下就业授权和/或就业授权文件(EAD)的自动延期”(最后更新于2025年10月30日),https://www.uscis.gov/i-9-central/form-i-9-resources/handbook-for-employers-m-274/50-automatic-extensions-of-employment-authorization-andor-employment-authorization-documents-eads-in。 126 参见美国国土安全部(DHS)、USCIS,《雇主手册》M-274,第5.1节“基于2025年10月30日之前及时提交的就业授权和/或就业授权文件续签申请的自动延期”(最后更新于2025年10月30日),https://www.uscis.gov/i-9-central/form-i-9-resources/handbook-for-employers-m-274/50-automatic-extensions-of-employment-authorization-andor-employment-authorization-documents-eads-in/51-automatic-extensions-based-on-a-timely-filed-application-to-renew-employment-authorization。
Response: On October 30, 2025, USCIS published an update to section 5 of the Handbook for Employers M-274, which contains guidance for employers regarding Form I-9, Employment Eligibility Verification. The update highlighted October 30, 2025, as the relevant date to determine whether an alien qualifies for the automatic extension announced in the 2024 Final Rule.125 USCIS further updated the Handbook at Section 5.1 to spell out the categories and sources of evidence for those aliens who qualified for the automatic extension.126 USCIS updates the M-274 periodically, and in addition offers guidance for employers on the agency website, hosts several monthly webinars for employers and employees on the I-9, and maintains a Form I-9 Contact Center with a phone number and e-mail address.127 125 See USCIS, Handbook for Employers M-274, 5.0 Automatic Extensions of Employment Authorization and/or Employment Authorization Documents (EADs) in Certain Circumstances (last updated Oct. 30, 2025), https://www.uscis.gov/i-9-central/form-i-9-resources/handbook-for-employers-m-274/50-automatic- extensions-of-employment-authorization-andor-employment-authorization-documents-eads-in. 126 See DHS, USCIS, Handbook for Employers M-274, 5.1 Automatic Extensions Based on a Timely Filed Application to Renew Employment Authorization and/or Employment Authorization Document Before Oct. 30, 2025 (last updated Oct. 30, 2025), https://www.uscis.gov/i-9-central/form-i-9-resources/handbook-for- employers-m-274/50-automatic-extensions-of-employment-authorization-andor-employment- authorization-documents-eads-in/51-automatic-extensions-based-on-a-timely-filed-application-to-renew- employment-authorization.

SAVE is an online service administered by USCIS that provides point in time immigration status and U.S. citizenship information to federal, state, territorial, tribal, and local agencies.128 SAVE is limited to its statutory purposes129 and, accordingly, does not provide employment eligibility verification to private employers. Employment authorization applications may contain sensitive information. Under the Privacy Act of 1974,130 Freedom of Information Act (FOIA),131 and other applicable laws, regulations and policies,132 USCIS and other immigration agencies are legally obligated to safeguard a petitioner’s and applicant’s information and limit information sharing.
